## 2026-07-29 (fj) — the incubator searches BEYOND the cage, and the first cage signal it produced was FALSE

Operator: *"proceed"* on going past the registry bounds. The search now covers the out-of-cage space — and the first thing it did was try to talk me into widening a bound on an artifact, which is the part of this entry worth reading.

- **Why searching out-of-cage is safe, and the reasoning must survive**: the incubator has **no actuator**. Its only consumer is the dashboard (verified — nothing outside `pnl_dashboard.py` reads `bot_state['strategy-incubator']`). The `fleet_tuning` cages bound what the SCOUT TUNER may ENACT; they were never a bound on what this organ may MEASURE. Clamping the search to them bought no safety and cost the entire out-of-cage question.
- **What IS at risk is confusion, not money**, so: every genotype is stamped `enactable`; the published **champion and the gametes are drawn only from the enactable subset**; out-of-cage results are published separately as `frontier`. `is_enactable` fails CLOSED (unknown gene or unclampable value ⇒ not enactable).
- **`RESEARCH_GENES`** extends each grid a step or two past its bound (~900 genotypes under today's vetoes, a full sweep in ~24s). Kill switch `INCUBATOR_RESEARCH=off` returns the search to the cage exactly.
- **THE FALSE POSITIVE, and the fix.** `cage_analysis` v1 flagged **STOP_LOSS** as binding: the best in-cage stop sat ON the −0.04 bound and −0.06 beat it (net 12.86 → 16.34). Adversarially checked before reporting it, and it does not survive — mean net improves **monotonically** as the stop widens across all 180 configs per value (**−15.10** at −0.015 → **−2.37** at −0.06). That is the textbook "a wider stop stops realising losses that later revert" artifact, the same shape that produced and then WITHDREW the funding STOP verdict — and **even the widest stop is mean-NEGATIVE**, so the bound was never what stood between this book and an edge.
- **The bar is now champion-grade**: a cage may be called binding only if the out-of-cage winner is positive, has a positive **lower bound**, AND is positive in **both halves**. A max over 900 candidates with a positive lcb is still the winner's curse `rank()` exists to avoid — measured: **17 of 900 genotypes are both-halves-positive and every one has a negative lcb**, so nothing in the research space clears the bar. All four genes now read `cage_may_bind: false`, which is the correct answer rather than a missing feature. The raw numbers are still REPORTED, never hidden.
- **A mutation found the safety property was call-site-deep.** Passing the unfiltered `scored` list to `select_elite` survived the whole suite, because no selftest can see `run_once`'s argument. The enactable rule now lives **inside `select_elite`**, where no call site can opt out ([[a-kill-switch-must-reach-the-consumer]]) — re-tested: passing an unfiltered list is now harmless, removing the rule from the function is killed.
- 6 mutations total, each red on its named assertion. Suite 304; five guards green (incl. the new `audit_changelog_letters`). Publish-only — no lever, no book, no row, no real money.

## 2026-07-29 (fd) — the tidy-up pass: the build stamp stops lying about WHICH files it hashed, three receipt-semantics bugs fixed, and the CHANGELOG's own integrity gets a guard

- **THE BUILD STAMP NOW PUBLISHES ITS FILE COUNT (`extra.build_n`).** Adding `fleet_tuning.py` to `_BUILD_SHARED` in (fi) made the hashed set IMAGE-DEPENDENT: `_build_files` silently skips a declared-but-absent name (deliberate — `Dockerfile.familyshadow` never COPYs fleet_tuning.py, so the clip lever cannot size a shadow book), so ONE tree stamps `6de64508c304` over 15 files in an image that carries it and `74d3b3178fa8` over 14 in one that does not. Cost, same day: a hash predicted from the REPO read as "the deploy never landed" on a perfectly converged `family-lighter-shadow` — four books chased as an anomaly. ROOT-CAUSED THREE WAYS (repo monkeypatch; an independent agent reconstructing the image's exact COPY set and running that tree's OWN `build_compute`; and the deploy job's verbatim log showing a fresh `containerimage.digest` + push). The born-dark guard structurally cannot catch this class — `fleet_tuning.py` is a DATA dependency of the stamp, not an import. FIX: publish the count beside the digest so a short set is self-describing; `_build_files`/`build_code_files` document why hashing absent files would be WRONG (it would make every image's id depend on files it does not run). CLAUDE.md's verify recipe now says **compare BOTH, and when they disagree check `n` first**. Selftest pins it with a temp-tree fixture (present vs absent shared file ⇒ different id AND smaller count); 2 mutations (drop the stamp; freeze the count) each turn their named assertion red.
- **THREE RECEIPT-SEMANTICS BUGS in (eu)'s §B receipts, found by an adversarial review of my own code — telemetry only, no trade behaviour touched.** (1) SLOPE: ratios were recorded for EVERY ranked candidate, including ones vetoed upstream that the slope gate never adjudicated — inflating n and skewing the distribution the census measures authority from; now recorded only for candidates that reached the gate (opened, or skipped BY slope). (2) CONVICTION: the unclamped score was appended at clip time, so a NOTIONAL-CAP-rejected or order-FAILED candidate counted as an "admitted entry" against its own docstring; now recorded after the fill. (3) EXPLORE: `pool_n` was published even with explore OFF, where the prefilter never populates the pool — a constant 0 that the census's ladder would have read as full swing across the bound, i.e. **spurious authority on a switched-off lever** (the live arm's actual state). Now published only while the pool is live, `explore.off` otherwise ⇒ n=0 ⇒ DARK, the honest verdict. Predicate passed from the caller's own `_ex_floor` so it has ONE source.
- **PER-ASSET CLASSIFICATION MADE FAIL-CLOSED (high-severity, my own (ey) step-3 code).** The gate keyed on a hardcoded frozenset while the UNIVERSE is env-driven, so `FAMILY_NONCRYPTO_COINS=SPY,ARKK` would have routed ARKK down the CRYPTO path and handed it BTC's 4h regime — the D5 violation re-entering through a config edit. `NONCRYPTO_EFFECTIVE = NONCRYPTO_SYMS | set(NONCRYPTO_UNIVERSE)`: anything the operator lists as non-crypto IS gated as non-crypto, and since the oracle will not grade an unknown book the gate then fails CLOSED. Adding an unknown symbol can now only cost entries, never mis-gate one. Selftest drives the exact ARKK fixture.
- **MAIN WAS RED TWICE OVER on the same guard.** `f6d7a2a` shipped `scripts/audit_secret_leak.py` unregistered, so `test_no_unregistered_selftest` — the rot guard for exactly that — failed on clean main. The (fb) session then fixed it by adding the module to `SELFTEST_MODULES`, which makes the SUITE fail anywhere `gitleaks` is absent (both its modes shell out to the binary and FAIL CLOSED — correct behaviour) and **`tests.yml` never installs gitleaks**, so the pytest job was set to go red on the next push. Measured, then moved to `SELFTEST_EXCLUDE` with the reason: its own `changelog-check` job installs a pinned, checksum-verified copy and runs both modes there, so the guard IS enforced — just not from the lean pytest env.
- **`scripts/audit_changelog_letters.py` — the sync channel gets its own guard, and the convention finally gets written down.** Entry letters are citable IDs (`railway-redeploy.yml` cites one; `tests/test_selftests.py` cites one), so a duplicate makes every reference ambiguous. It has now happened **NINE** times, three of them today while this very entry was being written. Era-scoped to >=18-Jul (17-Jul is the deliberate alphabet-restart day and legitimately carries two sequences — measured, not assumed); negative fixture proves it fires; CI-gating in `changelog-check.yml` + `ENFORCED_AUDITS`. The rule itself now lives in CLAUDE.md (continuous sequence; pick your letter at PUSH time; on collision the CITED entry keeps it; record the renumber inline; **git log is not a letter index**; date by git's clock, not by the handoff you are executing).
- **THE 23-JUL (co)-(cr) QUADRUPLE IS REPAIRED — the follow-up its own merge note promised six days ago.** Its note called the overlap "cosmetic"; it was not — four citations were ambiguous, one from tracked code. The parallel session's block moved to (fe)-(fh); the autonomy-brakes block KEPT (co)-(cr) because its entries sit in a contiguous run cited as a RANGE ("the brakes in (cq-ct)") and a range cannot survive a renumber, while every citation into the moved block is a point reference. All 16 edits resolved BY CONTENT (operator quotes, commit subjects, and a provenance proof that the moved set was authored ~13h before the kept set merged, so it cannot cite it); the merge note now carries the full record including what was deliberately left alone.
- Also: this session's five entries were dated 2026-07-30 while git says 2026-07-29 in both UTC and Sydney (it was executing the `NEXT_SESSION_2026-07-30` handoff and dated after the handoff) — corrected, including `explore-floor.yml`'s header comment. Scheduler tidied per P5: 5 spent one-shot triggers deleted (one `list_triggers` call was returning 180k chars).
- Suite green + all five guards green. **Deploy note:** `bot_pnl_store.py` is in EVERY image's hash set, so every service's build id shifts again at its next deploy — expected, and `build_n` now makes the resulting ids self-describing.
## 2026-07-29 (fc) — the incubator searches the WHOLE legal space, not 11 points around one genome

Operator: *"give it everything it needs to explore every option possible."* It turned out not to need more budget — it needed a search that could reach more than one dimension.

- **THE BLOCKER, measured before building.** `seed_population` emits the default genome plus its single-gene AXIS PROBES — it never varies two genes at once. The only thing that explored interactions was `breed()`, which returns `[]` when the elite is empty; `select_elite` demands `both_halves_pos`, and **nothing on this tape clears it**, so the elite IS empty and the population collapsed to **11 axis probes per cycle**. Gene interactions were unreachable by construction, permanently, on any negative surface.
- **And the budget was never the constraint**: one `evaluate()` is **~32ms** on the 2200-snap tape, so the old cycle spent **~0.4s of an hourly 3600s loop** — four hundredths of one percent.
- **`explore_slice()` (new, pure, selftested)** enumerates the JOINT gene product deterministically and walks it with a persistent **cursor**, covering a bounded slice per cycle and wrapping — so successive cycles sweep the entire legal space instead of resampling one neighbourhood. Measured on the live tape: the legal space under today's vetoes is **144 genotypes; the axis probes reached 11 (7.6%)**. The full space now scores in **3.7s** and coverage is published (`exploration.coverage_pct`), because a search that cannot be seen sweeping is indistinguishable from one that has silently stopped — the (f7cad49) explore-zero lesson.
- **It immediately found what the old search could not see: the best genotypes vary TWO or THREE genes at once.** Top of the full sweep is `{DIP_RANGE 0.05, MAX_HOLD_H 48, STOP_LOSS −0.04, TAKE_PROFIT 0.03}` — three genes off default, structurally unreachable by axis probes.
- **And the exhaustive result is a much stronger negative than the old one: 0 of 144 genotypes are both-halves-positive.** That is no longer "we did not find an edge" but "there is no edge in the legal space on this tape". Best full-window net is +$4.16 with h2 −10.25 — the same one-sided shape every other measurement this session found.
- Register stays durable: only the ranked **top 24** of a sweep land in it, or one cycle's population would fill the cap and evict every dormant entry. The cursor deliberately does NOT advance on a cycle that scored nothing, so a dark cycle cannot skip a slice it never measured.
- **A mutation caught a weak test of mine**: the determinism check compared two calls in ONE process, which passes even with `set()` ordering. The cursor has to mean the same genotype next hour in a FRESH interpreter, so enumeration is now pinned structurally (sorted names, first varying fastest) — `set()` and reversed order are both killed. 4 mutations total, each red on its named assertion.
- Also registered `scripts/audit_secret_leak.py`, which landed in `f6d7a2a` (fb) without its `SELFTEST_MODULES` entry and was failing the rot guard for anyone who pulled. Suite **304 passed**; four guards green. Publish-only — no lever, no book, no row, no real money.

## 2026-07-29 (fb) — a secret can no longer reach a commit; and the default scanner was blind to the exact credential that leaked here

Operator asked for hardening beyond Bitdefender + LuLu. This is the repo half: the local pre-commit catch plus the CI backstop. No bot, no lever, no row — tooling only.

- **`scripts/audit_secret_leak.py` + `.gitleaks.toml` + `scripts/install_git_hooks.sh` (all new).** Fifth guard in `changelog-check.yml`, same shape as the other four: a positive run and a `--selftest` negative fixture. gitleaks 8.30.1, pinned and **checksum-verified for all four platforms**; an unpinned platform is REFUSED, not installed with a warning (`audit_sdk_pin` doctrine).
- **MEASURED, and it is the whole reason this is not just `useDefault = true`: gitleaks' DEFAULT ruleset scans a full Postgres connection URI carrying an inline password CLEAN.** That is precisely the credential class that leaked here — a live Postgres URL accumulated into `.claude/settings.local.json` (which stores approved commands VERBATIM) and was one `git add .` from permanent. Four custom rules added for what the default set misses: `db-connection-uri`, `private-key-block`, `aws-access-key-id` (a bare `AKIA…` also scans clean), `railway-token`.
- **The first draft of the allowlist was BROKEN, and its own selftest caught it.** It matched the whole LINE against words like "example"/"masked", so a live GitHub PAT trailing `# example config` sailed straight through — the attacker, or a careless paste, controls the comment. Fixed to match the SECRET ITSELF; the bypass is now a permanent regression fixture. Mutation test went 3/8 → **8/8**.
- **Declared exceptions, not silence** (`BORN_DARK_OK` convention): the freqtrade `api_server` creds in 16 `config_*.json` are exempted by field name AND path only — re-verified 29-Jul that those ports are private-network-only and guard `dry_run: true` PAPER books, so a leak controls no real money. A new AWS key or DB URL in the same file still fails the commit. The guard's own fixtures are exempted PER LINE (`gitleaks:allow`), never by path, so a real secret pasted into the guard is still caught.
- **End-to-end verified, not assumed**: staged a Postgres URL, `git commit` was BLOCKED, nothing landed. Working tree scans clean (84 raw findings → 0, all triaged, none silenced). `.git/hooks` is untracked, so the hook cannot ship by committing it — hence the installer script and the CI job for any clone or `.claude/worktrees/` sibling that never runs it.
- Four existing guards green. Letter raced from (fa).

## 2026-07-29 (fa) — the incubator stops hoarding duplicates and corpses; and the honest answer on "fresh options": there are none in-cage

Operator: *"learning from the incubator so it doesn't make or duplicate bots that don't achieve anything, dead genotypes disposed of and fresh new models and options to test."* Three asks; the first two were real defects and are fixed, the third is answered with measurements rather than invention.

- **The breeding side was already clean** — worth stating so nobody re-fixes it: `select_elite` demands `both_halves_pos`, so a losing genotype can never become a gamete. Every defect found was in the REGISTER.
- **`prune_register()` (new, pure, selftested) — dedupe / dispose / re-rank.** Measured on the live register: **64 entries → 32 kept, 32 duplicates collapsed**, and the register was sitting **AT the 64 cap**, so the defects below were actively evicting live genotypes.
  - **DUPLICATES.** Two genotypes differing only in genes the current veto makes unevolvable are ONE experiment — the organ cannot tell them apart, and showing both invents a roster. Collapsed on the genotype REDUCED to currently-evolvable genes; the freshest representative survives and carries `merged_dupes`.
  - **IMMORTAL CORPSES — the worst of the three.** Cap eviction sorted dormants by `best_lcb`, a HIGH-WATER MARK, so one lucky historical score outranked every recently-measured genotype forever. Ranking is now on the LATEST reading, with entries scored this cycle ahead of any dormant.
  - **DISPOSAL, and it names which kind of dead**: `INERT` (dormant, never traded — achieves nothing) vs `LOSING` (dormant, fired and lost). Denominated in cycles seen and the latest reading, **never** `best_net` — the whole failure being fixed is a lucky score outliving the measurement that refuted it. Fail-safe throughout: never retires on unknown staleness, on a genotype scored this cycle, below the cycle floor, or on a positive latest reading; and the whole pass is skipped on a short tape (no scoring, no disposal). Disposal is REPORTED on `register_hygiene`, never silent.
- **"Fresh new models and options" — measured, and there are none available inside the cages.** Stated plainly because manufacturing some is exactly the behaviour being asked to stop:
  - **Every one of the 7 taker gene grids already spans its registry bounds exactly** (brk_range 0.90–0.97, dip_range 0.05–0.15, momo_chg 3–6, div_gap_pp 37.5–87.5, tp 0.03–0.06, sl −0.04…−0.02, max_hold 24–72). Zero unused headroom — new alleles would need a cage widening, which is an evidence/operator decision (the A1 class).
  - **One unbred registry lever existed** — `taker.sl_cooldown_h`, the same shape as the DIV_GAP_PP gap closed on 17-Jul, and the replay mirrors it faithfully so it IS measurable. Swept before proposing it: it is live **versus OFF** (0h → −$15.80 vs −$7.00) but **inert across its usable range** — 1h through 24h spans **$1.88**, against a one-fill swing of $3.50. Breeding it would mint genotypes differing by less than the evidence quantum ([[incubator-evidence-denominated-in-fills]]). **REJECTED on measurement**, recorded so nobody re-proposes it.
  - Finer resolution inside the existing spans was also rejected: the whole taker surface is negative on this window (default genome −$24.73 all-lens), so a finer search only searches a losing surface more finely.
- 5 mutations each turn their named assertion red: disposal reading `best_net`, retiring on unknown staleness, retiring a genotype scored this cycle, ranking on the high-water mark, and deduping on the full genotype. Suite 303; four guards green. Publish-only — no lever, no book, no row.

## 2026-07-29 (ez) — a dormant register row now states that it is stale, instead of looking like a measurement

> LETTER RACE, recorded rather than papered over: this landed as (ey) and was renumbered to (ez) on rebase — a concurrent session had taken **both (ex) and (ey)** the same afternoon (its entries are dated 2026-07-30; git says all of today's commits are 29-Jul Sydney). My (ex) below therefore duplicates a sibling (ex) and is left alone deliberately: its letter is already cited in commit `ccb5257` and in the study header, and a rename would break live references to buy tidiness. Second collision in one session — the letters are a weak coordination device under concurrency ([[read-the-changelog-before-building]]).

- **The trap, walked into first-hand.** A DORMANT prospect keeps the net/h1/h2/closes/lcb it had when it was last scored — `update_prospects` changed only `role`. Read straight off the payload those frozen numbers are indistinguishable from a current reading, and they are not close: the (ex) admission test measured the top dormant's carried **+$39.81** re-scoring to **−$11.12** on the live tape, a ~$51 gap in a field that looks current. This session quoted that +$39.81 to the operator as a live number before testing it.
- **Fix — every entry now states its own currency**: `stats_current` (was it scored this cycle?) and `stats_age_h` (hours since `last_seen`). Staleness was always *derivable* by comparing `last_seen` to the payload's `updated`, but that required every consumer to think of it, and one that didn't simply read a stale number confidently — the same "alive but wrong" class `fleet_immune` exists for. `_age_h` fails to **None**, never 0.0: an unknown age must not read as fresh.
- **The frozen numbers are untouched** — the stamp is the fix, not a rewrite of history, and `best_net`/`best_lcb` keep their deliberate high-water-mark semantics ("never regress on a worse re-score"), which are right for a register and never an admission decision. Publish-only; nothing trades on it.
- 3 mutations each turn their named assertion red: dormant stamped `stats_current=True`, unparseable age falling back to 0.0, and a dormant carry silently re-scoring. Suite 303; four guards green.

## 2026-07-29 (ex) — CAN the prospects join the fleet? Tested. No — and the register's headline numbers do not reproduce

- **`scripts/study_prospect_admission.py` (new)** answers the operator's "can we test if they are able to join the fleet" by scoring every register genotype TWICE on the same tape through the incubator's OWN `evaluate()` (not a parallel implementation): once ALL-LENS (reproducing what the register recorded) and once FLEET-FILLABLE (`live_lenses(fresh_lens_fwd(...))` — the trades the book would actually get to make), then putting the survivor through the real `assess_champion` gate. The gap between the two rows IS the answer, because the replay harness is veto-blind by design.
- **Verdict: NOT ONE of the 30 register genotypes clears the gate** (tape 2200 snaps / 184h; fillable = `{dip}`; breakout/momentum/divergence all brain-vetoed). Three independent reasons, all measured:
  - **The headline numbers do not reproduce.** The best prospect's recorded **+$39.81** re-scores to **−$11.12 all-lens** on today's tape. The register stores `best_net`/`best_lcb` — a deliberate HIGH-WATER MARK ("never regress on a worse re-score"), which is right for a register and wrong for an admission decision. Read `best_*` as a record, never as a current reading.
  - **What survives the veto is ~flat and fails both halves**: best fleet-fillable +$7.65 with h2 **−8.86**; every candidate lands 9–11 fillable closes against a **40-close floor**. The one fillable lens does not trade enough on this tape to have a fitness at all.
  - **30 register rows reduce to 16 distinct experiments** once vetoed-lens genes are removed — nearly half the "roster" is copies differing only in a `BRK_RANGE` the fleet cannot exercise.
- Context that keeps the verdict honest: the **DEFAULT genome is also negative** (−$24.73 all-lens, −$10.27 fillable), so this is not candidate selection failing — the whole taker surface is negative on this window, corroborating the recorded "no taker lens is significantly positive". Nothing here is evidence that a *better* genotype exists.
- Read-only: loads tape + register, scores in memory, writes nothing. No lever, no book, no dashboard row. Selftest offline (veto reduction, signature dedupe, ordering, empty-state) and registered. Suite 303 passed; four guards green.
- Also recorded (`6dd0f66`): the next cohort to earn dashboard rows is named for **Australian musicians** (`band-<surname>-lshadow`, mirroring `pm-<surname>-lshadow`) — a naming rule, explicitly not a licence to mint books, since neither the judge nor the incubator can create a row (candidates are levers on EXISTING books; minting one is a build).

## 2026-07-29 (ew) — the judge's next slot goes to the candidate the venue's tape actually supports

- **Serial queue REORDERED: `slope-gate-off` now runs before `tp-0.06`** (operator: "proceed on obvious best decisions"). The 28-Jul review (D7) queued them the other way round, and that order was correct *on its premise* — tp-0.06 was believed evidence-backed because "take_profit IS the Farmer's measured-positive exit family". The (ev) study measured that premise on Lighter's own tape and it does not survive: the exit-family claim is TRUE but does not imply raising the bar (TP exits are the only positive family at $1.010/trade, and 0.04→0.06 cuts them 224→117, pushing those trades into flip/cold/max_hold — two different populations), NO take_profit value is both-halves positive at either universe or either slip, and tp-0.06 vs live is **+$13.89 on top-25 but −$50.43 on all-79**. slope-gate-off by contrast carries a real Lighter prior in the supported direction: gate-on −$14.90 vs gate-off +$34.07 @5bps on durable history (dp).
- **tp-0.06 is NOT deleted** — it moves to second. A mute backtest is not a negative one, and the judge's paired live-vs-shadow bar is independent forward evidence the tape cannot provide. What changed is only which candidate spends the NEXT ≥7d slot on the fleet's only path to `live.funding.*`. Restrict-safe: no bar moved, no lever changed, the paired bar still gates every promotion. Verified against live state — `next_candidate` now returns `slope-gate-off` (cooldown lifts 30-Jul 19:20 AEST).
- The original rationale line is kept **verbatim** in `CANDIDATES` rather than deleted: its numbers are real, and it is the INFERENCE from them that was falsified. Order is now pinned by two selftest assertions (`names[:2]`, and the `next_candidate` chain) so a silent re-shuffle of the promotion queue cannot pass unnoticed.
- **NOT done, and deliberately**: `XPJ_GROWTH_DAYS` stays at 2.5d. The judge's own newly-published `growth_reach` confirms the floor is unreachable (`rate 4.07/day → 10.2 projected vs a 15-close floor`), and ~4d would make it attainable without touching the evidence quantity — but that widens a gate whose promotion writes `live.funding.*` on the real Farmer, against a recorded operator preference. Measurement published, decision left with the operator for the 04-Aug review.
- Suite 302 passed; four audit guards green; 3 mutations (order un-pinned in either assertion, and the reorder reverted) each turn their named assertion red. Ships with freqtrade-bots; no real-money service touched.

## 2026-07-29 (ev) — the promotion pipeline stops failing SILENTLY: sterility reported, drift un-blinded, and a harness that was re-speccing studies

- **The incubator→live funding pipeline is STERILE, and now says so.** `strategy_incubator.funding_proposals` marks a name tried if it sits in this organ's lifetime `proposed` ledger; that ledger holds all 8 names ever minted, which covers **every** name `FUNDING_GENES` can generate (4 non-baseline alleles after `max_hold_h` was structurally retired). Measured against the live payloads: `funding_proposals(...) == []` on every cycle, `xp-queue` republishing empty via the 28-Jul heartbeat, `strategy-incubator.proposed` frozen at 8 — the reproduction organ had gone sterile in **total silence**. New `proposal_capacity()` reports `{generatable, untried, names, exhausted}` on the payload plus a loud log line; `_funding_candidates()` is now the SINGLE source the emitter and the reporter share, so they cannot disagree about sterility. Deliberately NOT "fixed" by re-proposing from lifetime memory: 7 of the 8 are refuted (0.3/0.5 clamp to the already-run 0.075; both hold-caps recorded-refuted; 0.0375/0.0625 sit in the region Lighter's tape measured worst), so resurrection would spend 7-day judge slots re-running known answers. **Correction to the first read of this defect**: the pipeline does not go DEAD — `pick_candidate`'s `DONE_RETRY_D` fallback re-runs decided candidates after 28d, so the real failure is "no new hypotheses, only retries", which is what the report says.
- **Arm-drift: the row-seniority rule was pinning a blind spot, and is WITHDRAWN.** `_arm_drift_snapshot` treated "both arms have stamped rows and they match" as a FINAL all-clear that the current-build check could not override. Measured live: both Farmer arms' newest stamped rows read `ddd019900bf0` (28-Jul 20:00/20:02) while `bot_pnl` had them running `c2d0ccc64d7d` (live) and `89c2c56b2da5` (shadow) — two different builds, **neither the one the gate was certifying as converged**, i.e. the CHANGELOG (eq) claim that the pair stayed converged no longer held. The rows describe the past honestly; the promotion they gate spends FUTURE money through today's containers, so the current check always gets a vote now (`source: bot_pnl-current`). RESTRICT-ONLY — `arm_drift` claims only on positive evidence, so this adds holds and clears none, and `unknown` stays quiet. The selftest assertion that encoded the old doctrine was replaced with its inverse and the reversal documented in place, not silently flipped.
- **The growth pair's faster bar is UNREACHABLE at the arm's own rate — reported, not auto-widened.** 15 shadow closes in a 2.5d trailing window silently assumes ~6 closes/day; `perps-funding-lighter-lshadow` is closing ~1.6/day (4 in 2.5d, 19 in 7d, 58 in 14d), and only 1 close carries the growth receipt (they began 28-Jul 20:02). New `growth_reachable()` measures the arm's rate and publishes `growth_reach` on `xp-judge` + warns, so a multi-week "floors: shadow N/15" is distinguishable from a bar that cannot be met. **Deliberately report-only**: the 2.5d window is a recorded operator preference, and an organ must not quietly widen a real-money promotion window to make its own bar easier (`XPJ_GROWTH_DAYS` stays the operator's knob). Fail-safe UNKNOWN on a short/dark ledger; the projection is optimistic (receipt-ungated) so `False` means definitely-not.

DOCTRINE in the header: floors only ratchet UP (raise them in the PR that raised coverage); lowering one is an operator decision with a CHANGELOG entry; a floored file MISSING from the measurement is itself a breach (a rename/delete cannot silently delete its floor). Files with no tests yet (marks/shadow/hl_client/render()) are deliberately absent — a floor is a ratchet on ground already held. Selftest proves the detector sees breach + hold + missing; registered in `GUARD_ONLY_AUDITS` (full check gates in its own job, where coverage.json exists).
- **`tests/test_coverage_policy.py` — the born-dark pattern applied to TESTS** (the Finding-7 closure): every module calling `publish()`/`publish_paper_trade()` must be registered in `SELFTEST_MODULES`, imported by a `tests/` file, or DECLARED in `PUBLISH_TEST_OK` with a reason. The roster is SCANNED from the tree (never hand-maintained; the scan asserts it can still see the known surface so an API rename fails loud). Only RETIRED bots may be declared (a running book needs tests, not an exception — enforced), stale declarations fail, declared-AND-registered fails. Today: 13 registered + poller via tests/ + exactly the 4 retired bots declared (Trail Blazer, Bounce Catcher, Stock Leaders, Launch Sniper). Resurrecting one means removing its declaration and adding tests in the same commit.
- Suite: **278 passed** (274 + 3 policy + 1 guard fixture). Floors verified green against the real measurement before shipping (tightest margins: farmer 46.6 vs 45, taker 91.8 vs 90). ## 2026-07-29 (eg) — the release race closed single-writer, explore-zero SOLVED (the tail was always empty), and a blind live boot finally pages

- **Judge + release tool (audit R1 CLOSED, the honest way).** The (dw) tool wrote the judge's state directly under a row lock, but the judge's own hourly read→compute→save holds no lock — a release committed mid-cycle was overwritten by the judge's stale save seconds AFTER the tool printed RELEASED (a tool-side compare-and-swap cannot fix this; the losing write is the judge's). Now single-writer: `--execute` QUEUES a TTL'd request row (`xp-judge-release-request`, own updated+ttl_sec, fail-closed on staleness); the judge consumes it at cycle start through its own save path (`consume_release_request` + `release_transition`, both pure) — state written BEFORE the tombstone so a failed write retries next cycle; outcome (released / stale / mismatch / not-running) tombstoned on the request key, phone push on release. `--execute-direct` keeps the old direct write for the one case queuing cannot serve (a DEAD judge container), race caveat printed. The ABANDON transition itself moved to `experiment_judge.release_transition` — SINGLE SOURCE; the tool's `release_payload` is a thin wrapper and its unchanged selftest proves the payload byte-identical. Judge selftest + tool selftest green; freshness fail-closed and name-match refusal mutation-tested.
- **EXPLORE-ZERO SOLVED — measured, and it was never the cursor (`EXPLORE_ZERO_DIAGNOSIS_2026-07-29.md`).** Explore samples from `prelim[15:]`, but prelim requires |apr|≥5% AND ≥$10M turnover AND 4h persistence — and the venue has only ~11 books above $10M at all (202 active, 114 pass the gate, **3 pass both**; tail = 0 even at a $1M floor). The tail explore sweeps has structurally never had a member: the 24-Jul engine shipped sampling an empty set, f7cad49's cursor bugs were real but irrelevant, and the shadow arm's `SCAN_EXPLORE_K=2` has been an INERT flag — **the operator's growth A/B has really been measuring conviction-only** (receipts honest, `src=exploit` on every row, exactly as the ledger shows). O6's "explore lifts cadence" can never happen without a design change. Three fix options routed (explore-specific prefilter through the SAME B/C vetoes — the lean; lower the deep cut; re-scope the A/B honestly) — strategy surface, backtest-first, operator's call. No entry logic touched.
- **Farmer + watchdog (audit R5 CLOSED): a blind live boot now pages.** The Farmer publishes `extra.live_state_blind` while degraded (entries blocked, ':live' save suppressed — previously log-only, row said "online"); `fleet_watchdog_svc.evaluate` raises it as a PROBLEM (pages via the fleet's normal phone path), self-clearing on heal, fail-safe on junk extras. Both selftested (watchdog gains evaluate() fixtures). Farmer half rides the next live dispatch; watchdog half auto-deploys.
- Tests: full suite green; guards green; farmer 9/9, judge (+release-channel cases), tool, watchdog selftests green; new guards mutation-verified.

## 2026-07-28 (dz) — LIVE-MONEY surface hardening from the full-fleet audit: the Farmer's deposit double-count (phantom halt), the unchecked ':live' boot restore (durable state wipe), the same-loop conviction cap under-count, and four smaller truths

- **D1 DOUBLE-COUNT (lighter_funding_bot).** The boot/day-roll `day_start_equity = account_value()` goes through the EquityGuard, which can ACCEPT a deposit/withdrawal on that very read and buffer it — so the captured baseline is already capital-INCLUSIVE. The first loop then popped that same buffered move and shifted the baseline AGAIN (`capital_adjusted_day_start`): deposit D → day_start=E+2D vs equity=E+D → a phantom 'loss' of D, and a D ≥ 10% of book FLATTENS + HALTS the live book on the operator's own deposit. Fixed: the buffered moves are folded into the ledger immediately after any FRESH capture that survives boot (tracked via `_baseline_is_boot_read`; a PERSISTED pre-move baseline restored over the capture keeps the shift, which is then the correction) and after the day-roll capture. The taker already had this ordering (fold before adopt, no shift on the adopt path) — one rule, both live bots.
- **':live' BOOT RESTORE now CHECKED ([[load-state-seeds-durable-state-on-a-failed-read]], the whole blob this time).** The 22-Jul fix hardened the quarantine read; meta/explore_seen/initial_equity/capital_adjust still came through the UNCHECKED `load_state`. One boot-time DB blip wiped every position's max-hold clock/entry bars/conviction clip/explore src, re-based `live_baseline` to current equity on the first loop (lifetime P&L → zero), zeroed the D1 capital ledger — and the next save made it all DURABLE. Now: ONE checked read with the quarantine's own bounded retry; a genuinely failed read boots DEGRADED (`live_state_blind`): NEW entries blocked, the ':live' save suppressed (never overwrite a blob we failed to read), baseline adoption refused, and an in-loop self-heal re-reads until clean (halt record senior on heal). The exact `quarantine_blind` pattern, one shelf up.
- **SAME-LOOP CONVICTION CAP UNDER-COUNT (lighter_funding_bot).** `open_notional` prices a position in `pos` at its meta clip but a same-loop open counted only in `open_now` at the FLAT clip — so the 2nd open of a loop under-counted the 1st by up to (conviction_hi−1)×order_usd ≈ $30 at hi=2.2: a cap breach armed for the day conviction promotes to live. Fixed: a successful open is inserted into `pos` immediately (counts MORE notional, never less). Selftest pins the $126-vs-$90 shape.
- **Smaller truths, same pass:** Farmer W/L now counts wins on TOTAL P&L (price+funding) on the flatten path and counts them AT ALL on the blind-stop path (a profitable stop_blind close always published as a loss); the Taker's halted-day state save carries `sl_block` again (every halted cycle was erasing the post-stop cooldown stamps) and pages on a failed write; `implementation_shortfall.arm_drift` keeps the NEWEST row per bot (the judge passes DESC paper_trades rows — the old dict-comp graded each arm by its OLDEST close, masking current drift and asserting stale drift); `fleet_bus.stake_multiplier`'s docstring stops claiming "reduce-only" (two-way since 21-Jul).
- Tests: Farmer 8/8 + judge + impl-shortfall + bus selftests green; full suite 200 passed; born-dark / deploy-coverage / venue-purity / sdk-pin green. Deploys: shadow files ride the auto path; the LIVE Farmer/Taker pick these up at their next operator-dispatched redeploy ([deploy-live-farmer] / workflow 305025607) — nothing here deploys real money by itself.

## 2026-07-28 (dw) — the judge gains an OPERATOR-RELEASE path: `scripts/xp_judge_release.py` (born from review D3a — the operator ordered the contaminated 0.075 candidate released, and the only ways out were MAX_DAYS or raw DB surgery)

- **THE TOOL.** `release_payload()` reproduces the judge's own ABANDON `save(...)` field-for-field (phase idle, name into done/done_at, COOLDOWN_H cooldown, started_ts cleared, verdict appended + capped, last_eval preserved) with verdict `RELEASED-OPERATOR` + the operator's stated reason; constants/iso imported FROM experiment_judge (single source, no drift copy). Writes state + the fleet_regen history snapshot in one transaction so a later regen cannot resurrect the released candidate. The xp levers are deliberately untouched: an idle judge stops re-asserting and they TTL-expire (~2h), the twin reverting to env defaults — the judge's own fail-safe lapse.

Verified against the live DB in dry-run (reads the running 0.075 candidate, refuses nothing, produces the payload). The EXECUTE is the operator's hand, per the permission model.

## 2026-07-28 (dv) — CROSS-CYCLE up-regime cache COMPLETED: the (dk) candle read stops re-fetching every 5-min boot (~12x/hour → ~1/TTL per book; shadow-only in effect)

- **WHY.** The bot is RUN-ONCE, so `_UP_CACHE`'s 1h TTL died at every boot and `up_read` re-fetched each breakout candidate's DAILY candles every cycle — ~12x/hour for a signal that moves once a day, and worse since (dk) hoisted the read ahead of the veto (every breakout ticket triggers it). The (dg) audit flagged the dead cache; this makes the documented TTL real by persisting it in bot_state (`<bot_row>-upregime`).
- **WHAT.** `load_upregime_cache`/`save_upregime_cache` round-trip the UNEXPIRED entries (each `[up, strength, expiry_epoch]`) across boots; wired into `main()` gated `BULL_MODE and "breakout" in _allowed` — the LIVE arm never even takes the state read (its allow-list filters breakout before the relabel), so this is shadow-only in effect and a pure no-op on real money. Fail-safe both ways: a dark/corrupt read seeds nothing (up_read fetches fresh), a dark write drops the cache, junk shapes are ignored. TTL semantics are UNCHANGED (the same 1h the (dd) design claimed) — persistence just makes the claim true.
- **PROVENANCE, honestly.** Authored 24-Jul as (dm) WIP; a shared-tree rebase autostash carried it, and the review session's whole-file taker commit swept the PARTIAL in ((du)/f4f55cc shipped the helpers + an unconditional load with NO save — a harmless wasted read per cycle, key never written). This completes it: letter → (dv) ((dm) was taken by Increment B), load gated, save wired, selftests added. [[git-discipline-in-a-shared-tree]] made flesh.
- **Tests.** Round-trip through a stub store (served-from-cache with NO venue; strength round-trips), expiry dropped at BOTH ends (both mutation-caught RED), dark store seeds/drops silently, junk payload shapes seed nothing. Both taker entry points + replay + born-dark + venue-purity green. Ships to the shadow container on the freqtrade-bots auto path; no live dispatch (live path untouched by construction).

## 2026-07-25 (dt) — REFUTED on LIGHTER: the funding-slope gate is an HL-only edge; making its history durable (the Phase-2 "fix") would HURT the losing book (operator: "backtest the fix first")

- **The measurement that had never been run.** The Farmer's slope gate ("enter only while |apr| still BUILDING, >= 1h ago") is validated ONLY on Hyperliquid (`backtest_funding_leverage.py`: +1.3%→+8.4%, both halves) — `backtest_funding_lighter.py`'s own line ~156 said it was "not modelled" here. Added an OPTIONAL slope gate to that Lighter sim (default OFF → the existing sweep verdict is byte-identical, CONFIRMED against the default run) in three modes: `off` / `durable` (always active — the proposed fix) / `failopen` (live reality: ~11 restarts/day × 1h ≈ 46% of hours the gate is OFF). Mirrors the live `_slope_ref` exactly — the funding TAPE is the history (`apr 1h ago = m["fund"][t-3600]`), and a missing value FAILS OPEN, since a real tape gap IS the restart gap. Merged cleanly with the concurrent (dq) `entry_ok` hook (both entry-site params coexist).
- **RESULT (79 markets, 150d, both slips 5.0 + 0.86bps, gates 0.05/0.20/0.40): DURABLE never beats OFF.** Live gate 0.05: durable −14.90 vs off +34.07 (5bps); durable +3.11 vs off +53.57 (0.86bps). Across all 6 gate×slip cells `durable ≤ off`, usually worse by $5–50. The gate REMOVES net-positive entries on Lighter — it concentrates entries into the most-crowded (peak-funding) moments and durable's price P&L is markedly worse. Nothing is both-halves-positive (h1 negative in every row), consistent with the Farmer's known both-halves-negative Lighter verdict.
- **DURABLE ≤ FAILOPEN too** — the restart fail-open is accidentally MITIGATING a gate that is wrong for Lighter. So "recovering" the gate via durable slope history (the Phase-2 code change) would GIVE MONEY BACK (0.05/0.86bps: durable +3.11 vs failopen +24.81).
- **VERDICT: no bot edit.** The Phase-2 hypothesis ("restarts disable a validated edge") is REFUTED — the edge is HL-only and Lighter-NEGATIVE, the exact "backtest on Lighter only" failure the operator rule exists for. Backtest-first prevented a real-money change that would have worsened the losing book. (Separately: the slope gate itself hurts on this window — flagged, not actioned; it's live-config and both-halves is regime-bound on the single-regime tape.)
- **Complementary to the same-day (dq)/(ds) vol-character win.** This rules the slope TIMING out as a Lighter edge; the vol-character (coin-quality) filter is the config that DID measure both-halves-positive and went live via the (dn) marker. Two independent Farmer knobs on Lighter's own tape, opposite verdicts — the losing book's lever was coin CHARACTER, not entry timing.

## 2026-07-24 (dp) — the radar's trajectory sensor de-biased: gate the decaying/emerging label on the calibrated slope_t, not the sample-size-biased traj_delta + the audit-backlog triage filed (shadow-only; no real-money change)

- **THE BIAS (found auditing the (cn) radar's own new sensor).** The trajectory gate used `traj_delta = t_recent − t_full` — a HALF-sample t-stat minus a FULL-sample one. Because t scales with √n, that difference is biased NEGATIVE by ~(1/√2 − 1)·t from sample size ALONE, growing with t (Monte-Carlo mean −0.49 at t≈2, −1.53 at t≈6). A same-level book whose recent half is merely NOISIER (lower t, not lower mean) trips it with ZERO real decay: **~15% of constant-effect books read "decaying."** `slope_t` — a calibrated OLS-slope t-stat (~N(0,1) under a constant effect, **−14** on the real Farmer shape) — was already computed but UNUSED.
- **THE FIX.** The decaying/emerging label now gates on `slope_t` (≤ −2.0 / ≥ +2.0, a *significant* trend) with the median/faded corroboration unchanged; `traj_delta` stays a reported diagnostic. Measured false-"decaying" on constant books drops **~15% → ~2%**. The Farmer verdict is unaffected — it was over-determined by the decisive `_faded` ratio + independent thirds + the concentration collapse, never the trajectory gate (see `RADAR_TRAJECTORY_AUDIT_2026-07-23.md`).
- **MUTATION-TESTED, and the fixture DISTINGUISHES the gates.** New selftest 9b: a same-level, noisier-recent book (`traj_delta = −1.22`, `slope_t = 0.0`, recent median −0.5) must read STABLE. Reverting the gate to `traj_delta ≤ −1.0` flips it to "decaying" and the selftest goes RED (verified). Suite now 19 checks; `fleet_radar --selftest` green.
- **HONEST SCOPE.** `fleet_radar` is shadow, publish-only, zero consumers — no real money. The gain is a Monte-Carlo-measured noise-robustness property; a live-Lighter-tape confirmation that it never UNDER-catches a genuine gradual fade remains a follow-up (needs DB). The (cn) deploy-orphan fix and the deploy-coverage CI wiring I also scoped this session were landed independently by the bull change set (already on main) — not re-submitted here.
- **AUDIT-BACKLOG TRIAGE filed** (`AUDIT_BACKLOG_TRIAGE_2026-07-23.md`): the 5 lever-authority findings (live/xp.funding.enter_apr INERT-PINNED = the HL-hourly→Lighter-8h units bug; EXIT_APR/HARD_STOP UNLEVERED = real-money loss-decisive knobs; taker.sl_cooldown_h DARK) and the venue-purity backtest backlog — all real-money-gated or data-starved, verified still current on today's main, routed to the 28-Jul review, none declared in `*_OK` unilaterally.
- born-dark (20) + venue-purity (89) + deploy-coverage + conflict-marker guards green. `fleet_radar.py` rides the freqtrade-bots auto-deploy (now covered — the (cn)/(dg) orphan fix).

Now drops the forming bar (`raw = raw[:-1]`), matching the two sibling daily-regime reads (`lighter_trend_bot` `candles[:-1]`, family `parse_candles`). This is the gate the shadow capturability read depends on. Pinned by a falling-history-with-spiking-partial-bar test (mutation-caught).
- **FIX 2 — the live-exit inertness guard was TT_TRAIL_PCT, not BULL_MODE** (main() exit loop). (de) began passing `peak_ret` unconditionally, which un-dormanted the global `TT_TRAIL_PCT` knob on main(): with bull OFF but `TT_TRAIL_PCT>0`, a real-money position would silently switch from the fixed bracket to a trailing trend exit. main() now passes `trail=0.0` (never None) when bull is off, so the fixed bracket holds regardless of `TT_TRAIL_PCT` — the guard now IS BULL_MODE, matching the (de) claim. No active risk (live not redeployed, `TT_TRAIL_PCT` unset), but it is a real-money-exit latent hazard. Guarded by a bull-off + `TRAIL_PCT=0.06` inertness test (mutation-caught).
- **FIX 3 — the breakout trend exit bypassed the entry-stamped max-hold** (main() exit loop). `bars=(_ebars or pos_bars(m))` discarded the entry stamp for breakout, so `bull_exit`'s live module `MAX_HOLD_H` (a growth-rail lever) re-timed an open breakout mid-position — re-opening the (bw) flap class the entry-stamp fix closed. Now grafts the stamped max-hold onto the trend tp/sl (`(999, BRK_SL, pos_bars(m)[2])`); tp/sl stay the trend policy, hold honours the stamp. Guarded by a stamped-1h-hold test (mutation-caught).
- **FIX 4 — replay's breakout was NOT inert** (`lighter_ticket_replay.py`). Replay called `bull_entry_ok` without `up`, falling back to the oracle stamp, so breakout-long fired in replay on a DIFFERENT gate than the shadow arm's candle-EMA read — a non-comparable leg polluting the tuner's leaderboard, and the "breakout inert in replay" doc was false. Replay now forces `up=False` for breakout (long refused = truly inert); divergence keeps the stamp fallback. The shadow arm is the sole breakout instrument, as designed.
- **NOT fixed (correctly refuted / benign):** candle-order sort (Lighter returns chronological, verified against the API contract); up_read TTL "caching" (run-once = intra-cycle only — docstring corrected, behaviour benign/fail-closed); the up=None→oracle-fallback on a candle outage (restrict-only, admits at most the oracle's own graded verdict).

## 2026-07-23 (cv) — "COMPLETE BOTH": the funding arm is VIABLE at MEASURED cost (cu corrected), and shadow-widening is NOT warranted — the tuner already widens winners on evidence (operator: "complete both and save")

Two evidence-first follow-throughs on the "then widen" work:

- **EXECUTION-COST — (cu)'s "cost-bound, idle it" was an artifact of an UNFOUNDED 5bps slip; at the MEASURED ~0.5bps the arm is a real both-halves-positive edge.** `backtest_funding_lighter.py`'s `SLIP` is the load-bearing constant and its 5bps default "came from nothing" (its own comment). The fill-telemetry organ has since measured the live Farmer's round-trip slip at **~0.47-0.54 bps/fill** ("live executes TIGHTER than its shadow model ~0.87"). Re-running the gate sweep across the plausible band: live gate 0.05 = **+$33.47/180d, BOTH HALVES + (win 59%)** at 0.5bps, and it stays both-halves-positive out to 2bps (+$23); it only decays toward zero at the unfounded 5bps. **Widening the gate UP still loses in both halves at EVERY slip level** — slip-invariant, so that conclusion stands. Corrected verdict: KEEP the arm and its 0.05 gate (it earns at real cost); do NOT idle, do NOT widen; the one thing that kills it is slip drifting past ~2bps, which the fill-telemetry organ already watches. `FUNDING_GATE_LIGHTER_2026-07-23.md` rewritten; the backtest `SLIP` comment now records the measurement (default left at 5.0 as a pessimistic reference — run `BT_SLIP_BPS=0.5` for the real read). No live change — the gate is already right.

- **SHADOW-WIDENING — investigated against the LIVE tuner and found NOT warranted; the autonomy already widens winners on evidence.** Read `scout_tuner` on /bus.json: it has ENACTED two evidence-backed widenings today (`scout.dip_range_max→0.2`, `scout.div_gap_pp→30.0` — winner lenses n≈944/1062), REJECTS unproven widens on its own replay gate ("no improvement on a half"), and respects brain vetoes (breakout/momentum held at floor). `brain_stake_mults.mults` is EMPTY — nothing is pinned at the 1.5 expand ceiling; the brain expands nothing because no tag yet clears the (now brake-validated) expand bars. Regime is risk-off (event-sentinel −0.54), so the tuner is correctly crouching (`taker.momo_chg→6.0`). Conclusion: no bound is binding — the bottleneck is EVIDENCE, not bound width, and forcing a wider registry bound would be motion against the tuner's own replay verdict (the exact "authority without evidence" failure the brakes exist to prevent). The productive path to more autonomous widening is more winning-tag evidence + the validated brain expand — both now in place — not wider bounds.

Doc + comment + CHANGELOG only; no code behaviour, no real-money change. Both land on the draft PR behind the merge.

## 2026-07-23 (cu) — "THEN WIDEN": the evidence says the live funding gate must NOT widen up — measured on Lighter's own tape (operator: "proceed")

- **THE PIVOT FROM BRAKES TO EVIDENCE.** With the brakes in (cq-ct), the real-money widening was gated on ONE thing: a gate that's actually profitable on Lighter's tape. Ran `scripts/backtest_funding_lighter.py --days 180 --universe 25 --refresh` against Lighter's own API (settled hourly fundings + candles, 25 markets, 2026-01-24→07-23) — Lighter-only, per doctrine. Result in `FUNDING_GATE_LIGHTER_2026-07-23.md`.
- **WIDENING THE ENTRY GATE UP LOSES — decisively.** The gate curve: 0.02 +$19.82 (both halves +), 0.05 (LIVE) +$3.57 (both +), then it goes NEGATIVE and stays there — 0.08 −$10.54, 0.12 −$47.14 (both halves −), 0.20 −$28.38 (both −), 0.30 −$24.78 (both −). The premise "the lever needs more space to operate" is, for THIS lever, backwards: more space upward = more loss. The lever-authority audit's "the decisive ~10% apr sits above the 0.075 ceiling" is real, but reaching it is a LOSS — the tight ceiling is PROTECTIVE.
- **THE STRUCTURAL FINDING (bigger than the sweep): the strategy is COST-bound, not gate-bound.** At gate 0.05, carry earned over the 9h median hold = 0.5bps vs a 10bps round-trip slip → **operative breakeven ≈ 97% apr**, and the venue floor is 3.5% / ETH ~8%. 56% of trades exit because the funding signal EVAPORATED (flip+cold) before the carry could out-run slippage. The small positive rows are a few tp winners barely clearing cost — noise around zero, not a durable edge. No gate change fixes a cost structure.
- **CORRECTION to an earlier read.** The audit relayed the live LEDGER as "negative in both halves"; the SIMULATED gate at 0.05 is marginally POSITIVE both halves (+6.79/+1.33). Both are true — the live book is negative from execution + coin concentration (radar `cn`), the gate itself is ~breakeven in idealised fills. The unifying truth is stronger and unchanged: the edge is smaller than the cost, so widening the gate up automates a loss.
- **RECOMMENDATION (operator authority — surfaced, not taken):** do NOT widen `live.funding.enter_apr` up; the tight bound stays. The productive lever is COST (longer holds on genuinely high-funding coins, fewer flips) or idling the live arm — not the gate. `live/xp.funding.enter_apr`'s INERT finding is left OPEN on purpose (the lever-authority guard uses it as a test fixture); the WHY-it's-correct lives in the evidence doc. Doc + guard-comment only; no code, no behaviour, no money moved. Suite green; guard selftest green.

## 2026-07-23 (ct) — AUTONOMY BRAKE 4: the live Farmer no longer books PHANTOM closes that corrupt the deciders' data (operator: "fix the brakes, then widen")

- **THE BUG (real-money code).** `venues/lighter_client.market_close()` returns `None` (no exception) when the venue holds NO position under the key — already flat, externally closed, or liquidated between the `positions()` read and the close call. The Ticket Taker guards this everywhere ("None is a FAILED close … leaving position, retry"); the Funding Farmer guarded it NOWHERE. All three close sites (manage-loop `:1377`, blind-stop fail-safe `:1284`, `_flatten_all` `:971`) booked a close on `None` — fabricating `price_pnl` on the STALE `held` size and incrementing `n_closed`/`n_wins`. Those counters feed the promotion judge (`experiment_judge`, the only writer of `live.funding.*`) and the brain's reduce opinion, so a phantom close silently CORRUPTS the very data any autonomous widen/promote decision rests on — a data-integrity brake that had to close before authority widens.
- **THE FIX — mirror the taker, verified not to leak.** Each site now `if _res is None:` logs and skips the booking. Confirmed safe against a meta-leak: the manage loop is keyed on `set(pos) | set(meta)` and pops meta whenever the venue shows `held==0` (`:1256-1259`), so a vanished position's meta is reconciled on the NEXT loop (or the close is retried if it's still held) — we deliberately DON'T pop meta on `None`, matching the taker's conservative "leave it, reconcile/retry" semantics. All three `market_close` sites covered (the 4th hit is a docstring).
- **Restrict-in-effect — it can only ever record FEWER closes, never more, and never a fabricated P&L.** No entry/exit/stop/sizing logic changed. Existing `lighter_funding_bot --selftest` 4/4, real-money + financial suites green, full suite green, born-dark + sdk-pin + venue-purity + deploy-coverage guards green.
- **Honest test gap:** the close path lives inside `run()`'s loop and isn't extracted into a unit, so a dedicated None-close selftest needs a small `run()`-harness refactor of real-money code — deliberately deferred rather than rushed here; the fix mirrors the taker's already-fixture-tested None semantics. **DEPLOY: real-money file — reaches the SHADOW funding twin on the freqtrade-bots auto-deploy; the LIVE Farmer (`trail-blazer-live`, gate0) needs an operator ff/dispatch. Inert-until-then and restrict-only, so no urgency.** This completes brakes 1-5 of "fix the brakes, then widen".

## 2026-07-23 (cs) — AUTONOMY BRAKE 2: the proposal channel is now author-bound — no unauthenticated hand on a real-money release (operator: "fix the brakes, then widen")

- **THE HOLE.** `fleet_proposals.propose()` had NO author binding (unlike `fleet_tuning.write_levers`, which enforces `AUTHOR_LANES`): ANY organ importing the module could propose ANY registered lever, including `live.funding.*`. And the judge's `proposal_fade` treats a fresh RESTRICT proposal on a promoted live lever as an EARLY RELEASE (`experiment_judge.py:954` reads `fprop.fresh_proposals()`), so a buggy or spurious proposal from any author was an unauthenticated trigger that could pull a good, measured-positive promotion off real money before its fade bar. Exactly the surface you do NOT want open before widening authority.
- **THE BRAKE.** New `PROPOSAL_AUTHOR_LANES` + `_author_may_propose()` — the PROPOSE-side twin of `AUTHOR_LANES`, deliberately DISTINCT from it (proposing is evidence-in, so the shadow proposers are broader than the write authors: event-sentinel WRITES only its detection lane but legitimately PROPOSES `taker.*`). A live-lane proposal now requires an explicitly sanctioned author (`impl-shortfall` → `lighter-live`, the only one); an unlisted author may propose only non-live (shadow) lanes, never real money — the same fail-safe shape as fleet_tuning's unbound-author rule. Enforced at BOTH write (`propose`) AND read (`fresh_proposals`), so a live.* proposal that is stale, pre-brake, or DB-poked is filtered before any consumer sees it.
- **NO LEGITIMATE PROPOSER BROKEN — verified against the real callers.** The four live proposers keep working on their actual levers: brain / event-sentinel / respiration → `taker.*` (shadow), impl-shortfall → `live.funding.*` (live). Confirmed by reading every `propose()` call site and its exact `set_by` string, not assumed. New selftest pins the gate both ways (sanctioned live proposal survives; a rogue `live.funding.enter_apr` is dropped at write and filtered on read).
- Shadow/intelligence-layer channel — no real-money code touched, no live behaviour change; the brake can only ever REFUSE a proposal, never enact one. Full suite green, all guards green. Brake 2 of 5 (remaining: brake 4, Farmer phantom-close — the one real-money-file change, saved for last and its own commit).

## 2026-07-23 (cr) — AUTONOMY BRAKES 3 + 5: stop feeding the autonomous deciders bad data (operator: "fix the brakes, then widen")

- **BRAKE 5 — era-awareness was a fleet-wide NO-OP, so the brain graded every bot on its WHOLE retained ledger.** `ERA_START` is keyed on bare names (`freqtrade-mum`) but the ledger `bot` field carries a venue/shadow suffix (`freqtrade-mum-lshadow`; live rows `-lighter`), so `ERA_START.get(bot)` missed on EVERY trading bot — "today's strategy prosecuted for yesterday's crimes", exactly what the era block exists to prevent. Compounding it, callers did a STRING compare (`str(open_ts) >= era`), and a space-formatted `"... UTC"` stamp sorts below a `"T"` era at char 10. New single-source helper `bot_learn.era_epoch_for(bot)` strips the suffix and returns a PARSED epoch; both the production path (`compute_stake_mults`) and the `brain_replay` harness use it. Measured: `freqtrade-mum-lshadow` now resolves to the 14-Jul era it always should have. Conservative direction (fewer trades → less likely to cross the floor), shadow-only (no live bot reads mults). `bot_learn --selftest` 42/0.
- **BRAKE 3 — the judge's hardcoded live env-defaults could let a "restrict" proposal WIDEN a real-money lever.** `experiment_judge.proposal_fade` rules a release "restrict-only" by reverting to the funding bot's env default — but it keeps a HARDCODED COPY (`LIVE_ENV_DEFAULTS`). If that copy drifts from the live bot's actual default (operator raises `FUNDING_MAX_HOLD_H` → judge still thinks 72 → releases → live reverts WIDER), the restrict-only guarantee is defeated. The judge runs in a different process and can't read the live service's env at runtime, but it CAN pin its copy to the funding bot's SOURCE defaults: a new selftest drift guard reads `lighter_funding_bot.py`'s `FUNDING_*` default literals and fails the build if `LIVE_ENV_DEFAULTS` diverges (proved it bites on a simulated 72→84 drift). The per-service env-override caveat is now documented at `LIVE_ENV_DEFAULTS`. No runtime behaviour change — detective control only.
- **Both shadow/intelligence-layer — no real-money code, no live behaviour change.** Full suite green, born-dark + venue-purity guards green. Brakes 3 + 5 of the "fix the brakes, then widen" plan (1 = brain_replay expand validation, cq). Remaining: brake 2 (proposal author-binding) + brake 4 (Farmer phantom-close).

## 2026-07-23 (cq) — AUTONOMY BRAKE 1: the brain's WIDEN direction is now under test (operator: "the levers need full authorisation... but fix the brakes first")

- **WHY THIS IS FIRST.** The operator wants the levers/gates autonomous — no waiting on a human. The prerequisite is that the system's WIDEN direction is trustworthy, and it wasn't tested at all. `brain_replay.score()` did `if m >= 1.0: continue` — it dropped EVERY expansion, so the harness validated only the REDUCE half, and its VERDICT (16-Jul) predated the two-way expand ship (21-Jul) by 5 days. A widen that amplifies a loser in the second half — the exact "false raise" mirror of the false-throttle the harness exists to catch — passed undetected. You cannot safely hand a widen lever "full authorisation" while its widening is unvalidated.
- **THE FIX — score BOTH directions, prove both.** `score()` now books expand in a PARALLEL metric set (`expanded/gained/eh1/eh2/squandered`); the reduce accounting is byte-identical (the `m<1` branch), so the ledger no-regression and the A-F/G assertions are unchanged. Same sign convention: `s>0` = the mult HELPED (reduce dodged a loss / expand amplified a win), `s<0` = it HURT. Two new isolated scenarios: **H** — a clean n=40 / ~90%-wr bucket that v3 must EXPAND to 1.5x (EXP_HARD bars) while v2 has no expand path; **I** — the SAME shape at n=20 < the full-30 floor that v3 must REFUSE to widen (no soft-n path — the brake that stops the widen direction acting before it has the evidence). Both green; `score()` books H's winners as `gained=+$8.50`, `throttled=0` (never a false reduce).
- **MADE IT A STANDING CONTROL.** Extracted `run_synthetic_suite(t0)` and added an OFFLINE `selftest()` (fixed epoch, no ledger fetch) — the suite needed network before (it lived inside `main()` after the HTTP fetch), so even a correct expand test couldn't have run in CI. `brain_replay` is now registered in `tests/test_selftests.py`, so the widen direction is exercised on every push, not just in a laptop run. VERDICT docstring carries a dated addendum; the ledger LEG still needs a fresh online run to re-attest no-regression with expand armed (synthetic leg A-I green offline).
- **Shadow/validation only — zero real-money behaviour.** `brain_replay` grades; it signs no orders. Suite green (selftest harness incl. the new module + rot guard); no bot logic touched. This is brake 1 of the "fix the brakes, then widen" plan; brakes 2-5 (proposal author-binding, judge env-default drift, Farmer phantom-close, ERA_START keys) follow.

## 2026-07-23 (cp) — the optimisation audit's SAFE fixes: three fail-safe hardenings + the deploy guard made ENFORCED, each provably inert or restrict-only (operator: "make sure all moving parts are working... find solutions")

- **Ticket Taker freshness now future-stamp-safe (real-money-adjacent).** Three inline freshness checks in the LIVE Ticket Taker (`lighter_ticket_taker.py` scout/stress `:1359`, fleet-risk `:1378`, lens-forward `:1433`) tested only `age <= ttl` — a FUTURE-dated payload (clock skew / bad publisher, negative age) read as FRESH and its value was used. Now bounded `0 <= age <= ttl`, matching `fleet_bus.is_fresh` AND this bot's own coin-veto read (`:1458`), which already had the lower bound — the contract was inconsistent *within one function*. Behaviour changes only for anomalous future stamps, making them NEUTRAL like everywhere else (fail-safe). Found by the brain/scout audit pass.
- **Fill telemetry can no longer stall the live loop (both live bots).** `venues/lighter_client.py:783` — the authoritative auth'd fill read (tape 1) used the default 120s BLOCKING budget acquire, while the tape-2 fallback right below it is non-blocking (`gov_timeout=0`). In a 429/405 cooldown a post-close FILL MEASUREMENT could block the single-threaded live loop up to ~120s×3 — no stops evaluated on other held coins, kill/daily-loss checks delayed — in exactly the storm where risk management matters most, violating the module's OWN invariant ("TELEMETRY MUST NEVER STARVE AN ORDER"). Tape 1 now takes a free token or skips instantly to the spare-budget tape-2 peek. Fill precision under contention is the documented, intended trade-off. Order-placement paths (`market_open`/`market_close`) are UNTOUCHED — they still block for budget, correctly. Found by the real-money core audit pass.
- **deploy-coverage is now ENFORCED in the test harness, and its false comment is gone.** `tests/test_selftests.py` classified `audit_deploy_coverage.py` as `GUARD_ONLY` with the comment "it's informational, a census not a pass/fail" — FACTUALLY WRONG: `main()` returns 1 on an orphan, 0 otherwise. That mis-classification is why the `fleet_radar` orphan shipped unblocked. Moved to `ENFORCED_AUDITS` (green on main after the (co) paths fix); its full scan now runs in pytest too, belt-and-suspenders with the CI job (co) added. `audit_lever_authority` stays GUARD_ONLY — it legitimately exits 1 on 5 open triage findings.
- **Two doc-drift corrections (sync-channel honesty).** `fleet_tuning.py:388` "ENACT_LANES contains all five lanes" → SIX (event-sentinel added 21-Jul). `CLAUDE.md` overstated the live hurting-revert: the clip HOOK is wired at the consumer, but `live.clip_scale` can NEVER be graded `hurting` (`fleet_proprioception.grade_live` returns `recorded` for the live-clip group — the per-trade metric is invariant to clip size), so that auto-revert only ever fires for `live.funding.*`; corrected to describe the plumbing, not a revert that can fire.
- **DEPLOY REACH (state it honestly):** the two real-money-file edits (`lighter_ticket_taker.py`, `venues/lighter_client.py`) reach the SHADOW instances on the freqtrade-bots auto-deploy at merge; the LIVE Ticket Taker (`tide-rider-lighter-live`) and both live bots' venues layer are on NO auto-path and need an explicit `gh workflow run 305025607 -f services=...` dispatch. Both edits are fail-safe/inert-until-dispatch, so there is no urgency and no live behaviour change on merge.
- **Validation:** full suite 196 pass / 1 skip (identical to pre-audit baseline), taker `--selftest` green, all four shipping-code guards (born-dark, sdk-pin, venue-purity, deploy-coverage) exit 0, conflict-marker scan clean. NO real-money behaviour changed on merge (shadow-reaching, fail-safe). The audit's operator-authority items (live funding gate, unlevered stops, radar redeploy) and its behaviour-changing intelligence-layer fixes (ERA_START keys, brain_replay expand coverage, proposal author-binding, Farmer None-close guard) are SURFACED for focused follow-up, not bundled here.

## 2026-07-23 (co) — the fleet-optimisation audit: the newest organ (fleet_radar) was DEPLOY-ORPHANED, and the guard that would have caught it was never run by CI (operator: "make sure all moving parts are working as they should")

- **THE BUG — the last three commits' own work could never deploy.** `fleet_radar.py` is COPY'd into `Dockerfile.freqtrade` (:102) and looped in `run_all.sh` (:309, `--publish` every 30 min), but it was in **NEITHER** `railway-redeploy.yml`'s `paths:` block **NOR** the freqtrade-bots service `grep` — so a push editing it matched no path, selected no service, and deployed nothing. Green CI, green tests, old code still running: the exact [[railway-cli-frozen-services]] silent-no-op the two-list doctrine exists to prevent. Added `fleet_radar.py` to BOTH lists (kept in sync, as the comments demand); `audit_deploy_coverage.py` now exits 0.
- **THE ROOT CAUSE — a detective control nobody executed.** `scripts/audit_deploy_coverage.py` already existed, is stdlib-only, and has a `--selftest` negative fixture — but, exactly like `venue-purity` before 18-Jul, it was **never RUN by any CI job** (only referenced in a comment in the file it audits). So it rotted undetected while the orphan shipped. Wired it into `changelog-check.yml` as a `deploy-coverage` job (guard + negative fixture), mirroring born-dark / sdk-pin / venue-purity. Now a shipped-but-undeployable file fails the build.
- **SCOPE — real-money surface swept in the same pass (operator rule, 16-Jul).** Baseline verified clean before and after: 196 pytest pass / 1 skip, the full `--selftest` harness green, and all four shipping-code guards (born-dark, sdk-pin, venue-purity, deploy-coverage) exit 0. Confirmed the recent shadow/dashboard commits (ci–cn) touched **no** real-money source since the (cg) gate0 ff at 32d8ff6, so the live Farmer/Ticket-Taker are not silently missing behavioural fixes.
- **SURFACED, NOT TAKEN (operator-authority, unchanged doctrine):** `audit_lever_authority` reports 5 open findings on the live Funding Farmer — `HARD_STOP` (59.5% of gross loss) and `EXIT_APR` (36.2%) are UNLEVERED, `enter_apr`'s bound cannot reach its modal-decisive value — and (cn)'s radar verdict that the live Farmer edge is decaying + 2-coin. Both are real-money sizing/stop decisions reserved to the operator; this audit records them, does not act on them.
- This entry's only code change is CI/deploy plumbing (`.github/workflows/*`) — no bot logic, no real-money behaviour changed. Shadow/organ fixes from the audit's deeper passes land in follow-up commits.

## 2026-07-23 (fh) [authored (cr); commit 7219510's subject still says so] — the live spread gate is DEPLOYED (dormant, build-stamp verified) + an OPT-IN auto-path for the real-money taker, safe by construction (operator: "create an auto path and deploy this yourself ... built the auto path afterwards once safe")

- **THE (fe) DORMANT SPREAD GATE IS NOW LIVE ON REAL MONEY — verified by BUILD STAMP, not the branch label.** The live Ticket Taker (`tide-rider-lighter-live`) runs build `767448d24c5c` == `build_compute('lighter_ticket_taker.py')` at HEAD (ff) — fresh, `status=online`, `venue=lighter_live`, managing its 2 open positions. The gate ships DISABLED (`TT_SPREAD_GATE_BPS=0`), so this is a zero-behaviour-change deploy: real-money entries are byte-for-byte unchanged until the operator sets the env var. It rode onto the live book via the (ff) operator-approved dispatch (both live services, "go ahead and deploy"); a redundant `tide-rider-lighter-live` dispatch confirmed the same stamp. Enabling it (record-only 9999, then gate ~20) stays a separate operator-only step.
- **AUTO-PATH: OPT-IN, NOT BLANKET — safe by construction (the operator's "once safe").** The two REAL-MONEY services stay OFF the blanket push-auto-deploy: an unmarked WIP/held push must never auto-restart a real-money book — the exact barrier that kept the (ff) rail fix from surprise-deploying before the operator cleared it. New rule in `railway-redeploy.yml`: a push that changes the taker's own code (`lighter_ticket_taker.py`/`venues/`) AND carries the explicit marker `[deploy-live-taker]` in any commit message in the range ALSO deploys `tide-rider-lighter-live`. No marker → only the SHADOW (freqtrade-bots) ships, exactly as before. This gives auto-deploy CONVENIENCE (no manual `gh workflow run`) without removing the barrier. Mutation-tested (5 cases incl. the decisive one: a `(ff)`-style rail-fix commit with NO marker deploys the shadow ONLY — no surprise real-money ship). Restrict-by-construction: no marker, no real-money deploy.
