This commit touches only the workflow + CHANGELOG — it triggers no deploy and cannot self-deploy the live taker (no taker-code change, no marker). NO real-money code or behaviour changed.

## 2026-07-23 (fg) [authored (cq); commit ab97ddf's subject still says so] — the (ff) capital-net rail EXTRACTED to one tested rule, closing the Farmer's test gap (ff's own declared residual) (operator: "enhance, evolve, find solution")

- **WHY.** (ff) fixed both live rails but shipped an HONEST residual: the shift arithmetic was inline at THREE sites (taker loop-top + loop-bottom, farmer loop-top), and the Funding Farmer's `while True` `main()` has NO test harness (only unit selftests), so its copy was compile+logic-verified, NOT fixtured. Three copies of a money rule is one edit from drift — the exact argument `venues.safety.open_notional` was lifted for.
- **WHAT.** `capital_adjusted_day_start(day_start, cap_delta) -> (day_start, shifted)` now lives in `venues/safety.py` beside its sibling rails (`daily_loss_hit`, `confirm_daily_loss`). PURE: shifts the baseline by a folded capital move ONLY when a baseline already exists AND a move folded; returns `(None, False)` on no-baseline so the caller's capital-inclusive `equity` adoption is never double-shifted. All THREE sites call it — no bot carries the arithmetic anymore. No new import (both bots already `from venues.safety import open_notional`) → born-dark clean.
- **THE GAP IS CLOSED.** The rule now has a DIRECT unit test in `venues.safety._selftest` (deposit shifts up, withdrawal shifts down, no-move/None untouched, 2dp rounding on the book's own 66.87/32.22) that the Farmer's rail exercises by calling the same function — so the Farmer is no longer un-fixtured. Mutation-tested BOTH layers: neuter the helper (never shift) and the safety unit test AND the taker's end-to-end `--selftest-live` 4b BOTH go RED ("a deposit masked a real drawdown").
- **BEHAVIOUR-PRESERVING.** Byte-for-byte identical rail behaviour to (ff): the taker's 4b/4c end-to-end fixtures (production code through the helper) stay green, the db52724 deposit-heal regression stays **47/47 green**, full suite + born-dark + sdk-pin + venue-purity + conflict-marker guards green. `venues/**` changed, so every bot's build stamp MOVES (bytes, not behaviour); the shadow arms auto-deploy on the SAME push so no arm drifts.
- **DEPLOY.** `venues/**` on the push auto-deploys freqtrade-bots + funding-carry + market-context (shadow/instrument, behaviour unchanged); the two LIVE services dispatched again (`gh workflow run 305025607 -f services="trail-blazer-live,tide-rider-lighter-live"`) and BUILD-STAMP-verified on each running container; gate0 fast-forwarded so the Farmer's deploy branch stays current.

## 2026-07-23 (ff) — the LIVE daily-loss rails were capital-BLIND: a deposit MASKED a drawdown, a withdrawal FABRICATED a halt — BOTH real-money bots fixed to measure trading P&L NET of capital (operator: "go ahead and deploy")

- **THE DEFECT (live-surface audit, both real-money bots).** The Ticket Taker AND Funding Farmer daily-loss rails compared RAW venue equity to a RAW `day_start` that was never capital-adjusted, while `capital_adjust + CAPITAL_ADJUST_USD` was subtracted for DISPLAY ONLY (`pnl_abs` / `_live_pnl`) — no rail read it. `account_value()` returns the raw venue total, and on an EquityGuard heal/rebase that total INCLUDES a deposit/withdrawal. So on the live $2/day leash vs a deposit-driven ~$67 book: a same-day **deposit MASKS** an equal drawdown (raw equity rises, day_start doesn't → the rail can't fire) and a same-day **withdrawal ≥$2 FABRICATES** a flatten+halt (raw equity falls, day_start doesn't → the rail flattens on the operator's own cash-out). Latent, not theoretical — the book IS deposit-driven (34.648 + 32.22). No breach/halt/kill had ever fired; rails otherwise SOUND.
- **THE FIX — day_start rides the capital move (operator-confirmed NET of deposits/withdrawals).** `_fold_capital_moves()` now RETURNS the net $ folded; the caller shifts `day_start += delta` so a capital move lands in BOTH the equity read and the rail baseline and CANCELS in the rail's `(day_start − equity)` — the leash measures TRADING P&L only. Applied at BOTH taker fold sites (loop-top + loop-bottom persist) and the farmer's loop-top; the shift fires ONLY in the day_start-already-set branch (the None-adoption branch takes `equity`, already capital-inclusive — shifting there would DOUBLE-count). `venues/safety.py` UNTOUCHED (shared by 4 bots). REJECTED the tempting `equity − capital_adjust − CAPITAL_ADJUST_USD` form: that ledger is LIFETIME (vs `live_baseline`) but `day_start` is a DAILY baseline already capital-inclusive — subtracting it would trip the rail every day.
- **TESTS + mutation.** New taker `--selftest-live` **4b (deposit-mask: a +$100 deposit exactly offsets a −$100/−10% loss → the rail MUST still flatten)** and **4c (withdrawal-phantom: −$30 withdrawal, zero trading loss → the rail must NOT fire)**; `_StubVenue` gained `pop_capital_moves` mirroring the guard's pop-once contract. Mutation-verified: neuter the shift (`_fold`→`return 0.0`) and 4b goes RED ("a deposit masked a real drawdown"). The db52724 EquityGuard deposit-heal regression stays **47/47 green** — the fix does not reopen it. The Farmer shares the identical shift but has no main-loop harness (only unit selftests) → compile+logic-verified, not fixtured (a pre-existing coverage gap, stated not hidden). `--selftest`/`--selftest-live` green; full suite green; born-dark + sdk-pin + venue-purity + conflict-marker guards green.
- **THIS IS A REAL-MONEY BEHAVIOUR CHANGE (unlike (fe), which shipped dormant).** The live rails now fire net-of-capital. Restrict-correctness ONLY: it never sizes up, flips `dry_run`, or adds keys — it makes the rail fire when it SHOULD (a deposit-masked drawdown) and NOT when it shouldn't (a phantom on a cash-out). Residual (declared): a capital move landing inside the 60s `confirm_daily_loss` window isn't reflected until the next loop — negligible, fail-OPEN for deposits.
- **DEPLOY.** The SHADOW taker + organs ride the freqtrade-bots auto-deploy on merge. The two LIVE services are on NO auto-path: dispatched via `gh workflow run 305025607 -f services="trail-blazer-live,tide-rider-lighter-live"` (`railway up` from main), then BUILD-STAMP-verified on each running container (never trust the branch label — [[railway-cli-frozen-services]]). gate0 (`claude/lighter-gate0`, the Farmer's deploy branch) fast-forwarded to main so the fix is DURABLE across a Farmer rebuild — it was 8 commits stale (the loaded gun, [[gate0-stale-lacks-tonights-guardrails]]).

## 2026-07-23 (fe) — the Ticket Taker root-cause lands a lever: a proactive QUOTED-SPREAD gate, SHIPPED DISABLED (operator: "do 1 [the dormant, replay-validated spread gate] ... with winning in mind")

- **WHAT SHIFTED THE LIVE TAKER, and the fix it points to.** A 6-agent adversarially-verified root-cause (+ hand re-derivation) found the live Ticket Taker (flat +$0.18/10 closes) never lost its edge: a side-agnostic, liquidity-blind bot passively followed the scout's funding-driven emission flip (short→long ~19-20 Jul, an ENDOGENOUS Lighter funding drift in a CONSTANT risk-off regime — regime-oracle read "risk-off downtrend" every day; NO code/lever change in the window) into a thin, wide-quote universe (equity-perps + fresh micro-caps; ZHIPU ~91bps round-trip). The SIGNAL is intact (brain grades divergence forward-positive at n>11k); realised P&L is eaten by EXECUTION on books it can't trade in.
- **THE RIGHT GATE IS QUOTED SPREAD, NOT VOLUME — measured, then shipped dormant.** `venue_orders.spread_bps` is recorded at decision time (179/179 shadow rows) = a true at-open observable, historically present → BACKTESTABLE on the ledger. Quoted spread PREDICTS realised slippage and it HOLDS EX-BOT (pearson r=+0.44 all / **+0.42 ex-BOT**; per-fill: STRC 98→46, IWM 45→27, NBIS 37→45, **SOXL 24→78**). The gate `spread ≤ ~20bps` is the ONLY gate (spread OR volume) that improves BOTH halves EX-BOT while excluding ONLY losers (ex-BOT keep 46, cum +$14.15 vs +$8.96, **t=+1.30**, dH1 +2.18/dH2 +3.01, excl $0 win/−$5.19 loss). The VOLUME floor DIV_VOL_M was RE-REJECTED at grown n (its "+$13.57 t=1.83 both-halves" was a BOT mark-pathology artifact — BOT 47/99 closes, vol_m 0.12-0.59 but 747bps slip, already vetoed; ex-BOT every volume floor FAILS both-halves; volume≠toxicity: 1000BONK 0.12M/4.8bps/+$5.84 wrongly cut while SOXL kept).
- **`TT_SPREAD_GATE_BPS`, ONE knob, THREE modes, DEFAULT 0 = FULLY DORMANT** (no book fetch, zero new network calls, byte-for-byte prior behaviour). A HIGH value (e.g. 9999) = RECORD-ONLY (fetch + log the decision-time spread on LIVE fills — closing the live-spread telemetry gap, blocking nothing); a moderate value (e.g. 20) = ACTIVE gate. The book is fetched ONLY when enabled and ONLY for a candidate that cleared every cheap filter (≤MAX_OPEN fetches/cycle). Fail-OPEN (a missing/empty book NEVER blocks — same contract as the coin-quality veto); RESTRICT-ONLY (an over-wide spread only ever SKIPs). `book_spread_bps` mirrors the ShadowBroker's fill-time math exactly, so the gate reads the same number the ledger records.
- **HONEST BOUNDS, stated not buried.** SHIPPED DISABLED for three reasons: the edge it protects is real but NOT yet significant (t=1.30 < 2); enabling changes REAL-MONEY entries (operator-only); and the live arm records spread only once enabled — so validate on the live arm's own tape (record-only) BEFORE gating. The tape-replay harness is BLIND to spread ('lighter-market' snapshots carry marks only) — the gate is FILL-LEDGER-validated, not tape-replayed, and that limitation is what the live-spread telemetry exists to close. Not every ex-BOT loss is execution: NBIS (−$6.36, ~10bps slip) is DIRECTIONAL (long-divergence wrong in a downtrend), which no liquidity gate fixes.
- **Evidence + tests.** `scripts/study_taker_spread_gate.py` reproduces the table (spread→slip ex-BOT + the gate sweep, FULL and EX-BOT). Selftest pins both directions + mutation-noted: the DORMANT default (0) blocks nothing even on a pathological quote (deleting the `threshold≤0` short-circuit trips it), an enabled gate fails OPEN on an unknown spread (deleting the `is None` short-circuit trips it) and BLOCKS a wide quote. `--selftest`/`--selftest-live` green; full suite **198 green**; born-dark + venue-purity + conflict-marker guards green.
- **DEPLOY.** The SHADOW taker + organs ride the freqtrade-bots auto-deploy on merge; the LIVE Taker (`tide-rider-lighter-live`, service names lie) is on NO auto-path and the gate is DORMANT regardless — **no real-money code or behaviour changes on this commit.** Enabling on live is a separate, operator-only step (`gh workflow run 305025607 -f services="tide-rider-lighter-live"` to ship, then set the env var). Follow-up: #2 wire spread into the live path for record-only validation (the gate already does this when enabled); #3 enable on live is operator-only.
## 2026-07-23 (cn) — the radar digs deeper and catches its OWN flagship verdict as hollow: the "plausible" Funding Farmer edge is DECAYING and 2-COIN, not durable (operator: "dig deeper... make these more useful than we anticipated")

- **THE FIND (6-angle parallel dig into the live data, then verified by hand).** fleet_radar's ONLY `plausible` book — the Funding Farmer, INCLUDING the LIVE real-money arm — is illusory on two independent axes. DECAY: shadow thirds t = 3.28 / 1.10 / −0.54, recent-half MEDIAN negative (−0.18%), last-15 mean +0.036% vs +0.561% full — the edge lived in the first third and faded to zero. CONCENTRATION: drop just ZEC (n=5) + HYPE (n=6) and the LIVE book goes t 1.10 → **−0.32**, median negative. The proud "plausible · ~23 closes ≈ 4d to verdict" was a fading average of a front-loaded 2-coin streak; feeding it would confirm the fade, not reach a positive verdict.
- **THREE SENSORS BUILT (the dig's ranked winners).** 🧭 TRAJECTORY: per book (n≥24) t_recent_half, median_recent, traj_delta, decay_ratio, slope_t → label decaying/emerging/stable; a DECAYING book's ETA is replaced with "DIVERGING — effect shrinking; no verdict on this trajectory" (never a climbing ETA on a shrinking effect — the constant-effect lie the old ETA told). 🌐 CONCENTRATION: pnl-weighted leave-one/two-COIN-out (loco_t1/loco_t2, coins only — never an exit-inclusive reason, which is tautological); a plausible/real_edge book whose t collapses below 1.0 when its top coin is removed gets a `2-coin` caveat. 👯 TWIN-DEDUP: a live book and its own `-lshadow` control arm (measured +0.795 correlated) are tagged as ONE edge (live authoritative), so the radar never presents them as two independent plausible rows nor lets a consumer pool them into a mirage t.
- **FIVE DEEPENINGS HONESTLY SKIPPED** (the dig killed them with real data): regime-split MANUFACTURES false edges (every regime bucket clearing |t|≥2 was symbol concentration, collinear with regime); execution-adjust DOUBLE-COUNTS (paper pnl_pct is already off real fill prices; Farmer slippage is 1.3% of its edge — zero verdict changes); cross-book correlation too sparse (only the twin pair is robust); cell-rescue a clean null (no noise/losing/starved book hides a significant cell); leading-indicators non-predictive (a hot streak predicts forward returns NEGATIVELY — a fade signal, not a feed signal).
- **TWO REAL BUGS THE TESTS CAUGHT.** (1) The trajectory was INVERTED — `fetch_paper_trades` is closed_at DESC, so the "recent half" was the OLD half; now the book sorts oldest-first, with a reversed-order selftest guarding it. (2) `_t`/`_slope_t` exploded to ~1e16 on identical values (float-noise sd ~1e-17); guarded with sd/se > 1e-9. Both mutation-tested.
- 17-check selftest (all new sensors + both bug guards, mutation-tested); dashboard card + report now show the ↓/↑/→ trajectory arrow and ⚠fading/2-coin/twin caveats. Suite 198 green; born-dark + venue-purity + conflict-marker guards green. fleet_radar rides the freqtrade-bots auto-deploy; pnl_dashboard the dashboard path. Still publish-only, still no actuator — the radar is just far more honest now.

- **THE CARD.** `radar_card()` renders bot_state `fleet-radar` on the main dashboard page (into `brain_html`, beside the brain/evidence/autonomy/parliament/incubator cards): every LIVING book as a colour-coded row — 🟢 real_edge · 🟡 plausible · 🟠 artifact · ⚪ weak · ⚫ noise · 🔴 losing · 🌫️ starved — with n, t-stat, and its ETA-to-verdict in plain words ("~23 more closes ≈ 4.2d at 5.4/day"; "no destination — not converging"; "feed +12 closes to begin a read"). Header carries the class tally + freshness.

- **FAIL-SILENT, as every card is.** No `fleet-radar` state → the card returns "" and simply doesn't appear (it first publishes ~11 min after the freqtrade-bots container boots the (cl) run_all.sh, then every 30 min). Rendered live today against a manually-published row: 19 books, the two Funding Farmer arms the only 🟡 plausible edge (ETA 4d/18d), funding-carry 🟠 artifact, taker/georgia/counterweight ⚫ noise, gillard 🔴 losing, 10 🌫️ starved.
- **DISPLAY-ONLY — still a thermometer.** This surfaces the map; it wires no consumer and no actuator. Suite 198 green; conflict-marker + venue-purity guards green; `pnl_dashboard.py` rides the pnl-dashboard auto-deploy on merge.

## 2026-07-23 (cl) — 📡 the edge RADAR: the 22/23-Jul edge analysis made a STANDING organ, so feed/park/cull is a read not a re-derivation (operator: "this scanning needs radar, lidar, satellite... also tell the cars how long to the destination")

- **WHAT.** `fleet_radar.py` grades every LIVING book by ONE honest ruler, continuously, publishing bot_state `fleet-radar`. No single sensor is trusted — the map triangulates: 📡 RADAR (per-trade t-stat), 🛰️ LIDAR (both-halves stability), 🌐 SATELLITE (top-2-coin concentration + the JACKKNIFE t AND the MEDIAN trade), 📶 STARLINK (closes/day — the odometer), 🧭 ETA (trades + days to a |t|>=2 verdict at the current effect size). Classes: real_edge / plausible / artifact / weak / noise / losing / starved.
- **WHY THE MEDIAN IS LOAD-BEARING — it catches what a lone t-stat waves through.** A t-stat alone flagged `perps-funding-carry` as a "REAL EDGE" (t=2.23) when its whole edge is two lucky non-crypto trades — drop them and it is 1.74, **median trade NEGATIVE**. The radar's rule: a book significant by the MEAN but hollow by the MEDIAN is `artifact`, not edge. Mutation-tested: remove the median guard and the funding-carry-shaped fixture slips through as "weak" (selftest goes red).
- **THE ETA IS HONEST BY CONSTRUCTION.** A plausible book gets a real date (the Farmer shadow: "~23 more closes ≈ 4.1d at 5.6/day"; live: "~69 ≈ 17.9d"). A noise book gets "no destination — not converging" — because its mean is ~0 and no n moves it. A starved book (n<15) gets "feed +N closes to begin a read" and NEVER "arrived" however high its tiny-n t (avo-maria n=3/t=4.5 is starved, not proven). Class-aware so the ETA can't contradict the verdict.
- **LIVING FLEET ONLY.** Exact-matches `cleanup_legacy_bots.LEGACY_BOTS` (never a base-name collision that would drop a living `-lshadow` port); fail-open. Today's living map (19 books): the two Funding Farmer arms are the fleet's only `plausible` edge; funding-carry is `artifact`; the taker + georgia + counterweight are `noise` ("no destination"); gillard is `losing`; 10 books are `starved`. Zero `real_edge` — the honest fleet-wide truth.
- **PUBLISH-ONLY, born-dark-clean, wired STANDING.** Enacts nothing, proposes nothing; `--publish` is explicit so a bare run never writes the bus. COPY'd into Dockerfile.freqtrade + looped in run_all.sh every 30 min (`RADAR_INTERVAL_SEC`); `audit_image_imports` verifies its imports (bot_pnl_store + cleanup_legacy_bots) are in the image. Selftest registered (198 suite green); venue-purity + born-dark + conflict-marker guards green. Rides the freqtrade-bots auto-deploy on merge. Follow-up (not this commit): a dashboard surface for the map + a consumer (the scan is a thermometer; wiring feed/park to it is a review decision).

## 2026-07-22 (ck) — the coin-quality veto's "pooled fleet-wide" was a LIE for the six 1000-markets: the Ticket Taker's 1000BONK and the fleet's kBONK never merged (operator: "start with 1")

- **THE BUG, measured on live data.** `market_context.coin_quality()` did `GROUP BY coin` on `venue_orders`/`paper_trades`, but the **Ticket Taker writes `1000BONK`** while the funding/momo bots write **`kBONK`** (the fleet-canonical form, `venues/symbol_map.from_lighter`). So the SAME coin's evidence was SPLIT — BONK measured **7 rows (taker spelling) vs 12 (fleet spelling)** in the last 14d — and the veto this feeds, whose own receipt says "pooled fleet-wide", saw neither's full picture. Real, on the live Taker's entry actuator, for all six `1000*` markets.
- **THE FIX — normalise on read, single source of truth.** Extracted a PURE `_fold_coin_quality(venue_rows, paper_rows)` that canonicalises every coin through `from_lighter` (no second copy of the mapping in SQL) and re-aggregates from the raw SUMS, so the weighted slippage/spread AVERAGE is correct across merged spellings (not an average-of-averages). The Taker's veto lookup (`lighter_ticket_taker.py`) now matches the canonical form too — and checks BOTH the canonical and raw base, so it is robust to DEPLOY ORDER (market_context auto-deploys; the LIVE Taker is on no auto-path, so for a window the payload is canonical while the live code is old — matching either form vetoes correctly throughout). The Funding Farmer needed NO change: it already writes/looks up the fleet form.
- **MEASURED INERT ON TODAY'S DECISIONS — this is forward-looking correctness, not a live behaviour change.** Split vs pooled for BONK: 5.06bps (taker) / 9.32bps (fleet) → **7.75bps pooled, all well under the 15bps veto bar**, so nothing is vetoed either way today. The fix corrects the EVIDENCE BASE (19 pooled measured rows vs 7) so that WHEN a 1000-market does turn slippery, the veto sees the fleet's whole picture. Restrict-only throughout, so any change can only ever SKIP an entry, never force one.
- **THE TEST THE VETO NEVER HAD.** `market_context`'s selftest did not touch the coin-quality path (the audit's gap). Added a fold case that pins BOTH the pooling (`1000BONK`+`kBONK` → one `kBONK` with summed orders/measured/closes) AND the weighting (147/19 = 7.74bps, not the 7.17 an average-of-averages gives). **Mutation-tested**: removing the normalise trips the pooling assert; average-of-averages trips the slip assert. Taker selftest still green (covers the six-market symbol round-trip); full suite 197 green; guards green.
- **DEPLOY:** `market_context.py` rides the market-context auto-deploy; the SHADOW taker rides freqtrade-bots; the **LIVE Taker (`tide-rider-lighter-live`) is on no auto-path** — its lookup fix lands on the next dispatch, and until then the both-forms guard + fail-open keeps it correct and inert. No urgency (inert today). No real-money behaviour changes on this commit.

## 2026-07-22 (cj) — the immune organ was watching a DEAD sensor (retired Gap Scout) and had no eyes on the oracle whose grades feed the 28-Jul review: swapped, with both ends MEASURED live

- **REMOVED a provably-dead invariant.** `fleet_immune.organ_invariants` checked `gapscout-census` (`episodes_open < 0`), but Gap Scout retired 17-Jul so that key is never republished — MEASURED **stale 5.5 days** (age 7996 min vs ttl 3600) on the live DB right now. The check only fires `if _fresh(...)`, so it was permanently dead code. A dead sensor left in the body is exactly the rot this organ exists to catch (cf. [[a-dead-sensor-must-not-score-a-hit]], [[a-convergent-metric-is-not-a-health-check]]). Also pruned `gapscout-census` from `fleet_regen.REPAIRABLE` — a retired organ has no publisher to regenerate a snapshot from.
- **ADDED a regime-oracle invariant in its place, and PROVED it is not born-dark.** The oracle self-grades its own directional calls (`grades[sym][d{h}] = {n, hit, avg_pp}`) and those grades are the evidence the 28-Jul item-18 decision reads; a fresh-but-WRONG grade would corrupt it silently. The new check flags impossible values only — a hit-RATE outside [0,1], a negative `n`, negative coverage counts, `n_published > universe` — the same shape as the existing `brain-lens-forward` check. **Verified the source is LIVE before shipping the consumer** ([[born-dark-guard]]): `regime-oracle` on the live DB is **FRESH** (6 min, ttl 7200) with the exact fields checked (universe=16, n_published=16, 13 graded syms). The removed key was stale; the added key is fresh — the swap moves the organ's attention from a dead publisher to a live one.
- **Page-only, never quarantine — confirmed by reading the flow.** `organ_invariants` findings feed `notify_ledger` (phone) and the `sick` payload; lever QUARANTINE is a SEPARATE path (`q = lever_sickness(...)`). So a bad invariant here can only over-ALERT, never wrongly restrict real money. The oracle is healthy today, so no new page is expected; this is a tripwire for the day it isn't.
- **Mutation-tested BOTH directions.** Disabling the hit-range check drops the finding (test fails); making it fire on everything trips the fixture's VALID SPY grade (test fails). So the invariant catches real impossibles AND stays silent on healthy data — the selftest is not vacuous. Full suite 197 green; born-dark + conflict-marker + venue-purity + sdk-pin guards green. `fleet_immune`/`fleet_regen` ride the freqtrade-bots auto-deploy (shadow intelligence layer, no real-money code).

## 2026-07-22 (cg) — the day's own audit, ACTED ON: the sync channel was corrupt and CI could not see it, the withdrawn verdict was still steering real money, and the quarantine (cb) made durable could still be wiped by a DB blip

- **THE SYNC CHANNEL WAS CORRUPT AND EVERY CI RUN SAID GREEN.** `CHANGELOG.md` carried three COMMITTED stash-conflict markers (`:1` `<<<<<<< Updated upstream`, `:72` `>>>>>>> Stashed changes`, `:118` `=======`) from **31cf1ca (18:26 Sydney)** through HEAD — **seven commits**, every one reported `completed/success` by `changelog-check.yml`. The reason is structural: that job asserts only that CHANGELOG.md **APPEARS in the changed-file list**; it never reads a byte of content. **A guard that checks EXISTENCE, not CONTENT, is not a guard.** Resolved by deleting `:1`/`:72` and **REPLACING `:118` with a blank line** — deleting it outright would have re-committed today's own (bq) glue bug (bullet immediately followed by a `## ` header renders inside the previous entry). All 158 entry headers preserved, verified before/after.
- **NEW CI JOB `conflict-markers`** — scans **every tracked file**, not just the changelog (an unresolved marker in a `.py` is a SyntaxError that would ship to a real-money image), plus a negative fixture proving the guard can still see. Both directions tested locally: plants → exit 1, clean tree → pass.
- **THE WITHDRAWN VERDICT WAS STILL IN THE LIVE BOT'S OWN SOURCE.** (cf) withdrew the `+$21.32 / both halves positive / n=1911` STOP verdict and edited two files. It left the number asserted as live truth in **six more places** — the worst being **`lighter_funding_bot.py:136-146`, the LIVE real-money Farmer's own header, COPY'd into the funding image and edited by that very commit**. Swept: the live bot header (rewritten with the withdrawal, the mechanism, and the corrected −$11.57/both-halves-negative), `study_farmer_stop_isolated.py` (banner + the **runtime `print`** that emitted the stale reference rows on every run), `backtest_funding_persistence.py`, `backtest_xsect_funding_lighter.py`, `AUDIT_2026-07-17.md`, and the two CHANGELOG citations that still read as current. Also corrected `HARD_STOP`'s inline comment, which still said "Lighter disagrees" — Lighter does not; the inversion was the artifact.
- **LESSON: a verdict withdrawal is a repo-wide SWEEP, not an edit to the file that produced it** — and you must then ask **what that number SET**. Here it set `FUNDING_HARD_STOP=0.03` on `funding-farmer-shadow`, the experiment judge's CONTROL ARM, so the fleet's only path to `live.funding.*` is still a ≥2-variable A/B. **Surfaced, NOT taken — operator decision, unchanged from (cf).**
- **(cb)'s DURABLE QUARANTINE COULD STILL BE WIPED BY A POSTGRES BLIP.** (cb) made `cooldown`/`stop_hist` durable but restored them through the **UNCHECKED `load_state()`**, which collapses "no row" and "read failed" into the same `None` ([[load-state-seeds-durable-state-on-a-failed-read]] — the trap that already cost a 7d live promotion). A blip at boot therefore looked **exactly like a first run** and silently re-armed every stopped coin — reintroducing, one layer down, the precise failure (cb) was written to close. Both restore sites (live `:live` and the dry_run arm) now go through a new module-level `read_quarantine()` on `load_state_checked`, with bounded retry; a genuinely failed read sets `quarantine_blind` and the **entry gate refuses NEW entries** until a later cycle reads it clean. **Restrict-only by construction** — exits, stops, funding accrual and position management are untouched — and it self-heals, since the flag is retried every cycle.

So `PERSIST_H=4` bit **once per hot run** in the backtest and **every time** in production: **32-44% of the trades in every study built on this file were instant re-entries the real bot refuses.** Fixed with `hot.pop(sym, None)` at the close site.

- **THE LESSON, and it is bigger than this bot: a harness that does not mirror the production rule does not give a WEAKER answer — it gives a WRONG one, with the same confident sign.** BOTH of this file's headline verdicts came out of it: first "NO GATE PASSES / the strategy is dead" (an artifact of an unmeasured 5bps), then "+$21.32, the stop is the bug" (an artifact of this). Both branches now persist and restore the same keys, pruned on write and defensively typed on read. ## 2026-07-22 (ce) — the two Farmer "farmhands" are DEAD at the premise, and LIT was never the villain: a VERDICT record so neither is re-tested

No code ships from this. It is filed because the repo's rule is that a rejected
experiment gets a findable verdict, and both of these are the kind of idea that
looks obviously worth trying until someone measures the premise.

- **FARMHAND A — a per-book P&L memory ("this book keeps costing me money, stop picking it"): DEAD, and dead at the premise, not at the parameters.** Across the 438d tape's halves, per-book **P&L persistence is Spearman −0.016** — out-of-sample sign agreement **8/18 = 44%**, worse than a coin flip. Over the SAME books in the SAME split, realised volatility persists at **+0.830** and mean |funding APR| at **+0.725**, both 13/13 on sign. **Books have strongly persistent CHARACTER; their P&L is a near-pure-noise read of it.** A memory whose sensor has zero autocorrelation over a state with 0.83 cannot work at any (N, W, cooldown) — the 450 swept ledger cells and 144 tape cells are the CONSEQUENCE of that, not the evidence. **Do not re-sweep it.**
  - The stop-count form is **ALREADY SHIPPED** (`lighter_funding_bot.py:163-168`, `:1333-1347`, durable since (cb)): `STOP_COOLDOWN_H=12` escalating to `REPEAT_STOP_COOLDOWN_H=72`. Replayed over the entire live record it has blocked **0** trades (shadow: 1).

Worse at every friction. Standing rule says ship the hardcode if it matches the rule; here the hardcode is negative, so **what ships is nothing**.
- **LIT WAS NEVER THE VILLAIN — correcting a framing this session repeated several times.** "55.4% of the live book's gross loss" is a GROSS-LOSS statistic on a book that is also a top gross WINNER, and whose entire live deficit is two stop-outs: tape **−$9.72** (n=102), live **−$1.023** (n=7; gross loss $4.334 against gross win $3.311), shadow **+$2.410** (n=11, its **2nd-best** book). The fleet's own brain grades it `pair:LIT:earner`.
- **FARMHAND B — gate the Farmer's shorts on each book's OWN regime: DEAD as an actuator, both directions.** Loses at every friction (0/0.86/5/10bps: base 95.38 / 85.86 / 39.98 / −15.42 vs gated 54.20 / 44.51 / −2.17 / −58.55), and so does the mirror once the search is priced. **A study headline of "p=0.003" does not survive multiplicity**: adding the two sibling rules the same script defines → p=0.196; the **12 arms actually run** → **p=0.950**, and under max-T the real best arm (+$51.40) sits BELOW the null-max mean (+$68.67). Permuted verdicts beat the real ones 95% of the time given the same best-of-12 freedom. Struck.
  - It is also undeployable today even if it worked: **6 of 10** Farmer books are graded and exactly **one (XAU) is SHORT-window**; ungraded books carry **76.0%** of live gross loss. `regime-oracle` is absent from `fleet_immune.organ_invariants`, so a fail-closed gate on a silently halted oracle is indistinguishable from a quiet market.
- **DISQUALIFYING ON ITS OWN — what a veto costs the instrument.** Verified tonight: **99 real-money orders, 0 with a measured fill**; the Farmer is **81 of 99 = 81.8%** of all real-money order flow. A fail-closed regime gate cuts live entries **41 → 9** (78%), book coverage **10 → 3**, and time-to-100-measured-fills **12.9d → 58.7d**. Both fail directions turn a profitable real-money book negative (**+$5.514 → −$1.082** fail-closed, **−$1.558** fail-open). Starving the only instrument that can measure Lighter's friction, to fix a book that is up, is the wrong trade.
- **THE STANDING CONCLUSION: the Farmer is EDGE-blocked, not COST-blocked.** At **ZERO friction** its per-trade t is **+0.60**, and nothing across every arm of three studies exceeds **+1.38**. A veto can only remove trades; it cannot manufacture an edge absent from the entry distribution — and the book sits at MAX_OPEN 74.8% of hours, so every veto is a real opportunity cost. It also beats a risk-matched passive short ONLY at 0.86bps, a ShadowBroker model with **zero observations**; every ranking in the study flips between 0.86 and 5bps. **Which is the whole argument for (cc): measure the fills.**

(2) "LIT becomes gradeable 2026-07-24" is FALSE — LIT is in neither `regime_oracle.UNIVERSE` nor `NONCRYPTO`, so it is never fetched and its bar count is irrelevant (verified: LIT/SNDK/GRAM appear in `pairs`, `errors` and `coverage` not once).

## 2026-07-22 (cc) — the fill read fires BEFORE the trade is on the tape: the venue told us how long to wait and the repo had ZERO references to it

- **THE BLOCKER UNDER EVERY IDENTITY TIER, and it was never identity.** 0 of 81 live orders have EVER produced a measured fill. Three sessions rebuilt the naming — client_order_index, then (br)'s tx_hash — and the symptom never moved: both tiers report `no-match:both`, i.e. **both tapes WERE read and our trade was not on them**. No naming scheme fixes a tape that does not contain the trade yet. There is no wait anywhere on either live bot's order path: `read_fill` fires the instant the order is submitted and retries up to SIX tape reads back-to-back, all inside the window the venue itself says the transaction needs.
- **The venue hands us that window on every order and nobody read it.** `RespSendTx.predicted_execution_time_ms` is returned to the caller and stashed at `venues/lighter_client.py:628`/`:826`; `grep -rn predicted_execution` over the whole repo returned **0 hits**.
- **The fix, additive end to end:** `venues/fills.py` gains `read_fill(..., settle_ms=None)` — a post-submission wait driven by the venue's own estimate, clamped by `SETTLE_CAP_MS` (env `FILL_SETTLE_CAP_MS`, default 2500ms) so a zero/absent/garbage value can never stall a trading loop. It delays the MEASUREMENT, never the order (the order is already sent), and `settle_ms=None` reproduces the pre-22-Jul behaviour exactly, so every existing caller and fixture is untouched. `venues/lighter_client.py` gains `_settle_ms_of(resp)` — probed across response shapes exactly like `_tx_hash_of`, because a None here costs only the wait, never an error — threaded into BOTH order return paths. Both live bots pass it through: 8 sites in `lighter_funding_bot.py` (2 signatures, 2 read_fill calls, 4 call sites), 4 in `lighter_ticket_taker.py`.
- **MEASUREMENT ONLY, per this file's own rule:** nothing here may block, delay or unwind an order. The wait is after submission, bounded, and wrapped so a bad value degrades to no wait.
- **Two real breaks caught while writing it, both worth recording.** (1) `ast.parse` reported my first edit as valid while `py_compile` correctly rejected it — `from __future__ import annotations` had ended up after the new imports. **`ast.parse` is not a compile check.** (2) `settle_ms` was referenced in the body one edit before it existed in the signature. Both caught by compiling rather than trusting.
- Validation: 194/194 pytest (the single deselected case is `test_no_unregistered_selftest`, which trips on the still-unreviewed `fleet_agronomy.py` / `scripts/audit_lever_authority.py` written by a parallel workflow — NOT this change and NOT committed here), funding-bot + taker selftests green, born-dark + venue-purity guards green.
- **What it unblocks:** Lighter's real per-book slippage is the constant on which the Farmer's STOP verdict (recorded +$21.32 at 0.86bps vs −$18.24 at 5bps — ⛔ **the +$21.32 half was WITHDRAWN hours later by (cf) as a harness artifact; corrected −$11.57, both halves negative** — the friction point below still stands), Georgia's UNDECIDABLE short-sleeve verdict, `implementation_shortfall`'s live-vs-shadow attribution, and every future go-live all turn. The Farmer is 82% of the fleet's real-money order flow, so it is the instrument that measures it. **Deploy:** `venues/**` rides the freqtrade-bots path; the two LIVE services (`trail-blazer-live`, `tide-rider-lighter-live`) need an explicit dispatch — and the receipt to watch for is a live order whose `raw->>'measured'` finally reads `true`.

## 2026-07-22 (cb) — the post-stop quarantine survives a redeploy (a MEASURED real-money guard failure), + the coin-veto's three review defects and its replay parity [letter note: authored (bu)/(bv); renumbered to (ca)/(cb) after a pull — three parallel sessions took bu-bz while this one was measuring]

- **THE REAL-MONEY BUG.** `cooldown` and `stop_hist` in `lighter_funding_bot.py` were plain in-memory dicts (`:664-665`); the durable writes at `:1441`/`:1444` carried broker/meta/fund_realized/capital_adjust/day_start and **not** the post-stop quarantine. **Measured on the LIVE book:** LIT stopped out 2026-07-21 11:23:12Z and RE-OPENED 16:03:29Z — **4h40m against a 12h `FUNDING_STOP_COOLDOWN_H`**, and it was LIT's SECOND stop inside `REPEAT_STOP_WINDOW_D`, so the 72h escalation (`:167`) should have applied. The shadow twin, same code, honoured 12.00h exactly on both of its stops. The difference is that the LIVE service restarts constantly — **11 deployments on 22-Jul alone** (08:09→16:33 Sydney), and `railway variables` on `trail-blazer-live` (which is the live Farmer — service names lie) shows **no cooldown override**, ruling out a config cause. Every restart silently re-armed a coin the bot had just stopped out of. Same class as the flatten/halt redeploy incident.
- **Why it matters here specifically:** LIT is **55.4%** of the live book's gross loss ($4.33 of $7.83), its two hard stops are 51.1% of the entire book's gross loss, its slippage is a clean 1.60bps (so no slippage veto will ever touch it), and it is the bot's own top `hottest_apr` at +97% — the entry gate re-selects it every cycle. The guard was the only thing standing between the book and a loop.
- **The fix:** `cooldown` + `stop_hist` are persisted alongside `meta` and restored on boot, PRUNED on write (expired cooldowns and stops outside `REPEAT_STOP_WINDOW_D` are dropped, so the payload cannot grow without bound) and defensively typed on read. Verified by round-trip: the LIT cooldown survives a restart with 12.00h remaining, **the actual 21-Jul breach is blocked under both the 12h and 72h rules**, expired/stale entries prune, and all 7 malformed payload shapes (`None`, `"junk"`, non-numeric, non-list, `{"X": None}`, empty) degrade to empty without raising.
- **The coin-veto (bu) shipped with three defects, all found by adversarial review and all fixed here.** **P1 — the log lied to the operator:** it printed `COIN VETO — measured slippage ['ADA','BOT','SOXL']`, but `coin-vetoes` has TWO bars (`market_context.py:396-399`: slip > 15bps/>=5 orders/14d **OR** stop-rate >= 50%/>=5 closes/30d, pooled FLEET-WIDE) and ADA is a *stop-rate* veto whose own slip is 3.65bps. It now prints each coin's own reason, and the module header states both bars. This repo's own self-describing-label failure, in the actuator's own receipt. **P2 —** the `if coin_vetoed:` print sat OUTSIDE the try (indent 4) so `sorted()` ran on an unvalidated field, and the `except` tuple was narrower than the Farmer's actual bare `except Exception`; on the run-once LIVE arm an escape would have aborted the cycle before the durable write, losing `day_start` and re-baselining the daily-loss rail. Print moved inside, `isinstance` added, except widened to match. **P3 —** replay parity, below.
- **REPLAY PARITY — the same class re-committed 5 days after it was fixed.** `lighter_ticket_replay.replay()` models `sl_until` (its own `[2026-07-21 PARITY]` note) but knew nothing of the coin veto, so every replay-gated actuator — scout-tuner walks, `fleet_proprioception`'s $ counterfactual (the ONLY lane with one), incubator offspring scores — would have graded candidates on rules production no longer runs. `replay(..., coin_veto=...)` now mirrors it, **passed in, never read from live state** (applying today's veto set to historical tape is an anachronism). **Measured on the real 72h tape (2058 snapshots): divergence FLIPS +$3.41 → −$4.11** with the veto applied — a $7.52 swing on the one lens that trades real money (n=4 closed, so the magnitude is noisy; the sign flip is the point).
- **The selftest fixture was itself wrong first, and mutation-testing caught it.** The first draft asserted `breakout taken == 0` when the tape carries TWO breakout tickets (AAA, DDD) — it failed IDENTICALLY with and without the guard, the fingerprint of an assertion that never depended on the code under test. Corrected to `base-1` plus a total-veto case. A second mutation then showed the SYMBOL-side normalisation was untested (the veto SET is normalised at construction, so bare-symbol fixtures can never reach it) — a pair-form tape (`FFF/USDC` vs veto `{FFF}`) now covers it. **All three mutants — guard removed, symbol normalisation broken, set normalisation relied upon — are caught; the suite passes only with the real code in place.**
- Validation: 195/195 pytest green; taker/replay/Farmer selftests green; born-dark, venue-purity and sdk-pin guards green. Deploy: `lighter_ticket_replay.py` + `lighter_ticket_taker.py` ride the freqtrade-bots path; **the two LIVE services (`trail-blazer-live` = Farmer, `tide-rider-lighter-live` = taker) need an explicit `railway up` from a clean worktree and are NOT shipped by this commit.**

## 2026-07-22 (ca) — 🎫 the taker joins the coin-quality veto: the fleet's one automated actuator had ONE consumer, and the other book was bleeding 747bps into a lens the brain still grades POSITIVE

- **The third instance of "a kill switch must reach the CONSUMER."** `market_context` publishes `coin-vetoes` (measured slippage > 15bps over >=5 orders/14d) and it was the fleet's single automated restrict-only actuator — but its ONLY reader was `lighter_funding_bot.py:1033`. The Ticket Taker, which has a LIVE real-money arm, never read it. Same shape as FLEET_RISK_MODE and ENACT_LANES before it: the write side was built, the consumer never wired.
- **What it cost, measured:** BOT/USDC carries **747.6 bps mean |slippage| over 93 taker orders** (81.9bps spread) — the next-worst book it trades is SOXL at 20.9, i.e. ~36x. On 22-Jul the taker put **44 of its 52 closes** through BOT and the `short-divergence` realised mean fell to **-0.695%/trade**, dragging the all-time lens to -0.310% over n=70 with both halves negative. **The lens did not decay.** The brain's counterfactual grade of the divergence SIGNAL is unchanged and positive — `ehit4h 0.536 [0.516,0.557]` at n=10522 — because it grades tickets, not slipped fills. One un-vetoed illiquid book was eating a working lens, and the realised-vs-graded gap was the only visible symptom.
- **The fix:** `lighter_ticket_taker.py` gains `QUALITY_VETO` / `TT_QUALITY_VETO` (default on) + `TT_VETO_TTL_S`, loading `coin-vetoes` once per cycle beside the existing brain LENS veto and skipping NEW entries on vetoed books. Contract transcribed VERBATIM from the Farmer's so the two cannot drift: RESTRICT-ONLY (exits, held positions and stops untouched), fail-OPEN on a missing OR STALE payload (a fossil veto set is not evidence; a market-context that died empty must not silently disable the veto forever), never raises into the loop. Symbol form is normalised (`BOT` vs `BOT/USDC`) because matching one form only would have made the guard silently inert — precisely the failure class it exists to fix.
- **Verified against LIVE data, not just the selftest** (the taker's `--selftest` skips much of the entry path — mutation-test the suite that RUNS the code): payload fresh at 109s/3600s ttl, veto set {ADA, BOT, SOXL}, both symbol forms blocked, non-vetoed and null symbols allowed, stale payload vetoes nothing, and against the scout's CURRENT 20 tickets it blocks exactly one — `divergence BOT`. Full taker selftest green; born-dark + venue-purity guards green.
- **[CORRECTED, same day, by an 8-agent adversarial review]** the close-leg hypothesis below is **REFUTED**: `venues/fills.py:107` emits the `tx-miss:` token ONLY inside `if tx_hash:`, so a truthy hash DID reach both tapes; `market_close` returns the hash by identical construction to `market_open` (`lighter_client.py:828` vs `:635`); and "both were close legs" is SELECTION — there have been ZERO live open legs since the fix deployed. Historically closes match *better* (4/22 vs 1/17). **The real blocker is upstream of every identity tier:** the fill read is issued with ZERO wait, and `RespSendTx.predicted_execution_time_ms` — returned to the caller and stashed at `lighter_client.py:628`/`:826` — has **0 references anywhere in the repo**. No naming scheme fixes a tape that does not yet contain the fill.
- **Also corrected:** "RESTRICT-ONLY" above is really **restrict-with-substitution** — the veto `continue` precedes the one-per-lens slot check, so a vetoed top ticket hands its slot to the next same-lens ticket rather than forfeiting it. That mirrors the Farmer and the existing `sym in pos` skips, but it means vetoing BOT lets the live arm take LIT or VIRTUAL instead of nothing. Stated, not changed.
- **Standing observation for the next session (the (br) tx-hash fill fix):** it IS deployed — the tx tier first appears on 22-Jul orders — but its first two live orders both still MISS (`tx-miss: no-match:both(no-match:trades)`), and measured fills remain **0 across 5 days / 45 live orders**. n=2 is far too small to call the fix wrong; note that both were CLOSE legs, so "is the hash threaded on the close path as it is on open?" is the cheap next check.

## 2026-07-22 (bz) — MomoBreakout's missing BTC-TIDE parity gate RESTORED (operator-approved on the falling-half evidence): the lone entry-gate parity gap across the family, fixed — plus the entry-side budget evidence filed for review

- **The parity bug, confirmed in code:** the freqtrade original `MomoBreakoutV1` gained a BTC-tide entry gate on 14-Jul — `close>dc_high & close>ema_trend & (BTC.close > EMA(BTC,200)) & vol`, fail-safe closed — added after both carriers went 2W/9L 3-13 Jul as 11 pairs broke above their OWN 200-EMA and died in a market-wide fakeout. The Lighter port `MomoBreakout.signals` never received it (entry had no tide term). A full parity audit of the other three family strategies (`scripts/study_breakout_tide_gate.py` header) found NO other entry-gate gap of this class: TrendMomo's 14-Jul edit was sizing/veto only; SwingDip's trend gate is per-pair by design; DayTraderGated's BTC 50/200 regime switch is already ported through `extra["btc_regime_up"]`. MomoBreakout was the lone gap.
- **The fix (dad + breakout-4h):** a new `btc_tide_up(cache)` (BTC 4h close>EMA200 — ported EXACTLY, deliberately NOT `btc_regime_up`'s EMA50>EMA200, which the port already has for a different consumer) + a one-line gate in `MomoBreakout.signals`. **Present-key-gates / absent-key-opens form**: production `main()` always supplies the computed `btc_tide_up` key so it is fail-safe CLOSED (a missing/short BTC series → off-tide → block), while `stake_mult`'s `signals(bars, None)` and the two accepted study harnesses (which call `signals(win, {})`) leave it ungated — so NO baseline regresses (verified: study_breakout_boot_entry + study_family_time_stop selftests still green, baselines reproduce to the cent). Only MomoBreakout reads the key; the other five books ignore it. Kill switch `MOMO_TIDE_GATE=off` omits the key → ungated port. Shadow books only, like the port.
- **Evidence (Lighter 500d 4h tape, 25 coins, halves 2025-11-14; BTC H1 +15.2% rising / H2 −32.9% falling):** dad net +11.90→+27.32 (H2 −60.18→−32.64, ~46% of the bleed cut), breakout-4h +16.50→+66.84 (H2 −93.03→−27.15, ~71% cut); win% 35→37-38%; full window up in both. **The honest caveat, on the record:** the STRICT not-worse-both-halves bar is NOT met — H1 gives back $12.11 (dad) / $15.54 (breakout), 83-86% retained and still strongly positive, because the gate also blocks the minority of H1 breakouts that fired during BTC's brief sub-EMA200 dips. My adversarial refuter refuted on exactly this (benefit concentrated in one falling regime; flat-choppy regime untested on Lighter's one-falling-regime tape). Enacted on the DIRECTIONAL-restrict reading (cut the falling-half bleed without gutting the rising half) with **explicit operator sign-off** — it restores a gate the validated original already has, not a novel speculative filter, and the books are shadow + reversible. Item-18 does not block it (BTC IS the right tide for a BTC-beta crypto basket; no non-crypto books here).
- **📈 Entry-side budget allocation — EVIDENCE ONLY, filed for review (`scripts/study_budget_allocation.py`):** the lever the (by) time-stop study named. The 20-slot budget is **2.4× over-subscribed** — nine books' caps sum to 48. At the (bw) saturation snapshot the three parker books (breakout/mum/dad) held 14 of 20 slots for a COMBINED realized 0 wins / 10 closes / −$7.71, while georgia (top earner, +$0.56/slot-day, cycles ~24×/wk) was vetoed. The one cut that pays for itself in-sample is `breakout 6→5` (+$10.46, frees 160 structural slot-days — unlike the time stop, a lower cap cannot refill); `dad 4→3` and `breakout 6→4` trade H1 upside for H2 relief (regime bets); `mum 4→3` is worse in BOTH halves (its trend-follower 4th slot holds winners). Re-sizing what the 20-slot budget MEANS is the operator's review decision — enact_recommended=false, no diff; the operator declined the breakout trim when offered alongside the tide gate. On the FLEET_REVIEW agenda with the arithmetic.
- Selftests: family +4 (tide up→admit, down→block, absent-key ungated, None-safe). Both studies registered in SELFTEST_EXCLUDE. Guards green (born-dark: `btc_tide_up` is a local fn on existing `ema_series`, no new import; venue-purity; sdk-pin); full suite green. Deploy: `lighter_family_bot.py` rides the family-lighter-shadow service (manual dispatch at merge — NOT on the freqtrade-bots auto-path); verified by the running container's build stamp, not "it shipped".

## 2026-07-22 (by) — LEVER FLAP fixed fleet-wide (operator: "implement the flap to all those who need it"): the bars priced at entry govern the trade — plus the time-stop study returns DO NOT ENACT, closing the budget-saturation question

- **The defect ((bw) taker study, measured):** 9/22 taker SL closes were "already past the close-time bar" — a WIDER lever (sl −0.04) EXPIRED mid-position and the snapped-back default (−0.03) booked the whole gap instantly, up to 58 minutes of adverse move in one tick. The same mechanism sat armed on REAL MONEY: the funding bot re-reads `take_profit`/`max_hold_h` levers every loop on BOTH arms, so a judge promotion starting or fading mid-hold snaps bars onto in-flight live positions.
- **The rule now, uniformly: THE BARS PRICED AT ENTRY GOVERN THE TRADE.** Entries stamp the bars in force into position meta; exits check the stamp. A lever expiry/fade changes only NEW positions. Entry GATES stay live-read (a crouch still bites new entries immediately); the taker's post-close `SL_COOLDOWN_H` deliberately stays close-time (close-side policy, not a bar the position was priced at); the funding bot's `HARD_STOP`/`EXIT_APR`/flip are env-only, never levers, untouched. Fail-safe: unstamped/legacy/junk positions behave exactly as before; stamps are written even with the switch off (attribution is free); `LEVER_GRANDFATHER=off` reverts behavior everywhere.
- **Who needed it — the full census, so nobody re-audits:** 🎫 taker (`exit_reason` gains a per-position bars arg, `entry_bars`/`pos_bars` helpers; live arm runs env bars = ships inert there) — FIXED. 💸 funding bot both arms (`pos_bars`, entry stamp in meta, `_record_close` threads it) — FIXED. 🏛️ Parliament — ALREADY IMMUNE by construction (open_meta stores absolute tp/sl/deadline at open; verified, no change). Family/dislocation/sniper/scout — no lever-driven exit bars, nothing to fix.
- **Attribution got honest with it:** close rows now stamp the ENTRY-time bars with `bars_basis: entry` (legacy positions labelled `close-legacy`) — the exact contamination the (bw) study had to reconstruct around (11/22 SL closes ran a different bar than the stamp implied). The judge's `ran_candidate` receipt becomes strictly truthful: a row stamped with candidate bars now provably RAN them to its exit — and a trade opened BEFORE a candidate no longer false-stamps its bars. Runtime now matches every instrument that already assumed per-trade constant bars (replay sweep, judge pairing, proprioception counterfactuals) — the replay's 5-arg `exit_reason` call is untouched by default.
- **🚫 Time-stop study (the (bx) second lane) — DO NOT ENACT, the budget-saturation question closes:** `scripts/study_family_time_stop.py` (harness cross-validates against the accepted breakout study to the cent; 4h 500d + 1d 499d Lighter tape, both regimes). Max slot-day relief ANYWHERE on the grid is −6.1% (bar: ≥20%); at the only a-priori-valid rungs (≥ winners' P90) it is −0.2..−0.9%. Mechanism: level-condition entries refill a freed slot within ~2 bars — a time stop changes WHICH positions hold the slots, never HOW MANY — and 97-100% of stopped positions exit via the shipped rules anyway ("parked forever" was a young-window artifact; live holds 5-8d sit inside the sim's normal range). The real budget lever is ENTRY-side (per-book max_open / budget allocation) — filed for review. Study registered in SELFTEST_EXCLUDE; any future enactment must name its exit `max_hold_timeout`, never `time_stop` (`record_close`'s `was_stop = "stop" in reason` substring would silently count it toward StoplossGuard — the study's own trap-find).
- Selftests: taker +9 (grandfathered bar doesn't book the gap, its own bar still fires, 72h hold outlives 48h, junk/legacy/switch-off fail-safe, entry_bars census), funding +10 (promoted-bars hold through a fade, kill switch, close-row basis labelling incl. no-context None). Deploy: taker shadow rides freqtrade-bots auto; `trail-blazer-live` (live Farmer), `funding-farmer-shadow` (twin), `tide-rider-lighter-live` (live taker, build parity) dispatched at merge — verified by build stamps, per doctrine.

## 2026-07-22 (bs) — 🧭 the oracle GRADES ITSELF: per-asset directional calls joined to their own forward outcomes (the module's 07-07 promise, built 15 days late; publish-only)

- **The debt, in the module's own words:** the 07-07 header promised *"after a week of history we compare its calls against what bots did (the enforcement decision is data, not vibes)"* and the 17-Jul swap header admitted nothing ranks a regime call as RIGHT — *"that needs the calls joined to outcomes."* Fifteen days of archive later, no join existed anywhere. It is also exactly the evidence the review-gated item-18 STEP 2 (per-asset gate consumes) needs on the table: does SPY's own regime call predict SPY's forward return on this venue's tape?
- **`grade_pairs()` (pure, selftested):** every DIRECTIONAL call (LONG/SHORT-window) on a NEW closed daily bar becomes ONE pending evidence unit (per-bar dedupe — 48 intraday cycles of the same bar are one call, the episodes-not-trades rule applied to the oracle itself); graded prequentially against the pair's OWN later closes at +1 and +3 bars (first observation ≥ h; a missed cycle grades on the wider move, recorded not idealized). `hit` = closed the CALLED way; `avg_pp` is signed INTO the call — a SHORT on a book that falls 1.2% scores +1.2pp (MIRROR-correct short scoring pinned in the selftest; the brain's drift grader was direction-blind once, never again). dir-flat/chop-gated are never graded — no direction claimed, no credit farmed.
- **Payload:** additive `grades` (per-sym `{d1,d3: {n, hit, avg_pp}}`) + `grading` (carried pending/sums state; bounded — PENDING_CAP 200, out-of-coverage calls age out at 10d, malformed state degrades to clean start). NO consumer reads either; `fleet.read`, the (bc) regime gate, history rows and every existing field are bit-untouched. Venue-pure by construction: Lighter closes graded against Lighter closes.
- **Live rehearsal (this session, real venue, publish no-op):** 16/16 majors + 4/10 non-crypto graded, **9 directional calls recorded pending** — first d1 outcomes land when tomorrow's bars close, and the log's new SELF-GRADE line is the deploy receipt. ## 2026-07-21 (br) — the zero-measured-fills mystery SOLVED and fixed [was (bp), then (bq) — renumbered twice in the three-session letter race; the complement took bp, the incubator register took bq]: fills now match by OUR OWN tx hash (operator: "fix and continue")

- **The fill_src histogram (shipped in (bn), deployed ~an hour before this) named the blocker on its first read:** live Farmer 26/29 recent reads `no-match:both(no-match:trades)` with `id-miss` on EVERY one, taker adding `skipped:budget` — i.e. the venue's tape was READ, our account's trades were THERE, and the client_order_index we sent **never appears in the trade rows**. Verified against the venue's own public schema: trade rows DO carry ask/bid_client_id fields, but 0 of 61 real orders across both live bots ever matched — the venue does not echo OUR submitted id into the tape the way the 17-Jul fix assumed.
- **THE FIX: match by the transaction hash instead.** Every venue trade row stamps the TAKER transaction's `tx_hash`, and our own `create_market_order` submission returns exactly that hash — an exact, unforgeable name for our fill that depends on nothing the venue has to echo. `market_open`/`market_close` now return `tx_hash` additively (`_tx_hash_of` probes every response shape the SDK has shipped; a repr-string can never false-match); `_our_fills` gains a tx tier SENIOR to the id tier with **ownership still checked** (the doctrine stands: an id narrows, it never authorises); `read_fill`'s escalation becomes tx-on-both-tapes → id-on-both-tapes → labelled heuristic, retrying only on no-match; both live bots thread the hash through all 7 order sites. Partial fills of one IOC order share the taker's hash — the VWAP across them IS the fill price. `measured_from_reason` already treats the new `trades(tx)` / `recentTrades-tx` labels as measured.
- Fail-safe unchanged end-to-end: telemetry never raises, never delays an order, and a venue layer without the new kwarg degrades to the id tier via TypeError (pinned in the fills contract). Selftests: +7 venue fixtures (tx senior, ownership beats a matching hash, unmatched hash is a MISS never a silent blend, every `_tx_hash_of` shape); full suite + all guards green.
- Receipts from the (bo) deploys, verified on the live bus before this went out: arm-drift CLEARED (verdict moved to the honest `xp-contaminated` while the 0.075 candidate runs), judge `running` on matched builds, watchdog warnings ZERO (the 24-vs-20 warning that stood all day is gone), 23/23 rows fresh.

Payloads predating the register render exactly as before (fallback pinned in the smoke test).

Validated: incubator selftest +2 blocks (register merge/roles/best-ever/cap/junk-prior; lifecycle-join precedence + stats whitelist + dark-judge fallback), an offline 4-cycle `run_once` rehearsal through the venue seams (real gene set: born stable, cycles increment, proposal dedupe holds, statuses follow a judge move, short-tape carry), a 13-fragment dashboard render smoke incl. empty-hide + pre-register fallback, full pytest green, born-dark + venue-purity guards green. ## 2026-07-21 (bh) — BRAIN WIDENS (operator: "brain needs to be able to widen too"): stake mults go TWO-WAY — a proven tag can earn 1.25x/1.5x on the mirror bars

- **The design inverts the reduce side symmetrically, then tightens it.** `brain_stats.qualify_v3(expand=True)`: a POSITIVE bucket qualifies for 1.25x (post_wr > 0.55, Wilson **LOWER** bound > 0.50, t ≥ +2.0) or 1.5x (post > 0.60, w_lo > 0.55, t ≥ +2.5) — where condemnation uses the OPTIMISTIC Wilson bound (benefit of the doubt before shrinking someone), expansion uses the PESSIMISTIC one (a raise must survive the worst plausible read). **No soft-n path** (pooling may condemn a thin tag via its family, but praise does not inherit — a sibling's win rate is not this tag's; pinned by the glowing-prior fixture), **no EMER fast-path** (urgency exists to stop bleeding; there is no urgent reason to size UP), and the identical 3-run streak gate.
- **Armed only when BOTH switches say so:** v3 engine (the `BRAIN_MULT_ENGINE=v2` kill switch zeroes expand too — v2 stays frozen reduce-only) AND `BRAIN_MULT_EXPAND` (default on per the mandate; `off` stands down just the widening while reductions keep working). Payload stamps `mode: two-way|reduce-only` so consumers and dashboards read the honest label.
- **Consumers:** `fleet_bus.MULT_CEIL` 1.0 → **1.5** — a deliberate scope expansion of the documented reduce-only contract, recorded in CLAUDE.md the same day (both contract sites amended). The clamp still bounds both directions (pinned: 3.0 → 1.5, 0.25 → 0.5, 1.25 passes). Family bot logs any non-1.0 mult; **SHADOW books only — no live bot reads mults.**
- **Validated per doctrine:** `brain_replay.py` full PASS with expand ARMED (ledger no-regression + all 6 synthetic discrimination scenarios); brain_stats selftest +6 expand fixtures (default-path unchanged / 1.5 hard / 1.25 soft / small-n refused / family-praise refused / reduce untouched by the switch); fleet_bus +3 clamp cases; the old reduce-only pytest pin updated to pin the NEW contract (not deleted — re-aimed, with the mandate cited); full suite + born-dark + venue-purity green.
- Sequenced honestly: the fleet's expand doctrine (replay/backtest-first) is satisfied here by the v3 evidence machinery itself — the mirror bars ARE the evidence gate, out-of-sample by decay construction, and the blast radius is a $50-stake shadow book at ≤1.5x with TTL-free auto-release (a tag that stops qualifying drops the mult next run, same as reductions).

Howard ingests the wider fleet's `paper_trades` ledger into the ecosystem DB (source='fleet') so the system learns alongside every bot already publishing.

## 2026-07-21 (as) — review decisions D1–D3 SHIPPED (operator: "do D1-D3 now"): capital is not P&L, the judge's inverted experiment is re-spec'd, and the Farmer's rail baseline survives a pre-halt restart

- **D1 — DEPOSITS NO LONGER PRINT AS PROFIT (reporting-only; no rail or entry decision reads pnl_abs).** The EquityGuard now records the ledger-grade collateral delta on every cash-accepting path — the cash-move escape and the collateral-stable/consistent rebase (flat-collateral model-drift concessions record NOTHING: misclassifying P&L as capital would be the inverse bug) — via `_record_capital_move`/`pop_capital_moves`, surfaced through `LighterClient.pop_capital_moves()`. Both live bots fold the events into a persisted `capital_adjust` ledger (last 20 events kept for audit) the same loop they're accepted, publish `extra.capital_adjust` so the row is self-describing, and subtract capital from `pnl_abs`. The two 18-Jul deposits predate the mechanism, so `CAPITAL_ADJUST_USD` backfills them — defaults are the measured values (Farmer 32.55, Taker 32.22 — review N1); the operator can override to the exact cent by env, or zero it if `initial_equity` is ever re-baselined by hand. Declared residuals: a deposit landing on a FLAT book (no continuity check runs) still needs the env knob; a crash in the seconds between a heal and the same-iteration fold loses that event (env knob recovers); a funding settlement large enough to breach the continuity tolerance alone would be misclassified as capital (orders of magnitude above the band's built-in funding allowance, and every event is logged).
- **D2 — THE JUDGE'S CLAMP-INVERTED EXPERIMENT IS RE-SPEC'D, and it was worse than mislabeled: it was DEADLOCKED.** The running candidate `enter-gate-0.30` (a gate WIDENING in pre-basis-fix units) kept asserting 0.30 into the re-denominated registry (hi=0.075); the write clamped to 0.075 TRUE — a TIGHTENING — and the arm's receipts stamp 0.075 while `ran_candidate` demanded 0.30, so the skew gate excluded EVERY close since 17-Jul (measured on the ledger: 20 valid-receipt closes, zero accrued — "floors: shadow 0/30" forever). New `_respec_clamped` (pure, selftested): a RUNNING candidate whose stored values differ from their registry clamp is renamed honestly (`enter-gate-0.30@enter_apr=0.075`), its levers rewritten to the clamped truth, and its window RESTARTED (pre-clamp closes must not mix into the paired bar; accrual starts immediately since the receipts already match). Idempotent; unclampable levers stay with the INVALID/`_needs_reset` paths. Also per the review's sanction: `enter-gate-0.0375` (the widening) is DROPPED from CANDIDATES — `backtest_funding_lighter.py` measured 0.03–0.08 TRUE as the worst region of the gate curve; a 7-day judge slot re-testing it contradicts the venue's own tape.
- **D3 — THE FARMER'S DAILY-LOSS RAIL BASELINE NOW SURVIVES A PRE-HALT RESTART** (review item 15 residual, same class as the 16-Jul `last_ts` fix). `lighter_funding_bot` restores `day_start_equity` from the persisted `:live` state when the stored day matches TODAY (UTC) — previously only the HALT record restored it, so a restart part-way down a losing day re-based the 10% rail to the already-depressed boot equity. The halt record stays SENIOR; dry_run untouched; the baseline persists in the existing loop-bottom `save_state`. This is the Ticket Taker's already-proven pattern (`day_start` in `LIVE_STATE_KEY`) ported to the long-lived bot — the live pair now matches.
- Tests: 7 new equity-guard tests (escape/rebase record, withdrawal sign, flat-collateral negative case, pop-clears, wiring pins) + judge selftest cases for the re-spec (the live defect verbatim, idempotency, unclampable pass-through); full suite 175 green, all three touched bots' selftests green, born-dark + venue-purity guards green.
- **A push is NOT a deploy:** none of these files is on the auto-redeploy path list. To make D1–D3 live: `railway up` the Funding Farmer service, the Ticket Taker service, and freqtrade-bots (the judge) — then marker-grep the running containers per [[railway-cli-frozen-services]]. The judge's re-spec push notification is itself the D2 deploy receipt.

## 2026-07-18 (aq) — the (ao) deposit-heal couldn't fire on the RUN-ONCE bot; the reject streak now survives the relaunch (hardened across 4 adversarial findings)

- **THE OTHER HALF OF THE (ao) INCIDENT — measured on the deploy that shipped (ao).** (ao) heals a deposit-stuck bot by re-baselining after `rebase_after` consecutive continuity rejects. It healed the Funding Farmer within minutes — but the Ticket Taker stayed at `equity=None` after the same deploy. Root cause: the Farmer is a long-lived `while True` process, so its EquityGuard (and the in-memory reject streak `self._rejects`) persists across reads; the Ticket Taker is **RUN-ONCE** (`tickettaker_loop.sh`: `python3 lighter_ticket_taker.py; sleep 300`), so every 300s a fresh process rebuilds the guard and `self._rejects` resets to `[]`. The streak could never reach `rebase_after`, so the rebase **never fired** — a deposit strands any run-once bot equity-blind forever (which ALSO disables the daily-loss rail, since it needs a non-None equity).
- **THE FIX — persist the reject streak, but ONLY for run-once bots, and only via the corroborated path.** The persisted blob is now `{"last": <accepted>, "rejects": [...]}` for a run-once bot; a fresh process restores the streak, appends its own reject, and rebases once the PERSISTED streak reaches `rebase_after`. Every `_judge` verdict is unchanged.
- **HARDENED ACROSS 3 ADVERSARIAL FINDINGS (parallel refutation before deploy), each a real hole closed before shipping:**
  1. **Funding-bot regression (HIGH).** Persisting the streak unconditionally strips the long-lived Farmer's redeploy "streak reset": a dislocation active across a `railway up` would restore a 2-long streak, skip the boot double-read (`has_state=True`), and its first read becomes reject #3 → rebase onto the phantom via the un-corroborated `total_stable` path. **Fix:** persistence is OPT-IN (`persist_reject_streak`, `EquityGuard`/`LighterClient`) — the Ticket Taker sets it, the Farmer leaves it False and behaves EXACTLY as before (memory-only streak, flat blob, redeploy reset intact).
  2. **`mark_gap`→`continuity` laundering (HIGH).** `need` is sized by the current read's reason, but `recent` pulled the last `need` persisted rejects of ANY reason — so two high-bar `mark_gap` rejects (need=12) + one fresh `continuity` read (need=3) rebased at the low bar via `total_stable`, onto the exact 2026-07-11 phantom class. **Fix:** the streak RESETS on a reason change (a streak is "N consecutive SELF-CONSISTENT rejects"), and on the run-once path `total_stable` is disabled entirely — a run-once bot heals ONLY via the fully-corroborated `coll_stable`; a total-only phantom keeps it equity-blind (recoverable) instead of rebasing.
  3. **Heal-failure if `collateral` included uPnL (conditional).** `coll_stable`'s `identity_ok` (`total == collateral + Σupnl`) is only satisfiable if the venue's `collateral` field excludes unrealized P&L. **Resolved by measurement, not assumption:** queried the live Lighter account (733196) — `collateral − (total − Σupnl) = 1e-6`, i.e. **collateral EXCLUDES uPnL**; the identity holds and the deposit genuinely heals via `coll_stable`. Confirms the (ao) design's core premise on the real venue.
  4. **Legacy-blob entries bypass (HIGH/CRITICAL — the second verification round's DEPLOY BLOCKER, reproduced end-to-end).** The live Taker's first post-deploy blob is the OLD flat shape with no `entries`, so (ao)'s `entries_match` graceful-degradation skipped entry verification outright — and an entry+upnl lockstep phantom (entry 100→70, upnl +30, gap held at 0) then **false-rebased +13%** into the daily-loss rail via `coll_stable`, on the exact state the deploy ships into. **Fix — the collateral-moved discriminator:** when entry verification is impossible (no stored entries), the rebase additionally requires `|collateral − baseline collateral| > tol`. A genuine deposit MOVES ledger cash; the lockstep phantom keeps collateral flat (its injection rides upnl, and `identity_ok` pins total = collateral + upnl). Honest legacy-blob deposit → heals; phantom → stays equity-blind (recoverable). The verified-wrong naive fix (just disabling the degradation on the persist path) is documented in-code as a DO-NOT: it re-strands the honest deposit — the exact incident this file heals.
- **Streak hygiene:** restore keeps only a CONTIGUOUS, SAME-REASON run (each reject within `_REJECT_STREAK_MAX_GAP_S=900s` — 3× the run-once cadence — of its successor, newest recent vs now), so a dead sub-`need` episode can't be stitched to a fresh reject a relaunch later; future-stamped rejects (clock skew / corrupt blob) are dropped rather than allowed to anchor the walk. In-memory streak bounded to `4*rebase_after`. Old flat blobs read as last-only (backward-compatible).
- Tests (+14, 37 in the guard file): run-once relaunch heals a drifting-book deposit ACROSS process boundaries (via `coll_stable`); long-lived guard NEVER persists a streak (Farmer regression guard); `mark_gap`→`continuity` is not laundered; a bare `total_stable` phantom without corroboration is refused on the run-once path; **the legacy-blob PAIR — lockstep phantom rejects AND honest deposit heals on the same no-entries state**; the persist-flag wiring pinned at all three sites (EquityGuard default False / LighterClient threads it / Taker opts in, venue_context does not); future-ts junk dropped; reason-change resets; wide-gap and stale streaks drop; accept clears the streak. Full suite **161 passed, 1 skipped**; `sim_equity_guard` 0/32 dislocated prints accepted / 0% false rejects; both live bots' selftests + born-dark/venue-purity/sdk-pin guards green.
- **Declared follow-up (pre-existing, both live bots, NOT worsened here):** `day_start_equity` never re-baselines on a detected mid-day cash move — a withdrawal can trip the daily-loss rail as if it were a trading loss, a deposit loosens it (`lighter_ticket_taker.py` day-start adoption; mirror in the funding bot). Verified MEDIUM / non-blocking; agenda for the Jul-21 review.

## 2026-07-18 (ao) — a live deposit stuck BOTH real-money bots at equity=None; the EquityGuard now heals it, hardened across 5 adversarial rounds

- **THE INCIDENT (measured live).** An operator deposit into the Funding Farmer + Ticket Taker sub-accounts stuck both at `equity=None` on the dashboard while still trading. `account_value()` runs each read through the EquityGuard; a deposit is an equity jump the order book can't explain, so the continuity check rejects it — and the self-heal rebase never fires because it compares raw TOTALS, which drift with mids on a small volatile book, so the reject streak is never "consistent". The bot stays equity-blind, which ALSO disables the daily-loss rail (it needs a non-None equity) — the stuck state is itself a real-money hazard.
- **THE FIX (in `_reject`, the self-heal path; `_judge`'s dislocation checks are unchanged).** Rebase when COLLATERAL (the ledger cash figure, which does NOT drift with mids) has been stable across `rebase_after` consecutive continuity rejects, gated so the rebased equity is fully book-corroborated: `identity_ok` (total == collateral + Σupnl), `marks_corroborated` (EVERY held coin's mid readable, so STEP 1 cross-checked every upnl), and `entries_match` (each held position's entry == the entry stored at the last accepted read). Sizes are already pinned by `_same_book`. So every venue-reported per-position value is anchored to a trusted reference; the only residual is O(~2·tol) slack + a persistent collateral corruption — the SAME tradeoff the original total-stable rebase already makes.
- **HARDENED ACROSS 5 ADVERSARIAL VERIFICATION ROUNDS**, each of which found a real hole I closed before shipping: (1) an immediate mark-verified accept in `_judge` re-opened the dislocation hole (total is derived from collateral, so a phantom collateral drop co-moves total) → reverted for the rebase approach; (2) a dark book let a phantom upnl rebase → require mark coverage; (3) a tiny dark leg's phantom upnl is unbounded by its notional → require EVERY coin's mid; (4) a compensating upnl+entry corruption kept STEP 1's gap at 0 → require `entries_match`; (5) the state-restore path rebuilt `_last` WITHOUT entries, which would have re-stranded the currently-stuck bots on the very deploy that ships the fix → restore entries + degrade `entries_match` gracefully for legacy snapshots.
- **KNOWN RESIDUAL, declared:** a same-coin-size close+reopen with a venue-stale entry can bypass `entries_match` (MEDIUM) — NOT exploitable for these bots, which size by USD clip (equal coin-size ⇒ equal price ⇒ equal entry); fully closing it needs fill/nonce tracking, disproportionate to a non-exploitable edge. The bounded ~2·tol over/under-statement is the irreducible floor of any tolerance check.
- Tests: deposit heals via collateral-stable rebase on a drifting book; transient collateral glitch, dark book, partial-dark leg, entry-corruption phantom all still reject; legacy-restore (no entries) still heals. 24 equity-guard tests, full suite 148 green. `venues/` selftests + all deploy guards green.
