## 2026-07-17 (o) — INCIDENT: the disarm re-armed TIDE RIDER, not the taker — and the taker had no image to run in
- **REAL MONEY, and it was MY instruction that caused it.** The operator set `REAL_MONEY_KILL=DISARMED_I_UNDERSTAND` on `tide-rider-lighter-live` to take the taker live. That service is built from **`Dockerfile.trendlighter`**, whose `CMD` is `lighter_trend_bot.py` — the taker's code is not even IN that image, so `TT_VENUE` there would have done nothing. The kill switch was the ONLY thing holding Tide Rider down. Disarming it **booted the bot being RETIRED, live**, and it re-bought the exact position the operator had just flattened by hand: `OPEN TRX long $12 | ema50 0.3278 > ema200 0.3195` at 17:42:22 Sydney, on a $34.67 real book. Same shape as the 11-Jul incident (a halt removed → HYPE re-bought 37s after boot). Operator closed TRX and re-armed; the row is refusing again. **`lighter-ticket-taker-lighter` never existed — the taker never went live.**
- **MY ERROR, precisely.** The go-live instructions led with "set these vars" and buried the start-command change in prose. For a service whose image bakes a different bot's `CMD`, the env vars are inert and the disarm is the only thing that lands — the most dangerous possible subset of a config change to apply first. **A go-live instruction must name the IMAGE before the env**, because the image decides which bot the kill switch is protecting.
- **ROOT CAUSE: the taker had no image of its own.** It has only ever shipped inside `Dockerfile.freqtrade`, and it CANNOT go live there: `run_all.sh` runs `lighter_scout_tuner`, `lighter_ticket_replay`, `strategy_incubator` and `fleet_proprioception` in that same container, and **all four `import lighter_ticket_taker`** — `TT_VENUE=lighter_live` in that env would hand a real-money mode to four modules that must keep grading ALL FOUR lenses over shadow tape. Fixed: **`Dockerfile.tickettaker` + `railway.tickettaker.toml` + `tickettaker_loop.sh`**, the same shape as the trend bot's. The shadow arm keeps running in `freqtrade-bots` as the control arm the go-live rests on.
- **The loop wrapper is not boilerplate.** `main()` is ONE cycle (run-once); the trend/funding bots own their own `while True`. Pairing a run-once `CMD` with `restartPolicyType="always"` would restart it the instant it exits — a hot loop hammering the venue with no sleep. The wrapper carries the cadence at **300s, identical to the shadow arm's**, because two arms scanning the same scout payload at different rates are not comparable records.
- **VERIFIED:** born-dark audit now covers **20 images** and the new one is complete; the taker was booted from the new image's file set ALONE (isolated copy — imports resolve, `allowed_lenses('lighter_live') == {divergence}`, unset `TT_VENUE` refuses); `sh -n` on the wrapper; taker selftests green.
- **Dashboard:** `lighter-ticket-taker-lighter` gets a **900s** stale budget (its 300s cadence, ~2 missed publishes — matching the Funding Farmer's), and so does the shadow arm. `is_live_bot()` already resolves the `-lighter` suffix, so the row registers as LIVE on its first publish with no further wiring. The taker's card copy was **wrong and is now honest**: it advertised "breakout/dip/momentum lenses" — never mentioning divergence, the only lens with forward edge — and said nothing about the live arm being divergence-only.
- **Tide Rider's row DELIBERATELY NOT retired.** It is armed and refusing, but the sub-account still holds **$34.67 of real cash**, and retiring the row would hide real money behind a green dashboard — the exact "hiding a row is not retiring it" trap. A prior session also recorded the row as a deliberate 21-Jul review call. It stays until the money is out and the review says so.

## 2026-07-17 (n) — 🎫 Ticket Taker GO-LIVE build: divergence-only, the stranding bug, and the refusal lifted (operator: "do it now so we can go live")
- **Operator decision, on the record.** The taker takes 🌊 Tide Rider's vacated live slot, **divergence only** (Tide Rider's TRX flattened by the operator). The agent built and verified; **the operator flips `TT_VENUE` + `REAL_MONEY_KILL`** — never the agent.
- **🔴 THE BLOCKER, and it would have fired on the FIRST ticket.** `LighterClient` speaks **FLEET** symbols end to end: `positions()` runs every symbol through `from_lighter()`, and `market_close()` looks the coin up **in that dict** (`lighter_client.py:558`). This bot keys `marks`/`funding`/`meta` by the **venue-native** symbol from its own fetch. Live, `"1000BONK"` is looked up in a dict keyed `"kBONK"` → the arm **sees itself FLAT and re-opens every cycle**, while the exit pass never examines the real position — no stop, no max-hold. **MEASURED: `1000BONK` is a divergence ticket right now (short, gap 87.5pp) and the shadow arm holds it** — the first thing a divergence-only arm touches.
- **The half that strands it: `_resolve()` TOLERATES a native symbol.** So `market_OPEN` works while `market_CLOSE` misses its lookup and returns **`None` WITHOUT RAISING** — open succeeds, close silently no-ops, ledger books a close, `meta.pop()` runs, and the position **stays open on the venue untracked forever**. The existing guard caught only exceptions, so the block whose own comment read *"never book a close that did not happen"* **missed the exact case it was written for**. `None` is now a FAILED close at both call sites.
- **ROOT CAUSE — an asymmetry in `venues/symbol_map.py`.** `from_lighter()` is GENERIC (any `1000*` → `k*`); `to_lighter()` was a **hardcoded list of FOUR**, frozen at the 2026-07-09 market dump. Lighter now lists **SIX** 1000-markets, so `1000NOT`/`1000TOSHI` round-tripped to `kNOT`/`kTOSHI` and then to **themselves** — symbols the venue does not have. `to_lighter` now mirrors `from_lighter`. **VERIFIED across all 218 live markets: ZERO round-trip failures**; all 8 explicit entries unchanged (PEPE/SHIB/BONK/FLOKI raw-unit mults preserved); an unknown k-form still fails safe (`1000NEIRO` absent → skipped). Shared real-money file — behaviour-neutrality was proven, not assumed.
- **Converted at the venue BOUNDARY, not by normalising the bot.** `meta` + the broker snapshot are PERSISTED in native keys; changing the bot's key space would have stranded every open position on the shadow arm. One space, converted where the two meet.
- **DIVERGENCE-ONLY is fail-CLOSED and does not read the brain** (`allowed_lenses`, shipped in `afa6f7a`). `vetoed_lenses()` is fail-safe **OPEN** by design — a dark brain, stale payload, `load_state` blip or a dropped lens all collapse it to `set()`. Correct for a shadow book whose job is grading; catastrophic as the only thing between real money and three lenses rejected three times. Brain veto stays SENIOR on top; a hard assert sits at the last statement before money moves.
- **Two more real-money gates closed:** `TT_VENUE` is now **MANDATORY** (identity must never be inherited — same class as today's `VENUE` fix on the other two live bots; gated inside `main()` because four modules import this file). `apply_tuning()` is a **NO-OP in live**: the `taker.*` levers are the **SHADOW** lane, authored by `scout-tuner` (no live authority, gated on shadow tape) — going live would silently promote a shadow lane to a real-money lane. Only `live.*` levers may steer real money.
- **REFUSAL LIFTED — and what was traded away is written into the code, not just this log.** divergence's episode-deduped forward grade is **+0.004%/4h** (ehit4h CI **0.494–0.572**, straddling a coin flip) against a **6.68h** median hold; its realized **+4.23%/trade is 5–1000x** the brain's grade of the SAME signal at n=2934 — that gap is the luck; **$10.72 over 7 fills ≈ 3 fills of swing** at 100% WR. **The 30-day gate (WR>55% AND maxDD<15%) is NOT met: this is a deliberate exception, not a pass.** What replaced the refusal is guards, not permission.
- **A fixture had to be RE-POINTED, which is worth its own line:** the live suite asserted *"lighter_live must refuse"* — i.e. it asserted the presence of the very gate the operator lifted, so it would have demanded the code stay as it was. Re-pointed at the **identity** refusal (`TT_VENUE` unset), which no decision can lift. Same lesson as the 17-Jul "a test must not fail on GOOD NEWS".
- **VERIFIED:** `--selftest` + `--selftest-live` both green (all 10 live order-path checks); symbol round-trip fixture for all six 1000-markets; `TT_VENUE`-unset refuses; live `apply_tuning() == {}`; live `allowed_lenses == {divergence}`; **all four importers still grade ALL FOUR lenses with `TT_VENUE=lighter_live` in env**; born-dark green across 19 images.
- **STILL OPEN, operator's call:** *"divergence only" ≠ "shorts only"*. `short-divergence` n=7 (all TP, +$10.72); **`long-divergence` n=0** — and the live arm can take it (the shadow book holds one now, XPD). One-line change if the intent was the measured thing.

## 2026-07-17 (m) — INCIDENT: the shadow arm has been CRASH-LOOPING for an hour behind an "online" row — born-dark, one layer down
- **The Ticket Taker's shadow arm has never run. Not once.** `c5924e4` shipped `TT_VENUE=lighter_shadow` as the DEFAULT, which constructs `venues.lighter_client.LighterClient` → `import lighter`. **`Dockerfile.freqtrade` installs only `psycopg2-binary`** — it has never installed `lighter-sdk`. So from 05:35 UTC the taker died every 5 minutes on `venues.base.VenueError: lighter-sdk missing ... No module named 'lighter'`, caught live in the running container's logs.
- **MEASURED, not inferred:** the `-lshadow` row last published **05:48 UTC** and then sat stale for ~1h; `venue_orders` for this bot is **still ZERO**. The arm built to earn a go-live had not completed a single cycle.
- **THE FOSSIL.** `c5924e4` closed with "shadow arm healthy (1013.99, 18 closes)" and `09245dd` repeated it. Both were reading the **last publish of the OLD image** — a row that keeps its last-good values when the writer dies. A stale row is not a heartbeat, and `online` is not a health check. This is CLAUDE.md's own rule ("verify a NEW module by its OWN published output, never by 'it shipped'") failing in the exact way it was written to prevent.
- **WHY THE GUARD MISSED IT — the guard was right, its model was too small.** `audit_image_imports` models **repo-local** imports: it caught the missing `COPY venues/` (c5924e4's message credits it) and was satisfied. **`lighter` is a PIP dependency, invisible to it.** The guard verified the FILE and not the WHEEL. Same failure class as the four born-dark incidents, one layer down — and it is now **five in four days**. `requirements.txt` has always listed `lighter-sdk>=1.1.1`; no Dockerfile installs it by name, and this image never installs `requirements.txt` at all.
- **FIX: `Dockerfile.freqtrade` installs `lighter-sdk>=1.1.1` + `websockets>=12.0.0`.** Not optional — the taker's DEFAULT mode needs them. Deliberately NOT "fall back to PaperBroker when the SDK is missing": silent degradation to modelled fills is precisely the defect `c5924e4` set out to remove.
- **DETECTIVE CONTROL, because the hour of silence is the real finding.** A run-once process has no supervisor loop, so a crash published NOTHING and the row just aged — dead and broken look identical until someone greps the container. `_supervised()` now marks the row **ERROR** and re-raises (status-only; last good equity intact), the same reason `SafetyRails._publish_refusal` exists. Boot refusals (incl. the `lighter_live` evidence gate) pass through untouched — **a refusal is not a crash**. The fetch-fail skip path also gained a `heartbeat` (the funding bot's 12-Jul GO-GREEN rule, ported): **a skip is not a death.**
- **NEGATIVE FIXTURE, and it caught my own first draft.** Test 10 raises the incident's real error; the first version raised it inside `fetch_marks_and_funding()` — which `main()` CATCHES and returns from — so it passed against a supervisor that had never executed. The incident crashed at `LighterClient` construction, **uncaught**, propagating out of `main()` to `<module>`; the fixture now reproduces that shape. A fixture that cannot fail is the thing it is testing.
- **FOLLOW-UP (not done here):** extend `audit_image_imports` to third-party imports — map each image's `pip install` + `requirements.txt` against the non-stdlib imports its COPY'd files actually reach. That is the control that would have caught this at ship time instead of an hour later.

## 2026-07-17 (l) — 🎫 the Ticket Taker's ORDER PATH is built (still REFUSED); and the basis fix missed a THIRD book — the one that collects carry
- **THE ORDER PATH IS DONE — the half `09245dd` stopped short of, mirroring `lighter_funding_bot`.** That commit wired the signer + rails and deliberately left the orders, because every call site assumed a local broker (`broker.pos/.open/.close/.equity/.fees`) and refactoring a DEPLOYED loop mid-flight costs the very evidence we are waiting for. Now live reads **positions from the VENUE** (the account is the source of truth, not our memory — the 11-Jul ghost-position lesson), sends `market_open`/`market_close`, reconciles fills from the venue's own trade tape, and carries an equity guard, a daily-loss rail and a durable halt.
- **`lighter_live` STILL REFUSES, and that is the point.** Divergence remains the only lens the brain does not veto and it has **n=7 closes** (all `tp`, in a 28h window) against a fleet gate of 30-day WR>55% AND maxDD<15%. The path exists so the switch is one env var WHEN the evidence lands. **`venue_orders` for this bot is still ZERO** — not one of its 18 trades has touched a Lighter book; every fill it has ever booked was modelled. Lifting the refusal today would be the Trail Blazer mistake exactly.
- **THE BASIS FIX MISSED A THIRD BOOK — and it is the worst one to miss.** `93be95e` found two (`lighter_funding_spread_bot`, `lighter_family_bot`) and called the set complete; `31ec660` before it claimed five and fixed four. **`lighter_ticket_taker.py:361` was the third**: `drag = size * mark * rate * hours` — Lighter quotes per 8h, so it over-accrued **8x**. It survived both sweeps because it models carry INLINE rather than calling `funding_basis.to_hourly`, so a grep for the fixed call site could not see it. It is now the LAST self-modelled Lighter accrual in the fleet (verified: `funding_carry_bot:305` is Hyperliquid, hourly, correct — leave it alone).
- **MEASURED on the bot's own live state, not inferred.** All **5 open positions are `divergence`** — the one lens that COLLECTS carry and the ONLY one with a positive forward grade. Open `funding_paid` totalled **+0.4379 vs a true +0.0547** (artifact **+0.383**). The contamination lands exactly where it decides a go-live: `short-divergence` closes read **+4.48% / +5.12% / +4.50%** against a **+4% TP bar** — the excess above the bar IS the 8x-inflated carry credit, inflating the grade of the only lens that could earn this book real money. **The same shape as Counterweight, whose entire reported profit was this artifact.** Fixed FORWARD-ONLY; the 18 persisted ledger rows keep the scar.
- **The notional cap now lives on the RAIL that enforces it (`venues/safety.py: open_notional`).** `lighter_funding_bot` and `lighter_trend_bot` — **both real-money bots** — each carried their own copy, and the Taker's live path would have been the third. They were code-identical the day this moved, which is the argument and not the counter-argument: two definitions with **two different selftests** (the trend bot's never covered the short-at-own-entry case) is one edit from drifting, and the drift is a cap breach on real money (it breached on 15-Jul). Both bots re-export it under the original private name, so every call site is untouched and **their own selftests are the proof this is behaviour-neutral** — not a claim that it is.
- **The equity guard was MISSING from the live path, silently.** `LighterClient` only builds an `EquityGuard` when it has a signer, and `vet_account_read` returns the raw print **unvetted** when the guard is None. `09245dd` constructed the client without `guard_state_key`, so live would have run its daily-loss rail on UNVETTED equity — the exact 11-Jul failure where one dislocated print tripped the rail and the flatten sold into the dislocation for a real **-5.9%**. The funding bot gets this free via `venue_context()`; this bot builds its client directly, so it must pass it explicitly. It now does.
- **`--selftest-live` drives the live branch END-TO-END against a stub venue** (no network, no signer, no money), because a production-refused path is otherwise code no one has ever executed — which is how the fleet got a "live-ready" bot that would not have placed a single order (`c5924e4`). 9 checks: entry SENDS a real order + records the REAL fill; the notional cap is senior; the kill switch flattens the VENUE's book; daily loss CONFIRMS (debounce) then flattens + halts DURABLY; **a failed close books no phantom row and KEEPS the position**; live funding reaches the ledger but never modelled equity; **unreadable positions SKIP the cycle** (a phantom-empty set zeroes both the slot count AND the cap's input — "no positions" would read as "full book available" on top of real open risk); **a failed flatten reports the open book, not a green zero**; and the DEPLOYED shadow arm still trades on the true basis.
- **MY OWN UNIT TEST WAS NOT A DETECTOR, proved by mutation.** Re-introducing `rate * hours` in `main()` leaves `--selftest` **PASSING** — its basis assertions exercise `funding_basis` (which has its own selftest), not the CALL SITE. Only `--selftest-live` caught it. The block is kept as the worked arithmetic and now says so; the guard is the live selftest. A test that cannot fail on the bug is documentation wearing a test's clothes.
- **`TT_VENUE` deliberately KEEPS its default**, unlike `$VENUE` in (k). Same rule, different exposure: this bot has no service of its own (`run_all.sh` loops it inside the shared freqtrade-bots container with no env), so a mandatory var would kill the shadow arm collecting the evidence — and an unset var CANNOT produce a live taker, since live requires the literal `"lighter_live"`. **GO-LIVE PRECONDITION recorded in the code while it is cheap:** the live arm must be its OWN service with `TT_VENUE` explicit, or a lost var there double-writes `-lshadow` — (k)'s silent two-writers-one-row failure, rebuilt.
- Born-dark green (19 images); purity unchanged (8 violations / 5 files, none in the 4 files touched). Live-path additions are LIVE-ONLY by construction: the shadow arm's only behaviour change is the basis fix.

## 2026-07-17 (k) — the lighter_shadow fallback made the real-money bots' failure SILENT; VENUE is now mandatory for both
- **`ff5cd43` is RIGHT on the rule and it opened a hole — this closes it, it does not revert it.** Defaulting `VENUE` to `lighter_shadow` is correct ("the real money fallback has to be lighter") and the ordering was careful. But the two REAL-MONEY bots derive their IDENTITY from `VENUE`, and the change flipped their lost-var failure mode from **LOUD to SILENT**. Found by a 30-agent purity triage; every link then verified by hand against the code and the live board.
- **THE MECHANISM (Funding Farmer, real money).** `lighter_funding_bot.py:51` `BOT = "perps-funding-lighter"` + `venues/__init__.py:49` `_SUFFIX["lighter_shadow"] = "-lshadow"` = **`perps-funding-lighter-lshadow`** — which is EXACTLY `experiment_judge.py:62` `SHADOW_BOT`. Measured live: that row is **online, $1012.66, 39 closed**. So a lost `VENUE` on `trail-blazer-live` would: boot the LIVE service as a SHADOW book (simulated fills) while its **REAL positions ($67.22, 3 open, 23 closed) sit unmanaged on the venue**; DOUBLE-WRITE the judge's promotion arm (the judge is the only writer of `live.funding.*`); and **never page** — because the row looks healthy. Before `ff5cd43`, the same lost var gave the unsuffixed `perps-funding-lighter`, which publishes nothing → row goes stale → `STALE_AFTER` 15min → the watchdog PHONES. A detective control was traded for silence.
- **AND it silently disarmed Tide Rider's existing guard.** `lighter_trend_bot.py:172` refuses `hl_paper` as a daemon **because hl_paper was the default** — the comment says so. With the default moved, a lost `VENUE` sails straight past it into `crypto-trend-daily-lshadow` (**online, $999.77**), the row `tide-rider-lighter-shadow` already publishes. Two writers, one row, no page. The guard now covers only a case that can no longer happen.
- **THE IRONY, measured:** every SHADOW-only bot (`lighter_perp_sniper` / `_dislocation` / `_funding_spread`, family, momentum) carries a `setdefault("VENUE",...)` or a mode refusal. **`lighter_funding_bot.py` had NEITHER — the least-guarded file in the fleet was the one holding real money.** A lost Railway `VENUE` is not hypothetical: it happened 16-Jul, and it is why the others have setdefaults.
- **THE FIX: `VENUE` is now MANDATORY (no inherited default) for both real-money bots**, `--once` exempt for offline smokes. **Inert for production, verified:** all four services set it explicitly (`trail-blazer-live` + `tide-rider-lighter-live` = `lighter_live`; `funding-farmer-shadow` + `tide-rider-lighter-shadow` = `lighter_shadow`). It only converts the lost-var case back into a loud one. **NEGATIVE FIXTURE (a guard that never fires is not a guard): `VENUE` unset + daemon → exit 1 on BOTH bots; POSITIVE: `VENUE` set in either mode → no refusal; `--once` without `VENUE` → still smokes.** The deeper rule this encodes: a default is fine for a preference, never for an IDENTITY that decides whether real money moves.
- **UNRELATED, FLAGGED NOT TOUCHED:** the LIVE Tide Rider (`crypto-trend-daily-lighter`) is **`status: error`, equity $34.67, 1 open position** — its `REAL_MONEY_KILL` is **ARMED**, so `lighter_live` refuses to boot and the row error-loops. That is the kill switch doing its job, deliberately set, and disarming it is an operator-only action — but it means a real position is currently unmanaged. Contrast `trail-blazer-live`, which is `DISARMED_I_UNDERSTAND` and trading.


## 2026-07-17 (i) — 🛰️ market-context reads LIGHTER natively; the swap is behaviour-neutral, the DATASET is not
- **`market_context.py:44` was the LIGHTER-FIRST fleet's market context, sourced from HYPERLIQUID.** `audit_venue_purity` flagged `host:api.hyperliquid.xyz:44` as SHIPPED. Key names and units unchanged — `{oi_ntl, funding_apr, premium_bps, mark}`. Audit drops **10 -> 9 violations / 7 -> 6 files**; `market_context` leaves the report, no new source appears.
- **THE BRIEF'S PREMISE WAS WRONG, AND IT MATTERED: this is NOT a display feed.** The brief said "only `pnl_dashboard.py` imports market_context — it is a DISPLAY feed", and asked that it be verified rather than trusted. **Measured: NOTHING imports the module — `pnl_dashboard.py` included.** Its only mentions are prose/attribution; it reads `fleet-alerts` + `coin-vetoes` over direct SQL, never the `market-context` key. The real consumers read the bot_state key: **`lighter_funding_bot.py:838` — the LIVE REAL-MONEY Funding Farmer** — and `lighter_dislocation_bot.py:279` (shadow). Had "display feed" been trusted, this swap would have been graded against the wrong blast radius.

It is a DATASET, not a gate. Confirmed end-to-end by running the live bot's real `_mctx_slice` against a real Lighter snapshot: BTC/ETH/SOL populate; an unlisted coin degrades to nulls, not a crash.
- **MEASURED HL-vs-Lighter divergence, 96 overlapping coins** (`mark` median **-6.9bps**, max |26bps| — same asset, same price). **`funding_apr` median diff -0.0044**, |apr| medians **0.1095 (HL) vs 0.1051 (LT)** — those ARE the two venues' documented resting defaults, which is the conversion validating itself. Converted via `funding_basis` (8h); a `24*365` here would have read **0.84** and every number would have stayed self-consistent and merely 8x wrong.
- **TWO FIELDS ARE MATERIALLY DIFFERENT — findings, not papered over.** (1) **`premium_bps`: a different QUANTITY.** Lighter publishes no premium field; it publishes the ingredients, and mark-vs-index is the fleet's own established Lighter premium (`lighter_market_scout.book_stats`, same formula) — so this is the venue's convention, not a proxy invented here. But HL's `premium` matches **NO** instantaneous formula (mark/mid/impact-vs-oracle each reproduce it on only **36-44 of 177** coins): HL publishes a SMOOTHED funding premium, ours is instantaneous. |prem| median **6.4bps (LT) vs 2.4bps (HL)**, ~2.7x. (2) **`oi_ntl`: a LEVEL break** — Lighter/HL notional ratio median **0.052** (p10 0.022, p90 0.117); Lighter is a ~20x smaller venue. `oi_chg_1h/24h` are ratios and cross cleanly.
- **EPOCH BREAK, recorded in the header because a joined dataset spans it.** `heat_mean_apr` measures **HL 0.1278 vs Lighter 0.2210 (1.73x)** on DIFFERENT universes (Lighter lists 105 books HL does not, incl. equity/commodity perps resting at 0.03504). `heat` / `premium_bps` / `oi_ntl` are **not comparable across 17-Jul** — `evaluate_evidence`'s decision+context dataset must split at the swap or it will fit the venue change, not the market. This is the one real cost of the swap, and it is paid in the dataset, not in a trade.
- **`open_interest` is BASE units — VERIFIED, not assumed.** The LT/HL notional ratio holds at ~0.03-0.12 across coins whose marks span 63354 (BTC) to 0.072 (DOGE); were the field quote-denominated that ratio would scale with mark and the two ends would differ by ~10^6. (BTC: 1745 * $63.4k = $110M OI — plausible; $1,745 of total BTC OI would be absurd.)
- **COVERAGE moved TOWARD the consumers, and is explicit.** HL 232 books vs Lighter 201 active, only 96 overlap. 136 HL-only coins go — the fleet does not trade them; both consumers are Lighter bots, so **105 Lighter books that previously resolved to an empty slice now carry context**. A book with no venue rate gets `funding_apr=None` (never 0.0, which would drag heat toward zero); no index gets `premium_bps=None`. A missing input reads as "no evidence".
- **BORN-DARK, 5th outing, caught again — and the first fix was the wrong one.** `Dockerfile.marketcontext` COPY'd only two files; the new `funding_basis` + `venues.symbol_map` imports would have crash-looped the service. The first fix (`COPY venues/symbol_map.py` as a PEP-420 namespace leaf, avoiding the package's real-money surface) **works at runtime but defeats the guard**, which models top-level names only: it registered `symbol_map` as a TOP-LEVEL module — the exact masking its own docstring warns against. Took the precedented path instead (`COPY venues/`, as 8 other images do) and DECLARED `fleet_tuning` + `paper_broker` in `BORN_DARK_OK` with reasons. Audit green across 19 images. **Guard gap logged, not silently worked around** — see the report.
- **`--selftest` added** (house style): contract keys/units, BASE-unit OI, mark-vs-index premium, fleet symbol namespace, inactive/junk rows skipped, coverage Nones, and a **NEGATIVE FIXTURE that fails loud if `funding_basis` is ever "simplified" back to `24*365`**. Its first draft asserted heat saw 3 rates when it saw 2 — **the code was right and the test was wrong**; the fixture that proved it is kept.

## 2026-07-17 (i) — 🧭 the regime oracle reads LIGHTER now; the swap is a no-op and the COVERAGE TRAP is a payload field
- **`regime_oracle.py` was the fleet's last HL-fed SIGNAL organ** (`audit_venue_purity.py`: `regime_oracle.py [SHIPPED] host:api.hyperliquid.xyz :56`). It now fetches Lighter 1d candles (`/api/v1/candles`, paged backward under the 500-bar cap) for its real `UNIVERSE`. The HL path is GONE, not disabled. Guard drops **regime_oracle.py out of the report entirely**; its selftest stays green (a concurrent session had already redesigned that block to assert the DETECTOR'S CAPABILITY rather than the fleet's illness — so it no longer fails on good news).
- **THE SWAP CHANGES NO DECISION — measured on the SHIPPED code, not inherited.** `study_lighter_vs_hl_equivalence.py` proved a claim about DATA ("Lighter candles -> same verdict 99.4% of 3,085 coin-days"); it could NOT prove a claim about the `fetch_daily()` I actually wrote. New `scripts/ab_regime_oracle_venue_swap.py` drives THIS module's real fetch against the verbatim old HL fetch: **today, 16/16 majors IDENTICAL** — and **SEED-INVARIANT** (both arms agree under hysteresis seed None/0/1, so no major sits on the ADX knife-edge today). 60-day rolling replay: **895/900 coin-days agree (99.4%)**, 12 of 15 replayable majors at 100%. It independently reproduces the study's NEAR 07-15/07-16 split **to the decimal** (HL LONG-window adx 13.0/13.4 vs Lighter chop-gated 12.0/12.3) from a DIFFERENT fetch path. The other 3: TAO 05-24 + 06-15, DOGE 05-22.
- **The replay OVERSTATES the live risk, and knowing why matters.** It runs TWO INDEPENDENT hysteresis chains, so one ADX-band straddle latches the arms apart for days. **Production has ONE chain** — `prev_trend` is read from the SHARED published state, so the swap INHERITS the HL-era trend instead of starting a rival. Live divergence <= the replay's. The 0.6% is not price error either: it is this oracle's OWN discontinuity at ADX 17/11, and neither venue is "wrong".
- **THE COVERAGE TRAP IS THE REAL FINDING, and it is now IMPOSSIBLE TO MISS.** Lighter lists some books later than HL, so a young book falls under the 203-bar (`EMA_SLOW+SLOPE_BARS`) gate while HL publishes — the study measured Lighter's **ZEC ungradeable on 115 of 200 replayed days**. A swap that silently drops a coin is a BUG: the fleet reads a smaller universe and never knows. Every ungradeable coin is now NAMED in the additive **`coverage.missing`** with its reason (`no-lighter-book` / `short-history(n<203)` / `fetch-failed:<Exc>`) and still lands in `errors` as before. **No verdict > a wrong verdict.**
- **Dormant, not absent — which is exactly why it needed a field and not a glance.** MEASURED today: **16/16 majors clear the gate; nothing is dropped.** But ZEC has **287 Lighter bars vs HL's 521** — clears by 84, and is STILL too young for the A/B to replay 60 days at all (it is the one major PART 3 could not score). A coin that clears TODAY and vanished YESTERDAY is invisible to a spot check.
- **Coverage collapse degrades SAFELY, by construction not by luck:** `fleet.read` needs an ABSOLUTE n>=4 long or short, so a universe thinned by missing books decays toward "mixed / transitional" — neutral — never toward a confident wrong direction. A dark venue publishes NOTHING (consumers keep last-good + TTL).
- **Behaviour-neutral by construction, asserted MECHANICALLY.** `classify()`, `wilder_adx()`, ADX 17/11, EMA 50/200, `SLOPE_BARS`, `LOOKBACK_DAYS`, the hysteresis, the 203-bar gate, `UNIVERSE`, `KEY`, `TTL_SEC` and every existing payload field are **byte-identical to HEAD** — the A/B asserts this itself, so it cannot silently vary two things (17-Jul lesson: the Stock Leaders A/B differed three ways and its headline pointed the opposite way to the truth). `coverage` is ADDITIVE; `params.source` -> `"lighter-1d"` because it must not lie about the tape.
- **Bar stamps stay MILLISECONDS on purpose.** Both venues stamp `t` in ms at the bar's OPEN, UTC-midnight aligned, so `asof` keeps its shape across the splice — `bot_learn._load_regime_history()` joins this key's ARCHIVE and the seam is the only real risk here (pre-swap rows HL-derived, post-swap Lighter-derived, same column; at 99.4% identical, nearly harmless).
- **Closed candles are now dropped by TIMESTAMP, not by `iloc[:-1]`.** Position-dropping assumes a partial bar always exists; an illiquid book that has not traded today publishes none, and the old rule would then eat a REAL closed day. This is the documented intent ("the partial today-candle is dropped") implemented faithfully, and it is what the study's replay actually measured.
- **The brief's premise is overstated — VERIFIED by grep, not assumed.** "The FLEET'S regime signal" reads no bots: consumers are `pnl_dashboard` (display), `fleet_respiration` (freshness only) and `bot_learn._load_regime_history()` (the history join). No bot trades off this key. The swap's risk is LONGITUDINAL, not live.
- **`pnl_dashboard` fixed in the same pass because this change perturbed it:** the regime-oracle vital counted **TOP-LEVEL PAYLOAD KEYS**, not majors — it displayed **"4 majors tracked" for a 16-major universe** (params/pairs/fleet/errors), and the additive `coverage` key would have silently moved it to 5. Now reads `len(pairs)` (correct: 16) and surfaces `coverage`/`MISSING` where the operator will actually see a dropped coin. Guarded for pre-swap payloads, which carry no `coverage`.
- **New `--selftest` on `regime_oracle.py`** (offline, stubbed venue): closed-bars-by-timestamp, ms stamps preserved, backward paging + termination on a short book, payload shape held, dark venue publishes nothing — and the NEGATIVE FIXTURE that matters: **a 100-bar book must be NAMED in `coverage.missing`, never silently dropped**. ## 2026-07-17 (g) — the fleet could not measure its own execution, and the organ built to do it was reporting DRIFT as slippage (operator: "measure the real fill cost")
- **THE ORGAN WAS LYING, and the number it lied about decides a live bot.** `implementation_shortfall._slip_bps` compared the LIVE arm's **AVERAGE entry price** against the SHADOW arm's **AVERAGE entry price**, per coin, over a **7-day window**. The two arms enter at DIFFERENT MOMENTS at DIFFERENT PRICES — so the difference of their averages measures **price DRIFT**, not execution. No averaging fixes it; the quantity has no execution meaning unless the fills are the same order. Live proof: **HYPE entry_slip −363.2bps beside exit +359.3bps** — a claim the live arm filled 3.6% BETTER than mark — with ETH 197.3 and LIT 326.1, while the organ's own aggregate said **gap −0.237pp / verdict clean**. Read as execution it said the live book bleeds 2-3% a round trip. **WITHDRAWN**; both keys are now always None. `gap_pp` (the paired per-trade read it IS entitled to) is untouched.
- **Its selftest pinned the BUG.** The fixture fed ONE synthetic trade per arm — where "live avg entry vs shadow avg entry" trivially IS the slippage — so it could not fail. Re-pinned to the withdrawal, plus the honest replacement's contract.
- **THE REAL FINDING: the live bot has NEVER recorded a fill price.** All three `lighter_funding_bot.publish_venue_order` calls passed **`px_decision=px, px_fill=px`** — the decision price echoed back — so `slippage_bps` is NULL on all 48 live order rows. The shadow twin (ShadowBroker walks the real book) reports **0.86bps/fill over n=158, spread 1.70bps**. CLAUDE.md has claimed since 15-Jul that the funding bot "records fill prices"; the code never did. The bot HAS the real fill (`venues.lighter_client.last_fill`, the 16-Jul FILL RECON + the `_real_exit` helper feeding `paper_trades`) — it just never handed it to the order ledger. **FIXED**: both live close paths now publish the decision mid and the REAL venue fill separately, with signed per-order slippage.
- **An echoed decision price now records NULL, never 0.0.** `_real_exit` falls back to the decision mid when the venue read fails, so `px_fill == px_decision` means NO reading — recording it as zero slippage is indistinguishable from a perfect fill. That confusion is the entire blind spot. New `_fetch_order_slip` reports `measurable` + `echoed_decision` per arm so "measured 0" and "never recorded" can never be conflated again.
- **WHY IT MATTERS — the whole Funding Farmer verdict hangs on this one unmeasured number.** Driving slip to zero isolates a **+6.18 bps/trade GROSS edge over 1,911 trades, both halves positive** (TP 0.04 / **STOP 0.10→0.03** / hold 72h at gate 0.05 TRUE — the only config that survives; TP 0.06 fails h2 at every slip, STOP 0.10 never passes). So the bot is **FRICTION-BOUND, not edgeless** — and the answer flips entirely on the round-trip cost: **backtest ASSUMED 10bps (loses −$18) / shadow MODELS 1.7bps (wins +$20, both halves) / live-vs-shadow gap implies ~25bps (loses badly)**. Three estimates, 14x apart, **not one of them measured**. The 5bps/fill that produced "no gate has an edge" was an assumption too.
- **NOT TUNED — deliberately.** `STOP 0.10→0.03` is cheap and supported, but shipping it now means tuning a bar while the dominant term is unmeasured, and there is no `hard_stop` lever in the fleet_tuning registry (the supported path is the shadow twin via the experiment judge, which needs a registry addition — real-money-adjacent, operator's call). Agenda 17 updated.
- **Verification:** the bot's `_slip_bps_of` and the pinned mirror agree IDENTICALLY across 7 cases incl. every degenerate one; selftests green (shortfall/basis/immune/board); born-dark audit OK across 19 images; new `_fetch_order_slip` exercised against the REAL ledger (shadow: 158 orders / 79 with slip / 0.86bps / measurable=true; live: 48 orders / 0 with slip / measurable=false — exactly the blind spot, now visible instead of invented).
## 2026-07-17 (f) — COMMIT (a) of 2: the funding BASIS fix, fleet-wide and BEHAVIOUR-NEUTRAL (operator: "implement universally to all bots")
- **`funding_basis.py` — one authority for funding BASIS per venue.** A quoted `rate` is a fraction per FUNDING PERIOD, and the period differs by venue. Lighter quotes per **8 HOURS**; every Lighter consumer annualised it as HOURLY (`rate*24*365`), so every apr the fleet computed, gated on, logged and stored was **8x TRUE**. VERIFIED against the SETTLED series `/api/v1/fundings`: ETH predicted 9.6e-05 -> published 84.1%, settled 10.51%, **ratio exactly 8.00**; DOGE and SPY likewise (SPY's floor: 28.0% published -> **3.50% TRUE**). Arithmetic closes: `9.6e-05*3*365*100 = 10.512`. (Lighter SETTLES hourly at rate/8 — the quote is the 8h convention; both facts hold.)
- **NOT a global replace — that would have broken four CORRECT sites.** The fleet knows both conventions and gets them right everywhere except the one venue holding real money. UNTOUCHED: `funding_carry_bot` + `market_context` (Hyperliquid, genuinely hourly), `market_pulse` (Binance, already `rate_8h*3*365`), every `scripts/backtest_*` (HL). An unknown venue now RAISES rather than defaulting — a silent default is the bug.
- **Why it was never "just a label".** `FUNDING_ENTER_APR=0.40` was born in `funding_carry_bot.py` against HYPERLIQUID data — hourly, so `24*365` is CORRECT there and 0.40 meant a TRUE 40% apr. It was ported to `lighter_funding_bot.py` as a bare constant. **The PORT is the bug**: the LIVE book has admitted at **~5% TRUE** ever since, on a bar no backtest ever supported. Measured today: the gate admits **130 of 214 books — 61% of the venue** — for a rule meant to harvest extreme carry.
- **BEHAVIOUR-NEUTRAL, and proven so.** The conversion AND every apr-denominated threshold were divided by the same 8 in this ONE commit: `FUNDING_ENTER_APR` 0.40->0.05, `FUNDING_EXIT_APR` 0.15->0.01875, `SCOUT_DIV_GAP` 300->37.5, `TT_DIV_GAP` 500->62.5, `MOMO_AB_VETO_APR` 1.5->0.1875, the incubator's base + gene grids, the judge's candidate queue, the scout-tuner's widen ladder, and **the fleet_tuning registry CLAMPS** (`taker.div_gap_pp` 300/700->37.5/87.5, `xp`/`live.funding.enter_apr` 0.25/0.60->0.03125/0.075 — miss those and the corrected values get clamped back to nonsense). Verified against the LIVE cross-section: **entry AND exit decisions bit-identical on 214/214 books, same 130 admitted.** Same trades, honest labels.
- **The other half nobody looks at: the ACCRUAL.** Five bots modelled carry as `accrued -= rate * notional * dt_h` — right only if the quote is hourly, so **8x over-accrued** on Lighter (now `funding_basis.to_hourly`, which is exactly the venue's rate/8 hourly settlement). Live equity was always honest (the VENUE charges the real thing) — but the modelled figure reaches the **per-trade ledger AND the win/loss call**, so a book that COLLECTS carry had its win rate inflated. **The live Funding Farmer's 82% WR is therefore suspect and must be recomputed** before anyone cites it (its +$5.15 equity comes from `account_value` and stands).
- **Born-dark guard EARNED ITS KEEP AGAIN.** `import funding_basis` is unguarded, and `scripts/audit_image_imports.py` caught **6 images missing the COPY — two of them LIVE real money** (`Dockerfile.fundinglighter`, `.trendlighter`). Unfixed, both live bots would have crash-looped on boot. COPY added to all six; audit now OK across 19 images.
- **Two defects the tests caught in my own work:** a rewrite produced `round((x or 0, 'lighter'), 1)` — a tuple to `round()`, which COMPILES and only dies at runtime; and every old-unit test FIXTURE (scout divergence, taker, tuner ladder, judge candidates, tuning registry, incubator grid) had to be re-denominated or it silently inverted its own meaning — the scout's MOMO ticket, 50pp, would have started clearing a 37.5 bar it was written to fail. Fixtures must be denominated in the same units as the threshold they exercise. Mutation-tested.
- **`funding_basis` stamp on the tape now reads 1.0 + `corrected: 2026-07-17`** (was 8.0). The key STAYS at 1.0 deliberately: a 60-day join spans both epochs and the divisor is the only thing that makes pre-fix rows readable.
- **Verification:** 19/19 fleet selftests green (tuning/judge/incubator/tuner/taker/replay/immune/proprioception/regen/board/risk/brain_stats/brain_replay/respiration/clock/shortfall/sentinel/scout/basis), born-dark audit OK, bit-identical decision proof on the live venue.
- **COMMIT (b) IS NOT THIS COMMIT.** Re-tuning the gate is operator-gated and backtest-first — and the artifact it needs does not exist yet: **ZERO backtests have ever run on Lighter funding data** (every one loads Hyperliquid). Neither 40% nor 5% TRUE is established *on the venue holding the money*, and Lighter's economics differ anyway (zero perp fee). Next: `scripts/backtest_lighter_funding.py` on the ~31d of settled rates `/api/v1/fundings` serves retroactively, then optimise. Mixing (a) and (b) is how a reporting fix becomes an 8x live entry change.

- **THE ANSWER, and it is not the one the review was chasing.** The rule is NOT what failed: 86% of the loss that prompted this was a real market-wide crash (SNDK/MRVL/NBIS/MU printed -18.3/-18.7/-20.7/-10.1% on Yahoo over the same window) on **n=3 fills in 4 days**, well inside the strategy's own backtested envelope. - **The last hole closed — SIZING, the one lever ~10 tested designs never touched.** Drawdown scales **SUBLINEARLY** with deployment, so you cannot delever your way out: 90%->43.3% DD, 35%->25.5%, 20%->17.3%, and the **only** deployment clearing the gate is **15% ($30/slot) -> 6.7% CAGR / 13.9% maxDD = ~$67/yr** on the $1000 book. Strip the ~38% hindsight premium and it is **~$42/yr — at or BELOW risk-free cash, for a 14% drawdown**. Cutting size 6x only halves DD while cutting return 5x. (Harness calibrated against the workflow's control first: 37.9%/40.7% vs its 47.0%/43.0% — the first cut silently ran 1994-2026 off SPY's full grid and was thrown out.)

Retirement is a **code guard** (`MOMO_RETIRED`, default on) rather than `railway down`, because Railway auto-deploy RESURRECTS stopped services on git push (the perps-bot/momo-bot/triangular-arb trap). `SystemExit` re-raises through `_supervised()` so the container exits instead of crash-looping. **To un-retire: `MOMO_RETIRED=off` + drop the row from `RETIRED_ROWS`.**
- **What the epoch-2 A/B (c) was worth.** It ran ~1.5h and dies with the book — but fixing it was still right: v1 was reporting a number that pointed the OPPOSITE way to the truth, and an instrument that lies is worse than none. Its own postscript: `AB_VETO_APR` 1.5 = **18.75% TRUE**, ~3x tighter than breakeven — it vetoed affordable names and sat in cash. As configured it measured the wrong thing.

## 2026-07-17 (d) — the scout stores the CARRY cross-section — and doing it found that every funding APR the fleet prints is 8x TRUE (operator: "fix the funding cross-section first")
- **THE FINDING (real money, NOT fixed here): Lighter's `/funding-rates` `rate` is an 8-HOUR fraction, and the whole fleet annualises it as HOURLY (`rate*24*365`). Every APR the fleet computes, prints, gates on, and stores is exactly 8x TRUE.** Verified against the SETTLED series `/api/v1/fundings` (whose `rate` is %/hr): ETH predicted `9.6e-05` -> published **84.1%**, settled **10.51%**, ratio **exactly 8.00**; DOGE and SPY also exactly 8.00 (SPY's floor: published 28.0%, settled **3.50%**). The arithmetic closes to the digit: `9.6e-05*3*365*100 = 10.512`. Correct conversion is `rate*3*365*100` (3 periods/day). Compare only STABLE names — a spiking predicted rate against a 48h settled mean gives noisy ratios (MU 12.1, MSTR 26.5) and proves nothing. **The 8x is a MONOTONIC rescale**, so rankings, the extremes ordering, and cross-venue divergence (all rows share the basis) are UNAFFECTED; what is wrong is every ABSOLUTE apr a human reads and any threshold meant to express a TRUE apr. **Deliberately NOT corrected:** the same conversion denominates the LIVE Funding Farmer's `FUNDING_ENTER_APR=0.40` (really ~5% true), plus `AB_VETO_APR` 1.5 and `DIV_GAP_PP` — re-denominating is an 8x live entry-behaviour change and goes backtest-first with the operator (agenda item 16). Shadow books that accrue their own funding (`lighter_momentum_bot`/`lighter_index_bot`: `accrued -= rate*size*px*dt_h`) over-accrue 8x in their ledgers; the live bots are charged by the VENUE so their realized P&L is honest — only their DECISIONS use the inflated number.
- **`lighter-market` now persists `funding`: the FULL per-symbol APR cross-section over every ACTIVE book (~201), historized beside `marks`.** Until now the only funding this organ kept was the top-8 |extremes| — a VIEW, not the data. That gap is why 4 of 5 strands of the Stock Leaders review could not resolve: with no per-symbol history the crowding question had to run on ONE snapshot at n=25, where most names tie on the floor band and |rho|>0.398 is needed for p<0.05 — unresolvable by construction. Same argument that widened `marks` from liquid-only on 16-Jul, and deliberately NOT liquid-gated (a book can fall under the floor while still held — the exact bug that fix repaired) and NOT kill-switched (an env flag on a DATA key is a born-dark switch waiting to be flipped). Honest about scope: `/api/v1/fundings` already serves ~31d of settled rates retroactively; what only this key gives is depth past that window plus the PREDICTED rate as seen at the decision instant.
- **`funding_basis` stamp rides with the data** (`true_apr_divisor: 8.0`) so the 60-day tape is self-correcting instead of 60 days of 8x-wrong carry asserted as fact. Pinned in the selftest — and mutation-tested: dropping the stamp, wronging the divisor, emptying `funding`, or having history drop it are all CAUGHT.
- **`historized()` extracted** from main()'s inline dict-comp so the selftest pins the REAL history projection. This organ's cross-sections exist only to be joined to a decision after the fact, so a key silently dropped there is the difference between evidence and nothing. Behaviour-identical (verified by equality/JSON/key-order/fuzz).
- **COST, measured not estimated:** ~3.0 KB/snapshot raw JSON, +47% on a 6.3 KB history row, ~52 MB at the 60-day retention floor, on a DB reaching ~150 MB at that steady state regardless. Those raw figures are conservative — jsonb TOAST-compresses this key well (~43 distinct values across ~201 symbols): ~1.5 KB and ~32 MB on disk. Cadence confirmed at 288 rows/day.
- **Verification:** 4 adversarial lenses (consumer-safety / live-blast-radius / born-dark-and-ops / does-it-close-the-gap) + a final gate. Consumer safety proven by a differential harness — all 8 `lighter-market` consumers produce byte-identical output with and without the key; none iterates keys or validates a schema. Live blast radius ZERO, demonstrated: neither live bot reads `lighter-market` by any path, neither writes `bot_state_history`, and they ship in different images so share no connection. Not born-dark: already `COPY`d (Dockerfile.freqtrade:34), looped by run_all.sh:152, zero imports added, `audit_image_imports.py` OK. The `does-it-close-the-gap` lens is what caught the 8x basis — the change was structurally fine and would have shipped a tape of confidently wrong numbers.
- **Stock Leaders postscript.** With the basis corrected, its picks paid **~7.7% true APR** against a **~52% breakeven** — funding was never its problem, and the "adverse selection" read published in (c) is **refuted** (Spearman -0.174, p=0.40; sign-unstable; untestable at n=25). What stands is unchanged and needs no funding data: **maxDD 37-44% with zero carry vs a 15% go-live gate**. Note the epoch-2 A/B's `AB_VETO_APR` 1.5 = **18.75% true** — ~3x tighter than breakeven, so it vetoes affordable names and sits in cash; as configured it measures the wrong thing. Dislocation intensity at the bell is indistinguishable from any other moment on this venue. Kept honest as **"no support found", not "refuted"**: 3 sessions / n=36 in-window can't see an effect under ~1bps, and it measures intensity rather than the VALUE of acting in-window. ## 2026-07-17 (c) — 🏆 Stock Leaders' funding A/B was measuring the SEATBELT: epoch 2 makes the veto the only variable (operator: "constantly losing, review strategy and lets implement obvious changes")
- **The premise was checked first, and the RULE SURVIVES it — untouched.** The book is −$127.79 (−12.8%) on **3 closed trades in 4 days**, every one a catastrophic stop out of the single 13-Jul entry batch. Decomposed: **price ~−$110 (86%) / funding −$17.53 (14%)**. The price loss is not the rule's doing — SNDK/MRVL/NBIS/MU printed **−18.3% / −18.7% / −20.7% / −10.1% on Yahoo over the same window**: the marks were honest and the whole growth complex fell apart under it. Against this strategy's OWN backtested **44.3% maxDD**, a −12.8% week-one drawdown is well inside the envelope, and n=3 fills is not evidence — the same fills-not-hours lesson the incubator learned in (at) this week. Tuning a momentum rule on a 4-day momentum crash is the definition of overfitting; the obvious change was refused.
- **What IS structural: FUNDING ADVERSE SELECTION.** Live rates across the 25-name universe: 16 of 25 sit at Lighter's **28.0% baseline band** — but the rule's picks don't. **SNDK 967%, NBIS 687%, COIN 245%, MU 126%, HOOD 98% apr.** Ranking by 42d return ranks by CROWDEDNESS, and crowdedness *is* carry: 4 of 5 picks paid above baseline, two at 25–35× it. The justifying backtest (+43.7% CAGR / 44.3% DD, 15y, 10bps/switch) ran on **spot dailies and modelled no funding whatsoever**. Measured drag −$17.53 in 3.7d ≈ **2% of deployed capital**. Unlike the price P&L, this is a RATE measured across 5 names × continuous accrual — it converges far faster than 3 fills, and it is precisely the evidence this shadow book's docstring promised ("the shadow measures the real drag"). A +43.7% gross CAGR cannot pay a ~200% carry; even the 28% baseline eats most of it.
- **But the instrument built to measure that was measuring NOTHING.** The A/B funding-veto ledger differed from the real book in THREE variables at once: bootstrapped mid-cycle **at its own entry prices, in its own window**; carrying **no seatbelt** while the real book had one; and vetoing **only at rebalance**. It published `eq 829.30` against the real book's `872.21` — which reads as "the funding veto loses $43" — while `last_vetoed` sat **EMPTY: the veto had never once fired**. That $43 was the seatbelt. The 21-Jul review, the board, or a human would have drawn the exact opposite of the truth from it.
- **EPOCH 2 — the veto becomes the only variable.** The ledger is now SEEDED from the real book (identical legs/entries/accrual/marks plus a closed-book `ab_base`) so both ledgers start at the same equity **to the cent** — verified against REAL production state, delta `0.000000000000`. It MIRRORS the seatbelt and the rail flatten. The veto runs **CONTINUOUSLY**, because the spike is the entire cost: SNDK/NBIS entered affordable and only later printed 967%/687% (HOOD was observed moving **63% → 98% → 119% apr inside 20 minutes**) — an entry-only veto cannot see the carry that actually gets paid. Shared-leg entries inherit the real book's actual crossed fill and its mid-based sizing, so a 6bp spread can't masquerade as veto edge (verified `vs_real $0.00`). A veto-freed slot stays CASH to the next rebalance — back-filling mid-week would smuggle rotation cadence in as a second variable. Payload gains `epoch`/`vs_real`/`base`/`fund_paid`/`veto_exits`: `fund_paid` against the real book's `fund_realized+fund_open` attributes any edge to CARRY rather than price luck, and `veto_exits` explains a shorter `held` list that would otherwise reproduce the v1 mismatch. **Pre-epoch numbers are VOID.**
- **Two defects fixed en route.** (1) **Rebalance storm:** `(meta and not ab_meta)` gated the rotation — it re-fires EVERY loop for as long as the variant is flat while the real book holds, which is exactly what a fully-vetoed top-5 produces (SNDK 967% / NBIS 687% are one bad week from it). It would have silently demoted the validated WEEKLY rotation to ~6-hourly (bounded only by the Yahoo cache TTL) — and this bot's own sweep already found daily WORSE than weekly (maxDD 40.7 vs 36.9%, 176 vs 81 switches/yr). The one-time seed replaces it; the rebalance is the clock and nothing else. (2) **The daily-loss rail lost its BASELINE on every restart:** `day_start_equity` re-based to boot equity, so a redeploy part-way down a losing day re-anchored the 10% rail to the already-depressed number and it could no longer fire on that day's drawdown. The baseline now rides the persisted state (same UTC day) or the saved halt record. Same class as the 16-Jul `last_ts` fix.
- **LIVE-BOT RESIDUAL (standing audit rule — real money, NOT touched here):** `lighter_funding_bot` and `lighter_trend_bot` carry only the halt-record half of that rail fix, so they still re-base on a **PRE-halt** restart. Two adjacent suspicions were CLEARED in the same pass: `from_lighter` is identity, so the identically-printed rates (COIN/XAU 245.3, MU/CRCL 126.1) are Lighter's own discrete bands and not a symbol-map collision corrupting the live funding read; and the hourly-rate convention matches the live bot's `H = 24*365`. 21-Jul agenda item.
- **Verification:** epoch invariant against REAL production state (delta `0.000000000000`); 4 behaviour tests driving the SHIPPED `main()` through a fake venue (continuous veto exits a spiked leg; seatbelt mirrors both arms; no-veto/no-stop leaves the ledgers identical; storm trigger absent from executable code, checked against code lines not comments); shared-leg entry exactness at a 6bp crossed spread; live `--once` smoke against real Lighter mainnet. **The tests caught a real defect mid-build:** the first cut seeded AFTER the mark loop and so double-accrued one loop of funding — surfaced as a $0.01 epoch drift that had to be $0.00, fixed by seeding at the loop top. Ships on main; `perps-bot` (the recycled service that actually hosts Stock Leaders) verified by marker-grep per the trust-no-deploy-path rule.

## 2026-07-17 (au) — the incubator was breeding a bot that does not exist; the lens veto gets one authority; proprioception stops grading on zero trades
- **The incubator honors the brain's LENS VETO.** The replay is deliberately veto-blind ("external bus state isn't in this tape") — right for a pure harness, and each CONSUMER is expected to correct for it. The tuner does (never widens a brain-vetoed lens); the incubator did not. Measured live 16-Jul: the brain grades **breakout (n4h=2241, avg4h −0.184%, hit 40.5%), dip (n=2110, −0.438%, 42.6%) and momentum (n=1178, −0.233%, 41.0%) negative at sample size**, so the live taker vetoes all three and only **divergence (n=2463, +0.041%, hit 55.2%)** can fill. **Ten of the tape's eleven closes came from lenses the live bot refuses to trade.** Fitness now scores only fillable lenses (`live_lenses`/`fresh_lens_fwd`, fail-safe OPEN + freshness-gated, mirroring the taker's own direction); the entry gene of a vetoed lens is dropped (`evolvable_genes`) while pleiotropic exit genes stay; payload gains `lenses_scored`/`lenses_vetoed`. Verified on the real tape: veto-blind top net **−$16.68 over 9 closes** → veto-aware **+$3.56 over 1 close**. The organ's true live-relevant evidence base is ONE trade.
- **The veto rule had two hand-copies and was about to get a third.** The taker's inline loop and `lighter_scout_tuner.vetoed_lenses` each defined it; the incubator — which breeds the very bars the veto decides are worthless — would have added a third. Extracted to `lighter_ticket_taker.vetoed_lenses()` as the single authority (the taker IS the behaviour); the tuner delegates, same `TT_LENS_VETO_MIN_N` env, identical semantics.
- **`conform()` — crash fix on the upgrade path.** The gene set is now dynamic (`reachable_genes` drops holds, `evolvable_genes` drops vetoed lenses, `DIV_GAP_PP` was added) and `breed()` indexes `a[g]` for every `g in genes` — so the **currently published elite, bred before the divergence gene existed, would KeyError the whole organ on its next cycle**. Stored genotypes are projected onto the current gene set (missing → module default, unknown → dropped, an off-grid allele actually in force is kept).
- **`fleet_proprioception.grade_taker` stops grading on zero trades.** Observed live: a **3.13h / 37-snapshot** taker episode returned `stance_net 0.0 / default_net 0.0 / delta_usd 0.0` and was stamped **`"graded"`** — nothing closed on either arm, recorded as a measurement. The floors (`MIN_EP_H`=0.75 → 45 minutes, `MIN_EP_SNAPS`=8) count time and ticks against a strategy whose positions live for hours. An empty sample is UNGRADEABLE, not neutral — and neutral is not free: it spends an episode toward `MIN_EPISODES` and lets a verdict form out of nothing. New `MIN_EP_CLOSES` (4) floor denominated in CLOSED TRADES (one fill swings ~$3.50 > `HURT_USD`/`HELP_USD` $3.00, so a single trade can carry a verdict), taken on the better-evidenced arm; new `too-few-trades` status, which `lever_verdicts` already ignores. Also gains **IMB-10 marked scoring** (closed + unrealized) — the last replay consumer still blind to deferral. Restrict-only and fail-safe by the organ's own contract: an ungraded episode restricts nothing AND earns nothing.
- **Verification:** proprioception selftest fixture reworked to RESOLVE trades rather than merely span time (N dip symbols → N closes on the stance arm; the default arm takes none — that IS the counterfactual), plus the live episode's exact shape (long + tick-rich + resolves nothing → `too-few-trades`), a thin-but-nonzero sample refused, and `lever_verdicts` ignoring the new status. Incubator selftest pins the REAL 16-Jul grades resolving to `{divergence}`, fail-safe open on empty/stale/unparseable, a negative lens UNDER the floor not vetoed, entry-gene-only dropping, lens-restricted `_marked`/`_pnl_usd`, and `conform` round-tripping a pre-divergence elite through `breed()`. Green across proprioception/taker/tuner/incubator/replay/judge/immune/regen/tuning/board/brain_stats.
- **Still open (21-Jul agenda).** `grade_taker` is itself veto-BLIND — it grades `taker.*` levers on fills the live taker won't make, the identical defect one file over. Not fixed here: it would silence taker verdicts almost entirely (only divergence is fillable and no divergence lever exists today), which is a call for the review, not a drive-by.

## 2026-07-17 (at) — the incubator breeds on ELEVEN trades: selection moved to the gametes, gates re-denominated in fills (operator: "an egg needs good sperm and good eggs to fertilise to produce good offspring... let's think genes and DNA")
- **The measurement first.** The incubator's own fitness function, run against the real tape (15-Jul 04:27Z → 16-Jul 23:03Z, 42.9h): **11 closed trades, 10 of them the same stop-loss**, closed net −$14.89; breakout 5/5 SL, dip 3/3 SL, momentum 2/2 SL, divergence 1 TP. A ~90-genotype GA — crossover, mutation, elite carry, both-halves champion gates — was searching a landscape made of eleven fills. Every genotype in the population scores NEGATIVE (best −$14.08). The organ's bottleneck was never its genetics; it is that no fitness signal exists yet. Marks only publish since 15-Jul, so the tape is 2.5 days old and cannot be older.
- **The gates were denominated below one fill — and were hours from opening.** At clip $50 / TP +4% / SL −3%, one trade flipping SL→TP swings **$3.50**; `EDGE_MARGIN` was $2.00 and `HALF_MARGIN` $1.00, so a single lucky fill cleared every anti-overfit bar. They looked strict and were not. Worse, `MIN_TAPE_HOURS=48` was the ONLY thing holding the champion gate shut and the tape crossed 48h at **17-Jul 04:27Z (14:27 Sydney) — the same afternoon**; with `PERSIST_CYCLES=3` hourly cycles the organ would have published a "stable" champion backed by ~12 trades by evening. Now: `MIN_CLOSES` (40) gates on **closed trades, checked first** — hours are not evidence, a 200h tape with 11 closes passed the old floor and was still noise — and both margins are denominated in TRADE SWINGS (`INCUBATOR_EDGE_TRADES`/`HALF_TRADES`; an explicit `INCUBATOR_*_MARGIN` env still wins). Verified on the live tape: `TENTATIVE — 11 closed trades < 40 min`.
- **Selection ran at BIRTH, not at the gametes (the operator's actual point).** `assess_champion` is rigorous and was decorative: the breeding elite were `scored[:ELITE_N]` ranked by raw net, with `both_halves_pos` only breaking exact float ties — i.e. never. So the very +$2.35/h1=+$0.01 noise genotype the champion gate REJECTS still bred, and elite feed forward into the next cycle's population (`prior['elite']`), compounding noise across generations. The gate protected the REPORT; nothing protected the GENE POOL. New `select_elite` filters gametes on MEASURABLE (≥`MIN_GT_CLOSES`=12 closes — you cannot select a genotype you cannot measure; same "starving lens earns its grading diet" logic the tuner runs) AND ROBUST (both halves ≥ HALF_MARGIN — the birth gate moved upstream). On the live tape: **0/20 genotypes viable**, and the old net-max winner is now refused as a gamete. Champion and elite are deliberately decoupled — the fittest is still assessed+published so its reason explains WHY, instead of the card going quiet.
- **Ranking on the point estimate IS the winner's curse** — this file's header said exactly that about the champion in July, then ranked the breeding pool on the point estimate anyway. `rank` now sorts on a one-sided 90% **t lower confidence bound** on total net (`net_lcb`; std error of a sum = sd·√n; t table df 1..30 converging to `brain_stats.Z80`, the fleet's existing convention — the normal approximation is far too generous at n≈11). One trade evidences no edge, ever. Replay gains an ADDITIVE per-lens `pnl_usd` (per-trade net $) — the mean alone cannot price uncertainty; no existing field moved.
- **IMB-10 reconciled + finished.** (as) added deferral-proof marked scoring inside `evaluate` concurrently; kept, lifted to a module-level `_marked` mirroring the tuner's. The missing half: the **MAX_HOLD grid still offered 48h/72h alleles on a 42.9h tape** — both unreachable, and the real tape shows ZERO hold-exits, so with 10-of-11 closes being stop-losses "never close anything" was the strongest gradient in the landscape and the grid supplied the allele to express it. New `reachable_genes` drops unreachable holds before selection can reward them (mirrors the tuner's `_skipped_holds`; never strips a grid bare). Verified: `dropped [48.0, 72.0] -> grid [24.0]`.
- **The gene pool was long-only and excluded the only lens not losing.** `taker.div_gap_pp` is in the fleet_tuning registry (300–700), in the taker's `TUNABLE`, and in the tuner's ladders — but `TAKER_GENES` omitted it, so the fleet's only SHORT lens was unevolvable. Added, with a grid that also explores **tightening** (600/700): the tuner's ladder only ever widens (500→300), so nothing in the fleet searched that direction. Divergence is also the only lens with a non-negative tape result (+$2.10 — on n=1, which is itself the point). Dashboard genotype card gained the `div` label (it was silently omitting the allele).
- **Verification:** incubator + replay selftests grown (reachable-holds incl. never-strip-bare, `_marked` deferral case, `net_lcb` zero-spread/one-trade/more-evidence properties, `t90` table + Z80 convergence, gamete selection refusing one-half and 3-trade genotypes, the closes floor shadowing every later bar, one-fill-cannot-clear pinned against `TRADE_SWING`, every allele of every gene registry-legal). Green across replay/incubator/tuner/proprioception/taker/tuning/brain_stats; dashboard parses. Exercised end-to-end on the live tape read-only.
- **NOT fixed — needs a decision (21-Jul agenda).** `fleet_proprioception.grade_taker` has the SAME `closed_net` deferral blindness (lines 299-300) with `MIN_EP_H=0.75` — it grades taker levers on **45-minute** windows against a 48h-hold strategy — and `HURT_USD`/`HELP_USD` of $3.00 sit **below the $3.50 single-fill swing**. It is the retrospective authority that can override the tuner's prospective gates (HELPING unlocks the expansion walk before the brain's ruling floor; HURTING blocks re-assertion), so a deferral artifact there steers the growth rail. Shadow-lane only (the live lane grades per-trade vs baselines, a different path) — no real money, not on fire. Changing verdict semantics on that organ is the operator's call.

## 2026-07-16 (ar) — judge residuals: relative fade, per-half floors, landed-write guard + the immune application invariant (operator: "all 4 go today, assume control")
- **Relative + rolling fade bar (`fade_check`).** The promotion bar is RELATIVE (+0.5pp vs live) but the release bar was ABSOLUTE (mean < 0, cumulative since promotion): a lever that cut live from +0.80%/trade to +0.10% destroyed the edge without inverting the sign and could stay on real money forever — and the cumulative mean converges toward its early value, so a LATE fade became mathematically unreachable. Now: both bars run on the ROLLING last FADE_N closes, and a second release path fires when the recent mean falls ≥ MARGIN_PP below the live arm's own PRE-promotion baseline (the paired window's live mean, stamped into state at PROMOTE as `promote_baseline`). Restrict-only: an extra release path can only pull a lever OFF real money. Fail-safe: missing baseline (old promotions / regen restores) → absolute bar only, byte-identical to the historical behaviour.
- **Per-half sample floors (`paired_eval`).** The full-window floors (30/10) said nothing about the halves, so ONE live trade in a half set that half's entire baseline — the doctrine's central noise filter degenerated into a noise amplifier on the exact comparison that moves real money. Each half now requires shadow ≥ min_closes//2 and live ≥ max(3, live_min//2); an even at-floor split still clears, a lopsided one holds with `why="hX under-powered"`. Derives from the same XPJ_* env knobs.
- **A dropped rail write is not an assert (`_asserted`).** `write_levers` returns None when nothing valid survived (unknown lever / out-of-lane / clamp reject / no DB) and every judge call site discarded it: a silently-dropped write left the judge counting days toward promotion on an arm running env defaults, and could stamp phase=promoted + push "PROMOTED to LIVE" while NO live lever was in force. All four sites now verify the write landed: idle-start refuses to start (and a registry-impossible candidate is marked INVALID and skipped, spending no judge slot, instead of retrying forever); running-re-assert failure HOLDS with the same semantics as ARM SKEW (no promote, no ABANDON aging, clock restarts on recovery, one urgent push per episode via sticky `assert_fail_notified`); a failed PROMOTE write stays RUNNING and retries — the promotion IS the write; a failed promoted-phase re-assert warns once (the TTL expiry to env defaults is already the fail-safe, but it must not be silent while the judge reports "promotion in force").
- **Immune application invariant (`application_sickness`).** The organ whose remit is alive-but-sick could see a lever whose VALUE is impossible but not one whose value is IGNORED — the fleet's largest fresh-but-wrong signal. New invariant on the receipt lanes only (xp.funding.*/live.funding.*, where the arms stamp extra.bars from inside apply_levers — unforgeable by a lever-blind image): a lever in force whose consumer closed ≥2 trades since first-seen (+15min loop grace, tracked in the organ's own `app_seen` map — the tuning payload carries no set-time) with ZERO matching receipts → SICK "enacted but not applied" → sick-list + phone. Deliberately NOT a quarantine: quarantining a lever the consumer provably ignores reverts nothing (that IS the sickness) and would feedback-loop on a healthy arm (quarantine → consumer runs defaults → receipts stop matching → sick forever). Fail-safe: zero closes = no evidence; dark ledger/tuning = nothing flagged.
- **Verification:** judge selftest grows to 12 pinned scenarios (all 9 prior unchanged + per-half floors, relative+rolling fade incl. the cumulative-miss case, asserted-write guard); immune selftest pins the invariant (first-sighting starts the clock, receiptless→sick, matching→healthy, wrong-value receipts→sick — the deaf-arm signature, floor, grace, non-receipt lanes never judged, expired levers forgotten). fleet_tuning + fleet_regen selftests green (payload shape additive).
## 2026-07-17 (as) — the balance audit's VERIFIED-REAL findings ship: 10 fixes across risk, judge, tuner, scanners + the live entry path (operator: "continue")
- **IMB-02 (dd governor, feeds the real-money up-gate).** A carried base now keeps its REAL venue in the cohort key (new `equity_venue` payload field) — one stale-flap of the hourly Tide Rider publisher no longer wipes the 7d dd sample series. And `dd_governor` ABSTAINS (dd=None) until the window spans `FLEET_DD_MIN_SPAN_SEC` (6h): a freshly-reset window used to hand the board's deliberately fail-closed dd leg a passing 0.0 — the erasure always landed on the RELEASE side, snapping clips 0.25→1.0 mid-drawdown.
- **IMB-03 + IMB-17 (LIVE entry path, rides next gate0 ff).** The coin-quality veto — the one consumed payload on the live-money entry path with NO freshness gate — now ages honestly: publisher stamps `updated+ttl_sec`, consumer discards a stale/unstamped set with a one-shot warning (fail-OPEN, its documented direction; legacy `ts` + env fallback cover the deploy-ordering window). And the live Funding Farmer finally READS the L2 light it was counted into: fresh + enforce + budget full → skip NEW directional longs only (shorts/exits untouched — the short budget's non-enforcement is deliberate, IMB-05 refuted); mirrors the trend bot's block; `FLEET_RISK_MODE=advisory` kill switch respected.
- **IMB-04.** `pulse_panic` in the family bot + all 4 freqtrade strategies now gates on the payload's own `updated+ttl_sec` — a fossil panic flag from a dead market_pulse kept halving every new entry forever (the 39h-fossil class, on an input the immune organ doesn't scan).
- **IMB-07 (judge; -06/-22 landed independently in (ar) above — reconciled, their richer relative-fade + env-derived floors kept).** The done-list AGES so the finite candidate universe can't permanently self-exhaust — but as FALLBACK-only retry (`pick_candidate`, pure + selftested): untried candidates always win, an aged ABANDONED/FADED/INVALID entry re-runs only when nothing untried remains (`XPJ_DONE_RETRY_D` 28d; legacy names stamped on migration; `done_at` persisted and stamped at all three verdict sites).
- **IMB-12.** `gapscout.extra_exchanges` hot-adds now REVERT: the venue set derives each scan from base EXCHANGES + the lever's current value, dropped venues prune `exchanges`/`markets_by_ex` and rebuild the symbol map — this was the one growth-rail lever whose promised TTL auto-revert was unreachable (one-way-until-redeploy).
- **IMB-10 + IMB-23.** Every tuner replay gate scores `closed_net + unrealized` (survivors marked at last tape prices) — a variant can no longer win a gate by deferring losses past the tape's end — and SWEEP_HOLD values the tape can't even reach are excluded from the grid; the incubator's funding baseline derives from the bot's own env names instead of hard-coded literals (an operator env drift no longer makes a no-op allele look like an experiment), dead `get_lever` dict removed.
- **The 21-refuter adversarial verify (3 lenses per fix) caught TWO real defects in this very tranche before it shipped — both repaired in the same commit:** (1) my coin-vetoes `ttl_sec=3600` was 6x SHORTER than the publisher's real 6-hourly quality cadence — on a perfectly healthy system the live veto would have been discarded ~83% of steady-state time (a tighten reflex weakened by its own freshness fix, plus a cry-wolf warning every 6h); repaired per the refuter's preferred shape: market_context re-stamps the payload's `updated` EVERY 5-min loop as a liveness attest (content still changes 6-hourly by design), so ttl 3600 genuinely means "12 missed loops = dead publisher"; the consumer's warn-once flag now re-arms on ANY fresh payload. (2) plain done-list aging would have STARVED incubator offspring permanently — the 3 statics sit ahead of offspring in pool order and rotate (~48d) slower than the 28d retry window, so an aged static would be re-selected ahead of every never-tried offspring forever; repaired as fallback-only retry (see the IMB-07 bullet above). Merge note: this tranche landed concurrently with (ar)'s independent judge work — the judge file was reconciled onto (ar)'s richer relative-fade/floors/landed-guard implementation with only the IMB-07 aging grafted on top; judge selftest green with both suites' cases. Also applied from the amendments: `_marked`/`_tape_span_h` regression pins in the tuner selftest, the incubator's bred-champion scoring gets the same deferral-proof marked net (dashboard-only today; a future consumer inherits no bias), and the dd-governor comment now records the deliberate ≤6h post-reset tighten-blind window (shadow-clip lane only) for the 21-Jul review. G1/G3/G4/G6/G7 survived all lenses outright.

- **The adversarial verify finished (131 agents, 3 lenses per target).** Fixes F1-F2/F4-F8 survive 3/3. **F3 was REFUTED 2/3 and repaired the same evening:** the rail authors' unlocked fallback ignored `save_state`'s False — on a reads-OK/writes-FAILING DB (Postgres flipped read-only, conn reset between read and write) `write_levers`/`release_levers` returned a truthy payload that NEVER persisted, so the board's `live_write_ok` guard false-positived and the URGENT "LIVE clips x0.75" push could still claim a restriction that didn't land (one refuter reproduced it offline with a store stub). Both authors now honor the documented contract — None on a failed durable write — and the selftest pins the degraded-store case. Board-side guard logic unchanged (it was correct; its oracle was lying).

## 2026-07-16 (aq) — the XP judge was grading an arm that cannot hear it: fail-closed on ARM SKEW (operator: "continue fixes")
- **The defect, on the one path that writes REAL MONEY.** `experiment_judge` was asserting `xp.funding.enter_apr=0.30` at a shadow arm with **no lever-reading code at all** — verified on the running service: `funding-farmer-shadow` → `apply_levers 0 · fleet_tuning 0 · get_lever 0`, and `fleet_tuning.py` → *No such file or directory*. Its gate is `ENTER_APR = os.environ.get("FUNDING_ENTER_APR", "0.40")` with the env **unset**, so it runs **0.40 — the same value the live arm runs**. The paired bar was therefore scoring 07-11-code-at-0.40 against 07-16-code-at-0.40 and attributing the residual (version skew + mark-vs-real fills) to a candidate that was never applied. A PROMOTE writes `live.funding.*`, which the live arm DOES consume. Ledger proof: `perps-funding-lighter-lshadow` = **30 closes, 0 receipts**.
- **Nothing was promoted** (`promoted_ts` null; floors at shadow 1/30, live 0/10) — a live defect caught before it cost anything, not a loss. The incubator had **6 more candidates queued** onto the same arm, each worth a 7–14d judge slot.
- **The missing invariant: nothing verified enactment→APPLICATION.** Every gate validated the DECISION to write a lever (registry clamp, lane binding, AUTHOR_LANES, TTL, paired bar, proprioception episodes); not one validated that a bot ever READ it. `blind_cycles` only catches a DARK ledger ("I can't see the data"), never a lying one ("the rows I see don't reflect my experiment") — precisely the alive-but-sick class `fleet_immune` exists for, which the judge sits outside.
- **The receipt already existed and had ZERO readers.** `lighter_funding_bot.py:493` stamps `extra={"bars": dict(_ACTIVE_BARS)}`, and `_ACTIVE_BARS` is populated **only inside `apply_levers()`** — a lever-blind image structurally *cannot* forge it, which makes a missing receipt **disproof, not silence**. `experiment_judge.py:22` has claimed *"Every close row carries extra.bars"* since 15-Jul; it was never wired. `fetch_paper_trades` never even SELECTed the column.
- **Fix (restrict-only — the gate can only ever BLOCK a promotion, never enable one):** `bot_pnl_store.fetch_paper_trades` now selects `extra` and passes it through (additive; `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS extra JSONB` runs in the same function before the SELECT, so all 7 consumers stay safe). New `ran_candidate(row, levers)` proof predicate. `paired_eval(cand_levers=…)` counts a SHADOW close only when its receipt matches the candidate — the **LIVE arm is deliberately NOT gated**: it is the control running env defaults and only 1 of its 14 rows carries a stamp, so gating it would starve the baseline and freeze the pipeline for good.
- **ARM SKEW is a distinct state, not a false negative.** Closes in-window but zero attributed → HOLD + one urgent push, and explicitly do NOT age toward `ABANDONED` (which would permanently retire a possibly-good candidate on a verdict about an experiment that never ran — `enter-gate-0.30` is the 11-Jul review's own item). On recovery the clock RESTARTS, so days accrued while deaf can't instantly trip `MAX_DAYS`. A stuck, loud queue beats a phantom promotion.
- **Verification:** the 8 existing judge selftest scenarios pass unchanged; new permanent cases pin that the OLD bar *promotes* on unattributed rows (`"shadow beats live by 4.90pp/trade over the window AND both halves"`) where the gated bar blocks, that an APPLYING arm still clears (not a brick wall), that a wrong-value receipt (0.4 vs candidate 0.3) is not proof, and that `cand_levers=None` is byte-identical to the historical bar. Found by a multi-agent adversarial audit; two of its findings were REFUTED by the verify pass and dropped (prop_fade is *not* inert — proprioception slices held stances every 24h; and the `extra.bars` evidence is *not* already in the DB — 1 of 14 live rows).
