- **THE IMBALANCE, measured then removed (LIVE lane, `evidence_board.synthesize_live`).** Tighten had five reachable reflexes; expand had ZERO reachable ones. DB ground truth: the up-ladder required EVERY live row ≥30 LIFETIME closes AND lifetime-positive — Tide Rider is a position HOLDER (**0 closes ever, by design**; the judge excludes it for the same reason) and the Funding Farmer sat lifetime −$0.30 despite a +$2.38 measured week. Lifetime anchors also made the down reflex a RATCHET (a healed book stayed "hurt" forever). Both directions now anchor on the SAME 7d realized ledger window (new read-only `bot_pnl_store.fetch_realized_window`): TIGHTEN = 7d ≤ −$10, lifetime ≤ −$20 backstop, dark-window fallback = the OLD lifetime rule (tighten never weakens on missing data); EXPAND = whole cohort fresh + non-harming (lifetime bleed ≥ −$1) AND ≥1 row PROVES the edge (≥30 lifetime closes, ≥5 window closes, positive week) — dark window fails CLOSED. The roles split honestly: the active trader proves edge, the structural holder proves non-harm. Verified read-only against the live DB: the gate is now REACHABLE (~2 weeks of normal funding-bot trading) but not satisfied today (light red + 14/30 closes) — earned, not gifted. All other gates unchanged (green light, shallow dd, calm venue, 24h cooldown, top-step-needs-measured-HELPING, hurting-release, divergence reflex, frozen-row fail-closed).

Both sides now share `LENS_FRESH_S`; `inputs_fresh.lens_forward` reports real freshness (was `bool(lf)` presence).
- **The live lever's honesty + leash.** A lapse/hurting release is now an EXPLICIT WITHDRAWAL — new `fleet_tuning.release_levers` (author can only release its OWN levers; locked read-modify-write) removes the lever instantly instead of waiting up to 2h for TTL while the phone claimed "back to x1.0" — a true removal, not a 1.0 overwrite, so proprioception sees a clean `released` episode end instead of a phantom no-op stance it would have to grade; the URGENT push and the ladder/cooldown memory only advance when the rail write actually LANDED (failed write = log + retry, never a false "protection applied" push on the money channel); the live lever carries per-entry ttl 1800s (a dead board reverts real clips in ~30min, not 2h) via new `fleet_tuning.write_levers` per-entry `ttl_sec` that can only SHORTEN the call-level TTL; after any release the up-ladder waits 1h before re-asserting (anti-flap — a gate oscillating at threshold can't strobe URGENT pushes); the growth-rail step push lists only the board's own gapscout levers (was the whole merged dict incl. other authors' lanes).
- **Notification memory:** full `seen {key: first_seen}` map persisted (prior payloads only kept the top-20 DISPLAYED items — anything ranked 21+ lost first_seen and re-notified as "new" after the gap); `synthesize()` dropped its unused `prior_keys` param and its docstring no longer claims condition-ENTER firing it never had.

(c) LENS_FRESH_S now shared by both board callsites (the expand side had it hardcoded). Plus: growth-step memory only advances on a landed write.
- **Fleet-wide balance findings beyond the board (audit output):** 37 imbalances catalogued — 7 shipped now: the three above + brain `urgent` flag re-earned every run (a sticky flag from a v3 run kept fast-pathing straight through the `BRAIN_MULT_ENGINE=v2` kill switch — one line, brain_replay still PASSES), proprioception now observes only consumer-FOLLOWED levers (`observed_active`: quarantined / live-hurting levers stop generating episodes — a hurting verdict could previously heal itself on default-arm trades it mislabeled as scaled, then resume steering real money), and the live slot budget anchors to the operator's env clip (floor(cap/scaled_clip) meant a DOWN-scale minted position slots — a tighten action expanding a budget; venues/, needs the next gate0 ff to reach live services). ## 2026-07-16 (af) — dashboard: the EXPERIMENT bots now show their go-live gates (operator: "the experiment bots dont show go live metrics")
- **Root cause: the render suppressed them.** `render()` only emitted gate chips `if stg in ("ready","proving")`, so every `EXPERIMENT_BASES` book — Ticket Taker, **Snap Back**, Perp Sniper, Launch Sniper — displayed NO progress-to-live. Snap Back is the STANDING next go-live candidate (memory: lighter-go-live-decision), and you couldn't see how far off it was. `fetch_gate_metrics` was fine (it already unions both ledgers); the data existed and was simply never rendered.
- **Fix:** `("ready","proving","experiment")`. Stage classification is deliberately UNCHANGED — experiment books stay in the experiment section and can never auto-promote to "ready" on gates alone; this only makes their distance from the bar visible. `control`/`scanner` stay off by design (a reference book isn't on the promotion track).
- **Verified against the live DB first** (in-network), then rendered offline against each bot's REAL metrics: Snap Back `1/30 trades ✗ · WR 100% ✓ · dd -2.0% ✓ · age 5/30d ✗`; Ticket Taker `8/30 ✗ · WR 62% ✓ · dd -1% ✓ · age 2/30d ✗`; Perp Sniper `0/30 ✗ · age 6/30d ✗`; Launch Sniper `356/30 ✓ · WR 11% ✗`. All four have the `bot_equity_history` the dd/age gates need. Empty/dark bot renders all-✗ without crashing.
- **Also hardened `fetch_gate_metrics`**: its paper_trades leg did NOT exclude `side='skip'` / NULL pnl. Today those land under a separate id (`event-listing-sniper-skips`, 545 rows) so it only dropped a phantom gate row — but had skips ever been written under a real bot id they'd have silently inflated n30 and cratered its win rate. Same guard as fetch_bot_quality now.
- **Honest gap surfaced, not hidden:** Launch Sniper shows `dd —` / `age —` because its `bot_equity_history` rows carry NULL equity (the gate filters `equity IS NOT NULL`). A publisher-side gap, visible rather than faked.
## 2026-07-16 (ae) — dashboard: ALL bots (live + shadow) now share the same metrics (operator: "some bots have metrics others don't")
- **Root cause: two ledgers, one read.** `fetch_bot_quality()` computed win rate / profit factor / expectancy / last-close / 24h-count / per-mode breakdown `FROM bot_trades` on every query — the freqtrade era only. Every Lighter bot and scanner records to `paper_trades` and rendered BLANK. Worst of it: **both LIVE real-money rows write to paper_trades**, so the two books with actual money showed the LEAST detail while retired Kraken paper rows showed full stats.
- **Unified in SQL** (`UNION ALL` → `GROUP BY bot`), never client-side — `wr`/`pf`/`exp` are RATIOS, recomputable only from summed gross-win/gross-loss. Ledger differences handled: `pnl_abs` vs `profit_abs`, `closed_at`(TEXT, `pg_input_is_valid`-guarded) vs `close_ts`, `is_open IS NOT TRUE` (the column is nullable — `= FALSE` silently dropped closed-by-omission rows), and paper_trades' `side='skip'` rows (the sniper's gate-rejection LOGS, not trades) which would have poisoned every metric. `enter_tag` derived from `reason` mirroring `split_reason` — NOT the `tag` column (Gap Scout writes a venue ROUTE there). Keys stay fully-suffixed row ids: normalizing would merge a live book with its shadow twin. `COUNT(DISTINCT src)` logs any bot appearing in both ledgers (none today) — a card blending dead Kraken history with a live Lighter book is the one merge failure that looks plausible and is wrong. Output shape byte-identical, so no consumer breaks.
- **VALIDATED against the live DB before deploy** (in-network via `railway ssh`): **24 bots now carry metrics, up from ~7**. `perps-funding-lighter-lighter` (LIVE Funding Farmer) goes blank → `n=14 wr=86% pnl=+2.38`. Ticket Taker's lens tags resolve correctly (`long-breakout`, `short-divergence`, `long-dip`). `src=1` for every bot (no blending).
- **Two latent bugs fixed in the same change, because unification ARMS them:** (1) the `STOCKS` health-check skip compared BASE names against venue-SUFFIXED rows so it never matched — now `venue_variant(b)[0] in STOCKS`; (2) the flat `n24 > 15` over-trading bar was written for the slow freqtrade books — Launch Sniper closes **19/day by design** and would have banner-spammed every load. Now `OVERTRADE_LIMIT` per bot CLASS (Snap Back/snipers 60, Ticket Taker/Funding Farmer/Counterweight 40, default 15).
- **Honest remaining gaps (not papered over):** Tide Rider (`crypto-trend-daily-lighter`) shows blank because it has **zero closed trades** — 0 ledger rows, verified; it populates on its first close. Launch Sniper + Yield Harvester show `↳ ?` tags because their publishers write a bare `reason` with no entry attribution — a PUBLISHER fix (prefix `long_`), deliberately NOT fixed by loosening the parser (that regex gate cost the brain 92 wasted runs). Gap Scout renders on its optimistic-fill basis — not comparable to the rest of the fleet.

Answer to the question first: every bug FOUND in the 14–16 Jul sessions was already fixed and deployed (verified in code + gate0 at main HEAD + all 18 then-existing selftests green). But six fresh adversarial audits (live-money, 15-Jul organs, core intelligence, scanners, shadow bots, dashboard/watchdog) found a NEW layer. Now `_flatten_all("daily_loss")` retries every halted loop (idempotent once flat; skipped when the kill switch just flattened).

Board `synthesize_live`: up-scale now requires FRESH live rows (`updated_at` ≤ 2h10m — a frozen row's lifetime-positive pnl kept earning up-scales) and `_fresh` honors each payload's OWN `ttl_sec` (a 40-min-stale fleet-risk with ttl 900 gated real money through the fixed 2700s window). - **MEDIUM — the ~18h divergence blind window.** The board trusts a `live-shadow-gap` alert for 6h but market_context's 24h dedup only re-stamped `ts` daily: a PERSISTING divergence had the down-scale reflex blind ~18h of every 24. `_alert` now refreshes `last_seen` (+msg) on every dedup-hit; board + immune age off `max(ts, last_seen)` (a still-confirmed alert is not a fossil).

## 2026-07-16 (ah) — 🦾 the LIVE lane LEARNS: paired episode grading + restrict-first consumption on real money (operator: "The live lane needs to learn")
- **(af)/(ag) left the live lane record-only** — episodes were kept for the review but produced no verdicts and nothing consumed them. - **Grading (`grade_live` v2):** a live episode is graded on per-trade `pnl_pct` (size-invariant — the judge's own lesson) against TWO baselines: the books' own pre-episode window AND the shadow twins over the SAME window (identical signals; the only difference is the lever + real fills). `bad` requires being worse than EVERY available baseline by ≥0.25pp (`PROP_LIVE_MARGIN_PP`) — real money is never blamed on one noisy comparison; `good` is symmetric; thin data (n<5 during, no usable baseline) stays `recorded`. **Attribution split by author:** `live.clip_scale` (board) and `live.funding.*` (judge) now grade as separate groups — the board's movement is never blamed on the judge's. Verdicts need ≥2 same-direction episodes (`basis: live-paired`).

## 2026-07-16 (af) — 🦾 PROPRIOCEPTION: the autonomy stack grades its OWN enactments (operator: "advance, enhance and improve the autonomous organ")
- **The gap:** every autonomous actuator was gated PROSPECTIVELY — the scout tuner replays the tape that MOTIVATED a widening (in-sample), the board widens on census quiet-hours, the judge runs a paired bar. Once a lever was enacted, NOTHING looked back: no organ measured whether the fleet actually did better during the hours a lever was in force than it would have on the operator's defaults. The growth rail could act; it could not feel the consequence of its own action (the 15-Jul cap breach was only found by a human audit). Proprioception closes the act → sense → adjust loop.
- **NEW `fleet_proprioception.py`** (freqtrade-bots container, ~15 min): tracks lever EPISODES per lane-group stance (all taker.* bars = one joint stance — they replay together so they grade together; each scout.* diet lever its own; gapscout.* one; live.*/xp.* record-only). A stance change/expiry/release closes the episode (release END is backdated to the lever's own `expires` so a dead author doesn't inflate the window); stances held >24h are SLICED daily so grades accrue. Grades are OUT-OF-SAMPLE by construction (the deciding organ tuned on tape BEFORE the episode; the grade replays the tape recorded DURING it): **taker** = the true counterfactual $ (env defaults vs episode bars through the taker's real code via `lighter_ticket_replay`), **scout** = grading throughput (lens n4h delta — the diet levers exist to buy grades), **gapscout** = census activity (quiet_hours reset inside the window), **live/xp** = RECORDED ONLY — the judge's paired bar + fade-watch stay the sole real-money authority. Per-lever verdicts helping/hurting/neutral with anti-noise floors (n≥2 graded episodes AND ±$3; episodes under 45 min or 8 snapshots refuse to grade; joint stances share blame — conservative in the restrict direction; HURTING exists only on the taker lane, the one lane with a $ counterfactual).
- **First consumer (restrict-only — the always-allowed direction): the scout tuner.** `apply_proprioception` drops any would-be enactment whose lever carries a fresh HURTING verdict — the tuner stops repeating a movement that measured net-negative in reality, even while in-sample replay still likes it. Fail-safe: a dark/stale/absent proprioception drops NOTHING (a dead sense must not paralyze the body; levers stay bounded + TTL'd regardless). Expand-side consumption (helping earns anything) deliberately UNWIRED until the 21-Jul review rules — agenda item 12 written with the grading checklist.
- **Surfaced everywhere the operator triages:** evidence board 🦾 items (`synthesize_proprioception`: hurting = warn/restrict + names the auto-skip, helping = expand evidence, stale organ emits nothing), immune organ invariants (episode end<start, unknown verdict values — sickness that would mislead the tuner's skip), dashboard /vitals organ row + 🤖 Autonomy card "Outcomes" row + honest CONTRACTS row.
- **Verified:** new organ selftest (episode lifecycle incl. backdated release + daily slice, replay counterfactual win/lose/too-short, scout throughput, gapscout activity, live record-only, verdict floors + joint blame, fail-safe consumer hook) + tuner/board/immune selftests extended and green + dashboard card/vital smoke-tested crash-safe against realistic/empty/no-counts/malformed/stale payloads. **Doctrine intact:** no new expand authority, no live-money surface change (the live bots' code is untouched; live/xp lanes are record-only here), publishes-then-earns like every organ before it.
## 2026-07-16 (ae) — 🕐 fleet clock v2: market OPEN/CLOSE events (operator: "when any market opens or closes we are able to allocate resources")
- **The circadian organ now knows EVENTS, not just zones.** v1 published fuzzy crypto liquidity sessions (asia/eu/us/off + thin/weekend); nothing in the fleet knew that NYSE — the market behind the Index Rider 📊 / Stock Leaders 🏆 equity perps — opens and closes, or WHEN. Their underlyings only print during NYSE regular hours; the perps trade 24/7 against a frozen index the rest of the time, and no organ could time resource allocation around any open/close.
- **NEW in `fleet-clock`:** (i) `markets.nyse` — a real calendar: zoneinfo DST (13:30 vs 14:30 UTC opens handled), 2026-27 holidays incl. observed dates (3-Jul-26, 24-Dec-27…), 13:00-ET early closes (27-Nov, 24-Dec-26), `calendar_ok=false` degrade on unknown years, sessions-only degrade if tzdata is missing; (ii) `events` / `next_event` — the next open+close of EVERY market (NYSE + the three crypto sessions) with absolute `at_utc` (authoritative; the sampled `in_min` is display-only); (iii) `event_window` (±15 min pre/post, env `CLOCK_EVENT_PRE/POST_MIN`) with `window_events` naming what's opening/closing; (iv) **`heavy_ok` is now event-aware** — thin AND outside every event window (an open/close is exactly when resources belong on the market, not the sweep); (v) history writes on TRANSITIONS only (session change / thin flip / NYSE open↔close) — `fleet-clock` history is now an open/close event log instead of a 30-min heartbeat.
- **Cadence 30 min → 5 min** (`CLOCK_INTERVAL_SEC:-300`, ttl 900) so sampled window flags stay honest. Selftest extended: DST both sides, holiday + Monday reopen, early close, weekend, event ordering, ±window membership, thin+window→heavy_ok=false, 2028 calendar degrade — plus every v1 assert unchanged (existing consumers: none; dashboard renders old and new payloads).
- **Dashboard:** Autonomy-card clock row + /vitals now show NYSE open/closed + the next event with live relative time (`nyse open in 25m`), smoke-tested against rich/v1/empty payloads; honest CONTRACTS row added (published, unconsumed). **Doctrine intact:** advisory/publish-first — nobody is wired to obey it; candidate first consumers (equities books idle while NYSE closed · scanner cadence boost in event windows · heavy jobs on heavy_ok) queued as **agenda item 11** for the 21-Jul review.

## 2026-07-16 (ac) — HOTFIX: experiment judge crashed every cycle since the name-based refactor (surfaced within minutes by the new dashboard visibility)
- **The new /vitals surfacing immediately paid off:** the moment the growth-rail organs became visible, `xp-judge` showed DARK (>9h stale) while its sibling loops were FRESH. Investigation found it CRASHING every cycle with `KeyError: 'levers'` at `run_once` line 291.
- **Root cause:** the 15-Jul name-based candidate refactor (commit a214606) didn't handle MIGRATION from the old index-based state. The live `xp-judge` state was written by the old code (`cand_idx`, no `current`/`spec`), so post-refactor `spec={}` while `phase="running"`, and `cand = spec if spec.get("name")==current` collapsed to `{}` → `cand["levers"]` threw. The judge's run_once path was NOT covered by the selftest (only its pure functions were) — exactly the gap I flagged when refactoring, and the reason the audit rule (verify runtime, don't trust selftests) exists.
- **Fix:** new pure `_needs_reset(phase, current, spec)` guard — a running/promoted phase with no valid spec resets to idle and re-selects from the pool cleanly instead of KeyError. Selftested (the exact crash case + mismatch + valid + idle). The judge recovers on next deploy: resets to idle, re-starts candidate `enter-gate-0.30` with the proper spec format.
- **No real money touched:** a dead judge wrote no `live.funding.*` levers, so the live bots ran on env defaults throughout (safe). Ships main → gate0 ff.

## 2026-07-16 (aa) — AUDIT FIXES: a CRITICAL real-money notional-cap breach + 5 more (two parallel adversarial code reviews of the 15-Jul organs)
Two subagents ran a correctness bug-scan + an integration-synchronicity check over everything built 15-Jul. Synchronicity came back CLEAN (21 levers, one author each, no collisions, no dead reads, deploy wiring complete, immune-quarantine loop correct). The bug-scan found one critical defect and five lesser ones — all fixed here.
- **CRITICAL — live notional-cap breach (`lighter_funding_bot.py`, `lighter_trend_bot.py`).** The entry gate estimated open exposure as `open_now * order_usd` — correct only when every position was opened at the CURRENT clip. The 15-Jul live growth-rail made the clip variable: a `live.clip_scale` DOWN-scale (0.75) shrinks `order_usd`, which RAISES `max_open=floor(cap/clip)`, and with held positions still sized at the larger clip the understated estimate let a new entry breach the operator's hard notional cap (worked example: cap $150, 5×$30 held = at cap, down-scale → a 6th $22.50 entry allowed → $172.50, 15% over; up-then-down reaches ~2×). Ironically triggered by the risk-OFF down-scale, and it contradicted the "cannot add exposure" claim made when the live lane shipped. **Fix:** new `_open_notional()` measures REAL deployed notional — each held position at its OWN clip (`meta['clip']`, or `size*entry` fallback) + this loop's opens at the current clip. Unit-tested against the exact breach scenario on both bots (now correctly blocks the 6th).
- **MEDIUM — `pnl_pct` on the wrong clip (`lighter_funding_bot.py`).** `pnl_pct = pnl / order_usd` used the CLOSE-time clip; a position opened at $30 and closed after an up-scale to $37.50 reported per-trade return understated ~20% — and only the LIVE arm scales, so it skewed the shadow→live promotion judge (and the shortfall tracker). **Fix:** the entry clip is now stamped in `meta['clip']` and passed to `_record_close` at all close sites (both bots).
- **LOW ×3:** (`fleet_regen.py`) `is_clean` reused `immune.organ_invariants`, which freshness-gates — so an AGED history snapshot's corrupt content was never inspected, defanging tier-2 self-repair in its own 3–24h window; now it stamps a fresh `updated` for the content probe (tested: aged `phase=haywire` now detected). (`evidence_board.py`) the live UP-scale gate fail-OPENED on a missing `fleet_dd_7d`; now fails CLOSED (real-money default). (`bot_pnl_store.py`) `fetch_bot_pnl` omitted `pnl_pct`, so the immune organ's NaN/absurd-`pnl_pct` sickness check silently never fired on real rows; column added.
- **ACKNOWLEDGED, not fixed (LOW):** the immune `fleet-alerts` prune races `market_context`'s append; harm is a dropped fresh alert that re-fires next cycle (no data loss, both writers use the identical shape). Left as-is with its owning comment — a proper atomic-append fix exceeds the harm. Also low/by-design from the sync audit: `evidence-review` is operator/scheduler-authored (no in-repo writer); `-lshadow` Dockerfiles omit `fleet_tuning.py` (safe — live-only gate; a footgun ONLY if a shadow were promoted to live without adding the COPY).
Ships main → gate0 ff (both live bots changed — live restart round).
## 2026-07-15 (z) — 📏 implementation-shortfall tracker: is the LIVE book slipping vs shadow, and where? (operator question on the funding live-vs-shadow gap)
- **The finding that prompted it:** the operator read the live Funding Farmer as "much worse" than its shadow. By `pnl_abs` it looks that way (+$1.66 shadow vs −$1.39 live) — but those are DIFFERENT-SIZED books ($1000 paper vs ~$61 real). By the fair metric (return PER TRADE) the LIVE bot is actually AHEAD: +0.80%/trade vs shadow +0.62%, higher win rate (86% vs 71%). Its equity dip is two tail losses (avg −6.4%, one −10% hard-stop) on a tiny concentrated book at n=14 — variance made visible by book size, not signal decay. This is exactly why the judge compares per-trade %, never equity.
- **NEW `implementation_shortfall.py`** — the continuous tracker behind market_context's threshold alert. Pairs live vs shadow per-coin per-trade `pnl_pct` (same coins, same window; the only difference is execution — live=real fills, shadow=mark), publishes the weighted gap + per-coin breakdown + a rolling verdict (clean / live-ahead / live-slipping / insufficient) + history, and DECOMPOSES the gap into ENTRY-slip and EXIT-slip bps once fill prices are on the ledger — so "slipping on exits" gets a clean yes/no. Sustained (`SUSTAIN` cycles) slipping → phone. Board surfaces the verdict (dedicated-push, no double-send). Selftested incl. long/short entry+exit decomposition.
- **`lighter_funding_bot.py` now records `side` + `entry_price` + `exit_price`** on every close (both arms) — live's real fills vs shadow's mark fills are the raw material for the entry/exit decomposition, which starts accruing now (the metric answers "is it exits?" cleanly once ~a day of paired closes lands). Ships main → gate0 ff (funding restart round).
## 2026-07-15 (y) — bloodstream batched reads + incubator anti-overfit (operator: "improve the bloodstream" + honest critique of what we built)
- **BLOODSTREAM — `bot_pnl_store.fetch_states(keys)`**: one query returns many bot_state keys instead of N round-trips. Wired into the organs that read a fistful of bus keys per cycle: evidence board (~8→1), fleet_immune (5→1), fleet_respiration (4→1). Same fail-safe contract (batch {} on failure → callers fall back to load_state/defaults). Cuts Postgres round-trips fleet-wide — the blood carries more per beat, and fewer beats means less contention (the thing that causes the slow-cycle stale-flags).
- **INCUBATOR ANTI-OVERFIT** (the flaw the first live cycle exposed: "fittest" genotype scored +$2.35 but h1 was +$0.01 — all edge in one half = noise). A genotype is now only a trusted CHAMPION when it clears `assess_champion`: tape spans ≥48h (was scoring on ~7h), EACH half ≥ +$1 (both-halves BY MARGIN, not just >0), beats the DEFAULT genome by ≥$2, AND stays champion for 3 consecutive cycles (persistence). Until then the leaderboard is published but flagged `tentative (noise-risk)` — trusted by nothing. Funding offspring still only reach live through the judge's paired bar regardless. Selftested: the +$0.01-half case is now correctly REJECTED as noise; short-tape/thin-edge/streak-reset all covered.
- Held the line (per the loop-frequency discussion): cadences stay matched to each signal's refresh rate, NOT maximized — faster polling invites venue WAF/rate-limit bans, hammers the single Postgres, and self-defeats into MORE staleness.
## 2026-07-15 (x) — market pulse: 30-min history (operator: "more data to better inform") + its freshness made readable
- **`market_pulse.py` appends history every ~30 min instead of hourly** (`PULSE_HIST_GAP_SEC` 3300→1740), cap 200→400 (`PULSE_HIST_CAP`) so the ~8-day window holds at the doubled resolution. `latest` still refreshes every ~10-min loop; the brain's mood/panic-conditioned analysis and the board now read twice the history points.
- **Pulse now carries the fleet freshness contract** (`updated`+`ttl_sec`) — it never had one, so `fleet_respiration` couldn't read its age and FALSE-FLAGGED it as a dead oxygen feed (SpO2 dinged on first boot). Fixed at the source; respiration's market-pulse limit tightened 2h→30min to match the real refresh. Now every organ (respiration, immune, fleet_bus.is_fresh) can see pulse's age like any other feed.
- Note recorded for the 21-Jul review: loop cadence is matched to each signal's natural refresh rate, NOT maximized — faster polling past the signal rate invites venue rate-limits/WAF bans (Lighter has blocked Railway REST before), hammers the single Postgres, and is self-defeating (slower cycles → more staleness). Pulse at 30-min history / 10-min refresh fits fear-greed + funding cadence.
## 2026-07-15 (w) — FOUR organs: 🩹 regeneration, 🧬 reproduction, 🕐 circadian, 🫁 respiration (operator: "build all of the above" + "what is the oxygen / what processes it")
- **🩹 `fleet_regen.py` — REGENERATION (self-repair tier 2).** The immune organ recognizes + quarantines; regen RESTORES. When a STATEFUL organ (xp-judge phase machine, scout-tuner, gapscout-census) gets wedged on fresh-but-wrong content the immune organ flagged SICK, regen restores its last-good `bot_state_history` snapshot (age-bounded to 24h) or a registered safe baseline. Content-only, carrying the snapshot's ORIGINAL age + a `_regen` marker, so a restored payload reads as exactly the aged snapshot it is (fail-safe-stale until the organ republishes) — never asserts old data as current. Reuses the immune invariants for "clean". Never touches live/account state or the ledgers. Phone-pushes repairs.
- **🧬 `strategy_incubator.py` — REPRODUCTION.** Breeds whole GENOTYPES (crossover of the elite + single-allele mutation, deterministic, on-grid). TWO substrates with the safety line between them: TAKER genotypes are scored INSTANTLY by replay over the recorded tape (shadow-only $1k book — a leaderboard, zero live reach); FUNDING genotypes can reach the live bot, so they are only PROPOSED to `xp-queue` and the experiment judge runs each through the IDENTICAL paired bar (≥7d/≥30 closes/both-halves) — no offspring gets a shortcut a human-written candidate wouldn't. - **Experiment judge now consumes the queue** — refactored from index-based to NAME-based candidate tracking (`done`/`current`/`spec`) with a pure `candidate_pool()` (static CANDIDATES + admitted xp-queue offspring; an offspring carrying an unknown lever is rejected) + `next_candidate()`. The promotion bar itself is UNTOUCHED. Selftested.

- **🫁 `fleet_respiration.py` — RESPIRATION (answers "what is the oxygen").** OXYGEN = fresh market data (money is food — slow; data is oxygen — minutes). MOLECULE = mark/index/bid-ask/funding/vol/OI. LUNGS = the venue-fetch layer; MEMBRANE = the equity guard + freshness filters. The gap it fills: the watchdog catches organ DEATH, the immune organ catches SICKNESS, neither measures the SUPPLY. Respiration computes blood-oxygen SATURATION (weighted fraction of data feeds breathing fresh) and phone-alerts on a HYPOXIA transition — the fleet-wide data-starvation a per-organ check misses (exactly tonight's family-bot WS-drop, writ fleet-wide).

## 2026-07-15 (v) — 🛡️ IMMUNE + SELF-REPAIR organ: covering "alive but sick" (operator's "what self-repairs / what filters" framing, made concrete by the same-evening false-down-scale incident)
- **The gap:** the whole fleet's safety model treats failure as DEATH — every fail-safe contract says "stale/silent → ignore." That leaves ALIVE-BUT-SICK uncovered: an organ publishing FRESH, in-TTL, trusted data that is WRONG. It bit real money hours earlier (the 39h fossil driving a false live down-scale). The watchdog is a pain receptor (screams at DARK organs); nothing filtered the blood or recognized fresh-but-wrong.
- **NEW `fleet_immune.py`** (freqtrade-bots container, ~15 min). **FILTRATION (kidney/liver):** prunes the `fleet-alerts` feed of age-stale fossils (the feed count-caps at 50 but never pruned by AGE — that's why the fossil survived) and known-toxic ANTIBODY matches (seeded with the retired whole-book "P&L gap +X%" pattern). Safe by construction — only removes stale data. **ADAPTIVE IMMUNITY:** scans fresh payloads for invariant violations (scout n_liquid>n_books or stress.med<0; lens-forward n4h<0 or hit4h∉[0,1]; census negatives; xp-judge unknown phase; bot_pnl NaN/inf/absurd-pnl_pct; a fleet-tuning lever outside its registry bounds), and — the one active remediation — QUARANTINES a sick lever. `fleet_tuning.get_lever` now consults `fleet-immune.quarantined_levers` centrally (one hook protects EVERY lever consumer) and returns the operator default for a quarantined lever = revert to the operator's own value = the safe direction. Phone-pushes NEW sickness (the watchdog's blind spot). Fail-safe OPEN: a stale/dead immune payload quarantines nothing (a dead immune system must not paralyze the body; levers stay bounded + TTL'd regardless). Board surfaces sick findings as a 🛡️ warn item.
- **Explicitly restrict/clean only** — never opens positions, never widens, never an expand action. Selftested: age+antibody filtration, lever-out-of-bounds detection, organ invariants with death≠sickness (a STALE organ's impossible content is NOT flagged — that's the watchdog's job), bot-row NaN/inf/absurd, and the central quarantine hook in fleet_tuning. Ships main → gate0 ff.

The 48h freshness window let a fossil cut real money.

Net effect next cycle: no fresh divergence → `desired_live=None` → the 0.75 lever expires → live bots return to the operator's env sizing (the small live books were never in danger — down-scale is the safe direction — but it was wrong).

## 2026-07-15 (i) — watchdog alerts go to the operator's PHONE (user-requested; infra only, no strategy logic)
- **`fleet_watchdog_svc.py` gains an ntfy.sh push channel** (user: "send to my phone instead of email"). Auth-free pub/sub: the unguessable topic in `NTFY_TOPIC` (pnl-dashboard service env) is the only secret; the phone's ntfy app subscribes to the same topic — no account, no SMTP, no new credentials anywhere. Same transition-based semantics (🚨 alert / updated ≥30-min gap / ✅ recovery): phone push sends first (priority `urgent` for alerts, `default` for recovery), email second if SMTP is ever also configured; either delivery marks the transition sent. Titles are ASCII-sanitized (urllib headers are latin-1; emoji ride as ntfy tags), bodies stay UTF-8. `/watchdog.json` now reports `push_armed` beside `email_armed`. Selftest covers URL/headers/sanitization, unconfigured→False, network-failure→False-never-raises. SMTP remains the (dormant) path for report_emailer's scheduled P&L mails — push replaces email for WATCHDOG alerts only.

## 2026-07-15 (g) — HOTFIX: review-reminder.yml was invalid YAML (the source of the GitHub failure emails)
- `review-reminder.yml`'s issue body was a multi-line double-quoted string with column-0 lines, which terminated the `run: |` block scalar early — GitHub could not parse the workflow's `name:`, so it raised a FAILED run (and a failure email) on EVERY push (~13 today). Nothing in the fleet was affected; the noise was purely the unparseable file. Rebuilt the body with `printf` (each line a quoted arg whose indentation sits OUTSIDE the quotes) so the YAML is valid and the rendered issue body has no stray spaces. Verified: pyyaml parses it, `push` is not a trigger (so no more per-push runs), body renders clean. The workflow itself is kept — the MCP scheduler trigger still needs a claude.ai connector approval, so this remains the durable 21-Jul review reminder.

## 2026-07-15 (c) — HOTFIX: exposure harvest crashed fleet_risk on the live trend bot's row
- **`held` publishes in two shapes and the (b) exposure harvest assumed one.** Funding/family books publish `extra.held` as a DICT ({coin: 'L'/'S'/tag}) but the LIVE Tide Rider (and the equities ports) publish a LIST (`sorted(meta.keys())`); `held.items()` threw AttributeError on `crypto-trend-daily-lighter`'s row, killing every fleet_risk cycle post-deploy — the light froze (last publish 06:09Z) and consumers correctly failed OPEN, i.e. the long-budget veto went dark on the running fleet. Caught ~25 min in by the post-merge verification poller + the consumption-matrix audit. Fix: `held_items()` normalizes both shapes (list entries side-default long — correct for the long-only publishers of that shape), used in both harvest loops AND the pre-existing PERPS longs/shorts derivation (same latent hazard). Selftest covers dict/list/None + a direct simulation of the crashing row.

## 2026-07-15 (b) — what successful systems have: exposure view + replay harness (user-approved)
- **fleet_risk EXPOSURE VIEW (`exposure_concentration`) — the light counts positions, this says how many BETS they are.** From the ecosystem gap review ("you are 23× long one factor and nothing says so"): the same directional cohort the light counts now also publishes `exposure` — per-symbol pileup (top-8), distinct symbols, **effective independent-bet count (1/Herfindahl)**, crypto-vs-equity-perp cluster split (curated `FLEET_EQUITY_BASES`, env-extensible; ambiguous tickers stay crypto), and `sym_uncovered` (positions whose publisher exposes no symbol detail — the view only claims what it can see). Symbol harvest now ALSO reads `extra.held` (gate0 family books + live Funding Farmer), so those books join `pair_concentration` for the first time. Advisory publish-only (payload + lean history mirror); budgets/light/veto semantics untouched. `--selftest` covers pileup math (4×SOL+1×ETH → eff_n 1.5), clusters, coverage honesty.
- **NEW 🎬 `lighter_ticket_replay.py` — replay the recorded scout tape through the Ticket Taker's REAL code.** The Lighter-native fleet validated by forward-shadow only (days per rule tweak) while the scout was already recording the needed tape (5-min `lighter-market` history with marks since 15-Jul). The harness feeds that history through the taker's own `incredible()` bars and `exit_reason()` TP/SL/max-hold (imported, not reimplemented — it cannot drift from the bot), models fills at snapshot marks with the same 4bps fees, replays the stress veto, and reports per-lens seen/taken/closed/WR/net/exits + unrealized tail. Env-tunable variants (`TT_TP=0.06 python3 lighter_ticket_replay.py`) judge a bar change against recorded reality in seconds. Honest divergences documented in-file: no funding accrual (tape has no rates — long lenses read rich, divergence shorts poor), fixed clips (no ranges), no bus vetoes. Reads Postgres or the public `/bus.json`; writes nothing. Selftest (tp/sl/hold, dip bar, stress veto, one-per-lens, fee math) + first live-tape run green (19 snapshots: divergence TP +$2.10, momentum SL −$1.60, 6 open).

## 2026-07-15 — learning-loop wiring: point the brain at the living fleet (user-approved)
- **Evidence & learning assessment** (`EVIDENCE_AND_LEARNING_REVIEW_2026-07-15.md`): 120 brain runs, ZERO premature throttles (floors/streaks behaving), but the 14-Jul pivot broke the L4 mult loop on both ends — the only consumers were the stopped Kraken strategies, and the family bot's closes dropped the strategy tag so all seven books bucketed as enter-tag `long`. 10/16 ACTIONABLE hypotheses prosecuted retired bots (rows never leave the fetch window → the streak-retire path could never fire); Trail Blazer's paper engine STILL trades (16 closes since 14-Jul — stopping the momo service stays operator-only). Lens-forward was empty purely because scout `marks` began publishing 04:27 today.
- **Family bot rejoins the learning loop (`lighter_family_bot.py`, gate0 cross-merge needed to deploy).** Closes now publish `long-<tag hyphenated>_<exit>` (`ledger_reason`; hyphens because the ledger parser splits at the FIRST underscore — taker precedent 14-Jul (g)) so the brain buckets per strategy tag, and entries apply `fleet_bus.stake_multiplier(b.bot_id, ledger_tag(tag))` — the same helper derives ledger tag AND lookup key, so buckets and lookups can't drift. Reduce-only, clamped [0.5,1.0], fail-neutral, logged when <1.0; NO-OP at deploy (published table empty). `fleet_bus.py` added to Dockerfile.familyshadow. No entry/exit logic touched.
- **Brain LIVENESS filter (`bot_learn.py`).** Hypotheses/diagnoses/multipliers generate only for living bots (not in `cleanup_legacy_bots.LEGACY_BOTS`, ≥1 ledger close in 7d); scorecards keep printing for everyone flagged `[inactive/retired]`; existing dead-bot entries decay via the normal 3-run retire path. `_epoch` now parses the sniper's `'... UTC'` timestamps (liveness + drift joins). Verified with a 3-run fixture: identical losing evidence in a live/stale/retired bot → hypotheses only for the live one (candidate→ACTIONABLE at run 3).
- **Ledger tag contract extracted (`bot_pnl_store.split_reason`)** — the one parser the taker's and family bot's composers round-trip against (unit-tested: 36 tag/exit combos + legacy/funding/sniper edges). `/bus.json` now also serves `brain-lens-forward` (live + history) beside the other brain keys; run_all.sh taker comment corrected to `<side>-<lens>_<exit>`.

## 2026-07-14 (h) — the missing risk leg: measured risk, not counted (user-approved)
- **Fleet DRAWDOWN GOVERNOR (`fleet_risk.dd_governor`).** The fleet had per-bot seatbelts but nothing that said "the whole book is bleeding — shrink". fleet_risk now sums equity over the same authoritative rows the light counts, keeps a 7-day rolling sample window inside its own state (≥30-min gaps, redeploy-safe), and publishes `fleet_equity` / `fleet_dd_7d` / **`clip_scale`** (1.0 → 0.5 past −5% 7d drawdown → 0.25 past −10%; env `FLEET_DD_HALF`/`FLEET_DD_QUARTER`) — state + history. Ticket Taker CONSUMES it (clips scale down, fail-safe neutral on stale state); advisory for the gate0 books until ported. 7-case unit suite + end-to-end drive (seeded −10% → half clips, historized).
- **Ticket Taker: CONSTANT-RISK sizing (`vol_clip`).** Fixed $50 clips carried ~5x different risk across books; now clip = `TT_RISK_USD`(1.5) / expected adverse move (half the daily range, floored 0.5%), bounded $20–$80, fallback $50 when a book has no range data. Per-position clip stored in meta so ledger `pnl_pct`/fees use the real size; the brain's per-lens grading is on pnl_pct so sizing doesn't distort it. Calm books (BTC ~2% range) size up to the cap; wild alts size down to the floor. Selftested across the curve.

## 2026-07-14 (g) — divergence lens + stress veto (user-approved same evening)
- **Ticket Taker learns SHORTS + the FUNDING-DIVERGENCE lens (the strategy nobody in the fleet traded).** Scout gains `tickets.divergence`: when Lighter's funding diverges ≥300pp APR (`SCOUT_DIV_GAP`) from the binance/bybit/HL median for the same liquid symbol, ticket the RECEIVING side (positive gap → short, negative → long), skipping entries that fight the premium (no shorting already-cheap books). Ticket Taker takes them at ≥500pp (`TT_DIV_GAP`) with full SHORT support: signed funding accrual (a short under a positive rate is CREDITED — the lens's whole thesis), side-aware exits, and `PaperBroker`'s signed positions. Live at ship time: 6 divergence tickets, top HOOD +1118.7pp.
- **Stress veto:** when the scout's venue-wide |premium| median ≥15bps (`TT_STRESS_VETO_BPS`; normal tape ~6), Ticket Taker takes NO new entries that cycle (exits keep running) — a whole-venue dislocation means marks are unreliable and every book is one bet.
- **Per-lens brain grading fixed before it mattered:** the ledger's reason parser splits on the FIRST underscore, so the old `long_<lens>_<exit>` tags would have collapsed every lens into one "long" bucket for the brain. New format `<side>-<lens>_<exit>` → enter_tags like `long-breakout` / `short-divergence`; no closes had been recorded yet, so no history was mis-tagged. fleet_risk's short-detection (`"short" in tag`) works with the new tags unchanged.

## 2026-07-14 (e)
- **Lighter Scout: per-strategy TICKETS (user ask: "a scanner per bot so each bot has an edge").** One scanner engine, per-bot-family lenses — not eight services: `strategy_tickets()` hunts each family's setup across ALL liquid Lighter books instead of a fixed whitelist and publishes ranked candidates in the scout payload (`tickets.breakout` → Breakout Hunter/Dad: at the daily high on volume, not trading rich, longs not paying >100% APR; `tickets.dip` → Dip Buyer/Avo Maria: hard bounded flush to the lows, tagged `trend: unknown_v1` — no candle history keylessly; `tickets.momentum` → Stock Leaders/momo: strongest liquid movers, funding-sane). HONEST FRAME: a scanner amplifies a validated edge, it cannot conjure one — and the brain's diagnosis layer shows entries are NOT every bot's lever (georgia/intraday were exit_too_tight, trend-daily fee-scale). So tickets are ADVISORY + HISTORIZED: the weekly review grades each lens's forward returns against the bots' actual entries, and a lens only earns a consumer feed by that evidence. Live print: 6 breakout / 2 dip / 4 momentum tickets over 88 liquid books; selftest covers lens picks + rich/knife/illiquid exclusions.

## 2026-07-14 (c)
- **Pipeline-hiccup sweep (user-requested audit follow-up).** Three read/write defects found while auditing what could inhibit collecting/optimising: (1) **market_pulse history window was ~33h, not the intended ~7d** — the 200-entry cap assumed hourly appends but the pulse loops every ~10 min; history now appends at most hourly (`append_hourly`, gap 55 min), so the brain's mood-conditioned analysis gets ~8 days again. (2) **fleet watchdog's position warning was apples-to-oranges** — it summed open_trades over shadows/funding/stocks/scanner rows (64 on a healthy day) against the 20-position directional budget, warning forever; it now counts the directional cohort only (paper + real-live trading rows; gross kept in the snapshot line). (3) **paper_trades had no read endpoint** (funding bots, sniper, live Lighter closes were invisible to outside reviews) — `/trades.json?source=paper` now serves it. Also pruned the retired-bot LABELS dead code (rows deleted + RETIRED_ROWS filters republishes). FLAGGED, needs an owner decision (bots live outside this repo): equities-momentum-alpaca publishes raw $100k-scale Alpaca paper equity and equities-regime-ibkr raw ~$250k IBKR paper equity — 100-250x every other book, swamping cross-bot P&L comparisons and tripping the watchdog daily-loss alert; fix at source (publish vs a recorded baseline, like the Jul-10 live-Lighter fix) or normalize dashboard-side.
- **Dead bots removed at the source (user-approved).** `cleanup_legacy_bots.py` allow-list extended with the dashboard's RETIRED_ROWS set (Bounce Catcher + lshadow, Trail Blazer paper/live/lshadow, regime-switch + lshadow, Loop Scout, trendmomo-4h) and now runs `--apply` once on every freqtrade-bots boot (new COPY in Dockerfile.freqtrade + run_all.sh one-shot) — deletes the frozen `bot_pnl` snapshot rows ONLY; trade ledgers, venue_orders and bot_state history are kept (Manage-panel delete semantics), and a revived bot's row reappears on its next publish. `fleet_risk.py` counting lists pruned to the living cohort (PERPS_LS_BOTS now empty — the whole perps L/S cohort is retired). Belt (staleness filter) + braces (rows gone).
- **fleet_risk: GHOST-EXPOSURE fix — stale bot_pnl rows no longer count.** Post-merge verification of `/bus.json` caught the live light pinned at RED 32L/0S for hours while the real fleet held ~10 longs: `fleet_risk` reads the raw `bot_pnl` table (not the dashboard's filtered view), and rows outlive their bots — decommissioned Bounce Catcher (`perps-rsi-meanrev`, stopped 12 Jul) froze holding 6 longs, Trail Blazer's undisplayed paper row held 16 more. Rows older than 30 min (`STALE_ROW_SEC`) are now ignored in exposure counts AND all signal-bus mirrors (a frozen scanner row must not republish last week's dislocation). Unit-driven with synthetic fresh/ghost/stale rows. PREREQUISITE for the RED-veto enforcement wiring — a ghost-inflated light would throttle live bots for positions nobody holds. Review doc's "current state" stamp corrected (the GREEN 10L/0S figure was read off filtered `/pnl.json`).
- **Cross-bot advisory review (the one the 7-Jul design scheduled) — verdicts + evidence in `CROSS_BOT_ADVISORY_REVIEW_2026-07-14.md`.** Reconstructed the advisory week from the `/trades.json` ledger (121 trades, Jul-03→13; `bot_state_history` is unreachable off-Railway — itself a finding, fixed below). **L2 (fleet-risk light): evidence supports wiring the RED veto** — entries taken at 13+ ledger-visible fleet positions won 18% (n=44, −$18.04, worst bucket); peak visible concurrency 20 with two perps books uncounted; a RED(20L) veto would have cost ~nothing while capping the 26-position-scar tail. ## 2026-07-14 (b)
- **Brain DIAGNOSIS layer — it can now tell an entry problem from an exit problem from a fee/regime/venue problem.** Born from the dashboard's four "tighten the X entry gates" proposals: two were plumbing artifacts (fixed earlier today — exit reasons masquerading as entry tags), and the deeper flaw was that the brain had ONE prose template for every negative bucket. `bot_learn.diagnose()` now classifies each negative (bot, enter_tag) bucket at n≥10 into **exit_too_tight / venue_execution / fee_bleed / regime_timing / entry_quality / mixed_unclear** using: exit-path loss splits (ledger), **post-exit drift from public Kraken 1h candles** (did price reclaim entry within 24h of the losing exit? — the mechanized form of the manual replay behind the 13-Jul stop widening), fee-scale tests (median loser vs round-trip friction), a **regime-oracle-history join** (new `bot_pnl_store.fetch_state_history`; snapshots every 30 min since 7-Jul), and the venue A/B table. Proposals now name the lever; "tighten the entry gates" is only earned by confirmed entry_quality. Publishes bot_state `brain-diagnosis` (+history); hypothesis keys unchanged so existing streaks carry over. **Validated against known ground truth** (full-July replay, live candles): intraday/georgia trailing-stop bleed → exit_too_tight with 79–100% reclaim (the manual 13-Jul replay found 77–89%); crypto-breakout-4h breakout → entry_quality (22% reclaim, negative fwd — the tide-gate finding); freqtrade-dad → mixed_unclear on murky 11-trade evidence (declines to call it); trend-daily probes → fee-scale, "signals are not the lever" (the CONTROL verdict). 6-scenario unit suite green. Advisory-only: humans + dashboard consume it; nothing trades on diagnoses directly. Drift work bounded (once-per-pair candle cache, ≤120 evaluations/run); all evidence fail-soft — a missing source just removes its rule from contention.

## 2026-07-11
- **Funding Farmer: multi-factor POSITION SCANNER — picks the best risk-adjusted positions, not just the hottest funder.** Replaces the naive raw-|APR| sort (which spent slots on thin, high-vol, trending-against alts that run the 10% stop before funding pays — why it was break-even). Designed via a fan-out+judge+synthesize workflow (6 philosophies, adversarially judged) grounded in the real venue data surface, then PROVEN on a new portfolio backtest (`scripts/backtest_scanner.py`, 150d, 6-slot). Two layers: (1) BACKTESTED CORE — a realized-vol + adverse-trend VETO (skip stop-out traps, leave the slot empty) + an |APR|-backbone bounded-discount rank (downside-capped, never a vol divisor); (2) LIVE-ONLY overlay (shadow-validated) — cross-venue `_bench` sign-agreement + full-depth clip-slippage gate + book-imbalance tiebreak. Cheap funding prefilter → candle-scan top 15 (cached ~50min) → book-probe only top 5, sized to the ~21 weight/min governor (Railway ws is CDN-blocked so every book = 1 token). RESULT at the **default 0.40 gate**: net **+52%** ($21.6→$33.0/clip/150d), ret/DD **2.0→5.3**, maxDD **−43%**, FEWER trades (282 vs 336) — robust across both halves + OOS. HONEST: much of the edge is directional MEAN-REVERSION (regime-dependent), not extra funding — the vol veto is the durable piece; funding is thin. Gate-widening to 0.25 (+155% in-cache) is OPT-IN (`SCAN_ENTER=0.25`) and partly a flat-slippage artifact — shadow-validate first. `SCAN=off` restores legacy. Every entry logs scan evidence to `venue_orders`. Shipped core == backtested core (same veto/score/lookbacks). Adversarially reviewed (4-lens workflow, findings verified): 3 confirmed fixes — the live features were computed on the in-progress forming hourly bar (now dropped → closed-bar only, matching the backtest + no look-ahead); the feature cache is now keyed on the last CLOSED hour (a fresh vol spike is picked up next loop, not ~50min later); the top-15 prefilter divergence documented + env-tunable — plus 2 hardening (defensive best-first book sort for the Railway REST-fallback; scan wrapped so a bug can't crash stop-management). One "medium" (governor starvation) was verified a FALSE POSITIVE (scan runs after stop-management + degrades to no-entry + fail-safe flatten). Shadow-first — nothing armed.
- **Both-perp (self-custody) Yield Harvester hedge — BACKTESTED and REJECTED, not built.** Asked to take the delta-neutral Yield Harvester (`perps-funding-carry`) live on Lighter; it can't (perp-only venue has no same-coin hedge — the code hard-refuses a naked-perp funded run). Chosen self-custody alternative: hedge a hot alt's funding leg with a beta-weighted ETH/BTC perp. `scripts/backtest_carry_hedged.py` (150d real HL funding+price, reuses `.dirfund_cache.json`) compares directional vs major-hedge vs ideal spot-hedge on IDENTICAL signals. HONEST RESULT: the hedge is WORSE than the directional Funding Farmer it was meant to improve (+$5.5 β·ETH vs +$7.9 directional per $25 clip/150d). Structural, not tuning: the funding-receiving side's price P&L is already ~flat (little market-beta to strip), so the hedge mostly adds double slippage + its own funding drag, while the idiosyncratic alt risk it CAN'T hedge is the part that matters; higher gates go negative. BTC-hedge cuts drawdown (ret/DD 0.66->0.75) but at lower absolute profit + double the live surface. The only profitable delta-neutral structure remains the CEX-spot hedge (docs/cex_spot_hedge_scope.md). Verdict per repo rule (no ship without a proving backtest; cf Trail Blazer): DID NOT build the live bot. See memory both-perp-hedge-no-edge.
- **Funding Farmer (`perps-funding-lighter`): go-live prep — re-validated, shadow evidence checked, docs corrected.** Pivoted to taking the already-built DIRECTIONAL Funding Farmer live instead. Re-validated: `--once` runs clean (231 perps, no orders); proved `lighter_live` REFUSES to boot without BOTH `REAL_MONEY_KILL=DISARMED_I_UNDERSTAND` and `PERPS_FUNDING_LIGHTER_MAX_NOTIONAL`. Shadow evidence (Postgres): `perps-funding-lighter-lshadow` deployed + running, measured perp-leg slippage on traded coins is SUB-1bps (ETH 0.3–0.5, SOL 0.13, BTC 0.36, HYPE 0.92) — zero-fee + spread-gate thesis holds; only ~2 closed round-trips so P&L sample still thin (~net flat, as expected for ~break-even directional). Fixed GO_LIVE_LIGHTER.md env drift: the example still showed the pre-tuning `FUNDING_HARD_STOP=0.05` / `MAX_SPREAD_BPS=50` (a 5% stop LOSES) — corrected to the tuned defaults + a don't-lower warning, and added a dated shadow-readiness note. Keys/disarm/deposit remain the operator's — nothing armed. NOTE flagged: `crypto-trend-daily-lighter` (Tide Rider LIVE) is crashed/error ~11h (likely auth), separate from this work.

Dashboard shows it as "🌊 Tide Rider · SHADOW/LIVE" automatically.
- **Funding Farmer: risk defaults TUNED on a directional backtest (`scripts/backtest_directional_funding.py`).** Replays the exact directional logic on 150d of real HL funding+price (30 coins, intrabar stop fidelity). HONEST RESULT: the strategy is only ~BREAK-EVEN — funding capture is real (+$8/150d per $25 clip) but directional price risk nearly offsets it (-$6.5), as expected when funding compensates the risk it carries. Least-bad + most-robust config = WIDE stop + TIGHT take-profit (a tight 5% stop whipsaws out on noise and loses -$10.6; 10-12% stop breaks even). Tuned defaults: HARD_STOP 5%->10%, keep TAKE_PROFIT 4%, MIN_VOL $5M->$10M, MAX_SPREAD_BPS 50->20 (live Lighter shows hot+liquid candidates all <=3.5bps, so 20 is safe and excludes traps). Verdict: run tiny/shadow — the genuine edge needs the delta-neutral CEX-spot hedge (deferred), which keeps the funding WITHOUT the price risk.
- **Funding Farmer (Lighter directional funding) — new bot `lighter_funding_bot.py` (perps-funding-lighter).** "Unlock live harvesting" on Lighter only (no cross-venue hedge). A single perp-only venue can't be delta-neutral, so this captures funding DIRECTIONALLY: short positive-funding perps / long negative, collect Lighter's zero-fee funding, price risk bounded by a HARD STOP (not a hedge). Reuses the venue layer end-to-end (hl_paper|lighter_shadow|lighter_live) so lighter_live is auto-refused until REAL_MONEY_KILL is disarmed + a PERPS_FUNDING_LIGHTER_MAX_NOTIONAL cap is set. Funding accrued in paper/shadow; live reads it from account equity. Self-test caught+fixed a CRITICAL inverted-stop-sign bug before ship (stop now fires on adverse moves). Dashboard: "💸 Funding Farmer". Adversarially reviewed. HONEST: directional (real price risk), shadow-first.

Lowering ENTER_APR 40%->20% lifts net ~35% ON both-perp only; at 20bps-CEX-hedge a 15% gate goes NEGATIVE — so keep 40% gate unless hedging with a second Lighter perp. Edge concentrated (ME/JTO/INJ). Bot is still a paper simulator; live harvest needs a Lighter execution path (shadow-first). See memory funding-carry-structural-edge-lighter.
- **Trail Blazer: 200-EMA regime gate (REGIME_GATE_FRAC=0.97)** — only enters dips within 3% of the 200-EMA (a pullback in an uptrend), skipping falling-knife dips below trend (logged REGIME_SKIP). Backtest-tuned (scripts/backtest_regime.py): the loose -3% gate roughly HALVES the out-of-sample loss vs no gate (-7.7%->-3.3%) and trades more than the strict gate. Risk control, NOT an edge — the strategy is still net-negative even gated (see memory trail-blazer-no-durable-edge). Widening the universe was tested and REJECTED (made OOS worse -3.3%->-8.7%). Env-tunable; 0.0 disables.
- **live trade logging**: the live (lighter_live) order path now records every real open to venue_orders and every real close to venue_orders + paper_trades (venue='lighter', shadow=false, realized P&L, raw exchange response) — so the Lighter Live dashboard card shows trade history, win/loss and per-trade P&L exactly like the paper bots (previously only equity-based total P&L). Ledger writes are guarded separately so they can never mask an order failure. Also fixes a latent bug: live opens now set entry_ts (the 72h time-stop was inert on the live path). Applied to hyperliquid_momo_bot.py (Trail Blazer).
- **live bots no longer die silently (the 'stale' bug)**: when a lighter_live/testnet bot fails venue init (e.g. "private key does not match the one on Lighter"), venue_context now publishes an explicit status=error row with the reason before re-raising — so the dashboard card shows WHY the bot is down instead of just ageing into a stale flag. NEW `scripts/lighter_test_trade.py`: user-run order-path test — default places a POST-ONLY limit 50% below market (cannot fill) then cancels it (sign→submit→cancel proven, ~$0); `--fill` does a real ~$10 market round-trip.
- **live P&L measured from starting collateral, not the $1000 paper baseline** — a live Lighter bot funds from real collateral (e.g. $65), so the dashboard was showing an untraded live account as -$935 (equity - $1000). Fix: the live path records its starting equity once (persisted in bot_state `<bot>-lighter:live`) and publishes pnl_abs = equity - baseline (→ $0 until the first trade); the dashboard also never applies the $1000 paper baseline to `is_live_bot` rows. Applied to momo + perps bots.
- **venues/lighter_client.py — ws hardening**: Lighter's CDN 400s the order-book WebSocket upgrade from cloud (datacenter) IPs though its REST works from the same host, so the live bot spammed reconnect warnings. Now the ws presents a browser-like handshake (Origin + Chrome UA) as a best-effort, and after 3 quick retries goes QUIET (retries every 10 min, logs once) — orderbook() falls back to governed REST snapshots, which work fine for order placement. No functional impact either way.
- **Dockerfile.perpslive**: dedicated image for the bounce-catcher-live Lighter service (hyperliquid_perps_bot.py; same inert-until-disarmed pattern as Dockerfile.momolive).
- **dashboard: dedicated "⚡ Lighter Live" section** — real-money `<bot>-lighter` rows are pulled out of the paper grid into their own bordered, red-accented section at the top of the page (header shows live equity · P&L · bot count + a REAL MONEY tag), so live money is never lost among the paper cards. Render-tested. `perps-donchian-breakout-lighter` went live this session ($64.93, Trail Blazer on Lighter).
- **venues/lighter_client.py — live-path fix**: `SignerClient.__init__` builds an aiohttp `ApiClient` internally (→ `asyncio.get_running_loop()`), so constructing it in the synchronous `LighterClient.__init__` crashed the LIVE/testnet path with `RuntimeError: no running event loop` (never hit before because the signer path can't be tested without a key). Now the signer is constructed ON the client's loop thread via `run_coroutine_threadsafe`, same as the read-only ApiClient in `_build()`. Verified with a dummy key: construction now reaches auth (fails cleanly on key validation, not the loop). Shadow/paper paths unaffected.

## 2026-07-09
- **Dockerfile.momolive + trail-blazer-live service scaffold**: dedicated image for Trail Blazer's LIVE Lighter service (CMD baked in, funding-service pattern; select via RAILWAY_DOCKERFILE_PATH=Dockerfile.momolive). Deployed inert by design: VENUE=lighter_live with REAL_MONEY_KILL armed refuses to boot until the user supplies LIGHTER_API_PRIVATE_KEY and explicitly disarms — the user-only steps.
- **fleet_risk: venue-aware (no-miss-sync for go-live)** — the fleet risk light + signal bus counted directional exposure and read funding by BASE bot name, so once a bot goes live on Lighter (publishing as `<bot>-lighter`) the live book would be INVISIBLE to the fleet view and funding would be read from the wrong venue. Now `authoritative_row()` prefers the live Lighter row over the paper twin (counts real Lighter exposure, no double-count), the shadow/testnet cohort is surfaced as `info_only` (modelled, never moves the light), and the signal-bus funding prefers the Lighter-facing funding-carry row + tags `funding_source` (HL vs Lighter funding genuinely differ). Verified against the live DB: shows `shadow(info) 5L/0S` from the running shadow cohort. regime_oracle deliberately left on HL (majors' daily regime is venue-identical, nothing consumes it yet, and Lighter's candle REST is WAF-blocked — moving it would force the Lighter SDK into the shared freqtrade image for ~zero benefit); cross-exchange/triangular arb are inherently multi-venue and unchanged.
- **NEW bot `lighter_perp_sniper.py` (🎯 Perp Sniper) — Lighter-native new-listing sniper, SHADOW-FIRST**: replaces the spot Launch Sniper for the Lighter go-live (the spot sniper can't run on a fixed-market perps DEX). Detects freshly-listed perp markets by diffing the active market set against a persisted baseline (source of truth; `AnnouncementApi` text is context only), seeds the baseline on first run (never snipes the existing 215), then snipes new listings long with TP +15% / SL −10% / 6h max-hold + global cap, all through the venue layer (Lighter-only; defaults to `lighter_shadow`, refuses `hl_paper`). UNVALIDATED by design — runs shadow (modelled fills on live books → `venue_orders` ledger) until Eamon disarms the kill switch. Verified: seed cycle (198 active markets baselined, 0 sniped) + a simulated new-listing detection that shadow-snipes ADA/WIF crossing the real book (~3–4 bps entry slippage). Registered on the dashboard (EXPECTED + label); `railway.lightersniper.toml` deploys it from the base Dockerfile.
- **Dockerfile.funding**: COPY `paper_broker.py` + `venues/` — funding_carry now imports the venue layer, so the lean image needs them (validated by importing the bot with only the Dockerfile.funding file set present).
- **Lighter.xyz venue layer (Gate 0) — `venues/` package**: NEW `VenueClient` abstraction (place/cancel/amend, positions, balances, funding, orderbook, candles, ws) with `HyperliquidClient` (byte-identical passthrough of the old inline HL logic) + `LighterClient` (lighter-sdk 1.1.1). `VENUE` env selects `hl_paper` (default, unchanged) | `lighter_shadow` | `lighter_testnet` | `lighter_live`. Trail Blazer (`hyperliquid_momo_bot.py`), Bounce Catcher (`hyperliquid_perps_bot.py`) and Yield Harvester (`funding_carry_bot.py`) refactored onto it. **Parity proven**: HL candles byte-identical old-vs-new; one-cycle decision diff = 0 for Bounce Catcher, and only the 2 intended cap-clips for Trail Blazer. Both pilots ran one clean shadow cycle on LIVE Lighter mainnet books.
- **Trail Blazer global position cap (pre-live blocker)**: `hyperliquid_momo_bot.py` gains `MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS=8` + `MAX_NEW_PER_LOOP=2` gated on a live open count (the same pattern Bounce Catcher got Jul-5) — it was holding 15 concurrent longs alone vs the fleet budget of 20. Valid buys blocked by the cap log `CAP_SKIP`. In lighter modes the cap derives from the per-bot notional cap.
- **Adapter safety rails (`venues/safety.py`, `venues/governor.py`)**: `REAL_MONEY_KILL` ARMED by default — `lighter_live` refuses to start and flattens+halts mid-run unless set to `DISARMED_I_UNDERSTAND` (checked EVERY loop); per-bot notional caps from env (no growable code default); `LIGHTER_MAX_DAILY_LOSS` flatten-and-halt. Weighted TX-budget governor for the 60-req/min standard tier (order tx weight 6, exponential backoff on 429/405), ws-first market data with one shared reconnecting book cache.
- **Shadow mode (`venues/shadow.py`)**: `ShadowBroker` fills paper orders by crossing the LIVE Lighter book (walk asks for buys, bids for sells) and logs every order to the new `venue_orders` Postgres table (decision px vs modelled fill px, spread/slippage bps, raw response) — realizable-spread evidence for sizing. Never sends.
- **bot_pnl_store**: `paper_trades` gains `venue`/`shadow` columns; NEW `venue_orders` table + `publish_venue_order()`. All guarded (never raise into a loop).
- **dashboard: LIVE distinction + separate live P&L total** — venue-variant rows (`<bot>-lighter`/`-ltest`/`-lshadow`) are detected dynamically, badged (LIVE red / TESTNET amber / SHADOW blue), kept OUT of the paper crypto headline, and summed into their own **🔴 LIVE · Lighter** totals line + `/pnl.json` `meta.live_fleet`. Real-money live P&L folds into GRAND TOTAL; shadow stays out (modelled).
- **day-1 verifications → `docs/lighter.md`**: 215 mainnet markets, ZERO fees, $10/order min, ETH/ADA/HYPE listed (Cowork excerpt was wrong on ADA/HYPE), BLUR/ATOM/INJ/ORDI/TON unlisted, kBONK↔1000BONK / PEPE↔1000PEPE remaps, native cross-venue funding (lighter+HL+binance+bybit in one call), signer binaries verified on linux/amd64 AND Mac arm64, ws ~800ms. Scripts: `scripts/lighter_day1.py`, `scripts/lighter_testnet_smoke.py` (6/6 read-only pass), `scripts/lighter_accounts.py` (read-only sub-account mapper). `GO_LIVE_LIGHTER.md` = sub-account-per-bot user-only checklist. NO keys, NO funds, NO `lighter_live` deploy — that stays with Eamon.

Built for the alpaca anomaly: its +$10,673.77 "today" was the equity-delta vs a glitched Jul-6 88k snapshot (the −$19.9k anomaly's mirror), while its "lifetime −$5,365.91" was actually Alpaca's day P&L published into pnl_abs (fixed in the equities-momentum-alpaca repo: pnl_abs → lifetime vs $100k baseline, day P&L → pnl_daily).

# Changelog

## 2026-07-07
- dashboard: **reset-proof "All-time Σ (ledger)" row on every bot card** — lifetime P&L + closed count straight from the durable `bot_trades` ledger, so a live-DB wipe can never again masquerade as "no trades" (July-6: Range Raider's card showed $1000/0 while the ledger held 8 real post-rework trades, 3W/5L −$2.47). If this row and Total P&L disagree, a reset happened.
- investigated Range Raider "no trades since rework": logs CLEAN (no errors/OOM/crash-loop); the regime-legs fix DID trade (8 closes 07-05→07-06 03:00); live counter wiped ~July-6 during the family-bot restructure deploys (exact mechanism aged out of the log window). Retracted the suspected mum/8089 port collision — regime-switch polls in its own container via FT_POLLER_BOTS.

Shared log of every change that affects a running bot, the dashboard, or deploy
behaviour. **Multiple Claude sessions work this repo in parallel** — this file is
how they stay in sync. If your diff touches bot code (`*.py`, `*.sh`, a strategy,
a config) you MUST add a dated entry here in the same commit (a CI check enforces
it). Newest first. Keep entries one line; link the commit if useful.

## 2026-07-05 (later)
- ⚡ Range Raider (`DayTraderV5Gated`): DIAGNOSED the "no trades since rework" — not a bug (logs show a clean load, no populate_indicators error); it was OVER-GATED: every mode needed a pullback AND an uptrend on the same 1h candle, a rare confluence, so it sat idle in this choppy market. FIX = two new regime-covering legs so it operates regularly: `trend_breakout` (ADX≥25 + close>EMA50 + break of 20-bar high — trades RISING phases; grounded in V7's Donchian edge; `confirm_trade_exit` vetoes the range-top sell so breakouts run) and `range_meanrev` (ADX<20 chop + buy the range low, no uptrend requirement — the engine for a choppy market; low-ADX gate is the research-backed guard against knife-catching). Both fee-band gated; range_meanrev half-stake + 2.0×ATR stop. Distinct tags so the brain measures each.

## 2026-07-05
- **NEW bot: `RegimeSwitchV2`** (⚖️ Two-Way Tide) — rebuilt `perps-regime-switch` from the fleet's combined lessons + evidenced public practice (AQR trend-following, Hyperliquid docs). The fleet's only dual-direction engine: 4h Donchian breakout LONG in up-regime / SHORT in down-regime (mirrors the proven V7 PF~1.8 edge to finally profit from a downtrend). Per-pair daily regime (EMA200+slope, ADX gate w/ hysteresis), structure-break exits (let winners run — not trailing/ROI cap), wide ATR disaster stop, inverse-vol sizing, low leverage, correlation-aware global cap, pulse half-stake, Postgres persist. UNVALIDATED (no OHLCV to backtest) — dry-run, robust-by-construction, brain-watched.
- `RegimeSwitchV2`: tuned for more frequency — Donchian 20/10→15/8, ADX gate 22/16→20/14, universe 2→10 liquid majors, max_open 3→6 (still <60% of book). Stays on fee-viable 4h (not 1h churn).
- dashboard: **trendy + descriptive bot names** (emoji + plain "what it does") replacing the technical labels.
- ci: deploy path-filter tracks `RegimeSwitchV[0-9]+.py` so future V-bumps auto-deploy the regime service.
- dashboard: **Total P&L on every bot card** — freqtrade bots publish `pnl_abs=None` (gain is `equity−$1000`), so they showed no P&L line; now computed from equity for all paper bots.
- learning: **brain now covers ALL bots** — `bot_learn.py` read only `bot_trades` (freqtrade); added `bot_pnl_store.fetch_paper_trades()` and merged it so perps + sniper closes (in `paper_trades`) are analysed too. The `reason` field splits into long/short enter_tag + exit_reason, giving the brain long-vs-short expectancy.
- day trader (`DayTraderV5Gated`): widen buy zone 0.22 → 0.37 (enter higher up the pullback for materially more fills; captured move thinner ~0.41×band, still fee-positive). Doubled live bounce eligibility 0→2 pairs.
- day trader (`DayTraderV5Gated`): loosen risk-off band gate 2.2% → 2.0% (more relief-rally pairs, still above fee floor).
- day trader: add third entry mode `bounce_pullback` — trades the relief rally in a risk-off regime when the pair's own 1h EMA50 is above price AND rising; half stake, fast exits. Fixes the "idle in a bounce" gap.
- perps `rsi-meanrev`: global position cap (`MAX_OPEN_POSITIONS=6`, `MAX_NEW_PER_LOOP=2`) gated on a live open count — was holding 14 longs (70% of book) in one correlated dip with no cap.
- ops: schedule the learning brain (`bot_learn.py`) every 2h in the freqtrade supervisor (`run_all.sh`) — it was never scheduled, so it never accumulated the 3 runs needed to promote a lesson to actionable.
- ops: add `CHANGELOG.md` + CI enforcement + `CLAUDE.md` session-coordination rules (this change).
